print("\n" .. string.rep("=", 80))
print("Yin Yang V39")
print(string.rep("=", 80))
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local LocalizationService = game:GetService("LocalizationService")
if not LocalPlayer then
    error("ERROR: No local player available")
    return
end
do
local GENV = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G
if GENV.YY_UnlockAll and GENV.YY_UnlockAll.Disable then
    pcall(GENV.YY_UnlockAll.Disable)
end
GENV.YY_UnlockAll = (function()
    local M = {}
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
    local PREFIX = "[YY-UnlockAll]"
    local LOG = {}
    local function log(...)
        local parts = {}
        for i = 1, select("#", ...) do
            parts[i] = tostring((select(i, ...)))
        end
        local line = PREFIX .. " " .. table.concat(parts, " ")
        LOG[#LOG + 1] = line
        if #LOG > 200 then
            table.remove(LOG, 1)
        end
        print(line)
    end
    local function path(root, ...)
        local cur = root
        for _, name in ipairs({ ... }) do
            if not cur then
                return nil
            end
            cur = cur:WaitForChild(name, 10)
        end
        return cur
    end
    local function req(label, ...)
        local names = { ... }
        local ok, res = pcall(function()
            local inst = path(RS, table.unpack(names))
            if not inst then
                error("no se encontro la ruta")
            end
            return require(inst)
        end)
        if not ok then
            log("FALLO cargando", label, "->", tostring(res))
            return nil
        end
        return res
    end
    local active = false -- parches aplicados
    local gen = 0 -- invalida un Enable en curso cuando se llama Disable/Enable de nuevo
    local patches = {}
    local patchResults = {}
    local fakeE = {} -- emotes:    [indiceSlot 1..12] = idEmoteVisual
    local fakeC = {} -- cosmeticos: [nombreSlot]      = idItemVisual (p.ej. "CosmeticSlot_1")
    local pending = nil -- ultimo slot pulsado en la rueda { slot, t }
    local popupInst = nil -- instancia del popup de la rueda
    local warnedOverlay = false
    local lastBuild = {} -- diagnostico del ultimo armado de TU personaje
    local emoteOn, cosOn = false, false
    local Registry, Inventory, Loadout, PopupService, EmotePopup, EmoteClass, ItemSvc, RigClass, DataRegClass
    local origGetOwned, origOwns, origIsEquipped, origGetFromSlot, origSlotTypeForSlot, origSetEquipped
    local ALL, ALLSET = {}, {} -- emotes
    local TYPE_NAMES = { "Cosmetic", "Character", "Unusual", "Perk", "CarryAnimation", "Nametag" }
    local ENABLED, KNOWN = {}, {}
    for _, n in ipairs(TYPE_NAMES) do
        ENABLED[n] = true
        KNOWN[n] = true
    end
    local INGAME = { Perk = true, Nametag = true }
    local function emptyByType()
        local t = {}
        for _, n in ipairs(TYPE_NAMES) do
            t[n] = {}
        end
        return t
    end
    local BYTYPE = emptyByType()
    local TYPE_OF = {} -- [id] = tipo
    M.Log = LOG
    M.FakeEmotes = fakeE
    M.FakeCosmetics = fakeC
    M.LastBuild = lastBuild
    local function writable(t)
        local function probe()
            return pcall(function()
                t.__yy_probe = nil
            end)
        end
        if probe() then
            return true
        end
        if type(setreadonly) == "function" then
            pcall(setreadonly, t, false)
        end
        if probe() then
            return true
        end
        if type(make_writeable) == "function" then
            pcall(make_writeable, t)
        end
        return probe()
    end
    local function patch(label, tbl, key, build)
        local orig = tbl[key]
        if type(orig) ~= "function" then
            log("PARCHE NO APLICADO (no existe la funcion):", label)
            patchResults[#patchResults + 1] = { label = label, ok = false }
            return false
        end
        if not writable(tbl) then
            log("PARCHE NO APLICADO (tabla de solo lectura, falta setreadonly):", label)
            patchResults[#patchResults + 1] = { label = label, ok = false }
            return false
        end
        patches[#patches + 1] = { tbl = tbl, key = key, orig = orig }
        tbl[key] = build(orig)
        patchResults[#patchResults + 1] = { label = label, ok = true }
        return true
    end
    local function slotIndex(slot)
        return tonumber(string.match(tostring(slot), "^EmoteSlot_(%d+)$"))
    end
    local function realSlotId(idx)
        local ok, id = pcall(origGetFromSlot, "EmoteSlot_" .. idx)
        if ok and type(id) == "number" then
            return id
        end
        return 0
    end
    local function owns(id)
        local ok, res = pcall(origOwns, id)
        return ok and res == true
    end
    local function pickBase()
        local ok, owned = pcall(origGetOwned, "Emote")
        if not ok or type(owned) ~= "table" then
            return nil
        end
        local list = table.clone(owned)
        table.sort(list)
        for _, id in ipairs(list) do
            if not origIsEquipped(id) then
                return id
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function getPopup()
        if popupInst and popupInst.Frame then
            return popupInst
        end
        if PopupService and type(PopupService.GetPopup) == "function" then
            local ok, p = pcall(PopupService.GetPopup, PopupService, "Emote")
            if ok and p then
                popupInst = p
                return p
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function wheelSlotFrame(popup, idx)
        local wheel = popup.Frame:FindFirstChild(idx <= 6 and "Wheel" or "Wheel2")
        return wheel and wheel:FindFirstChild("Emote" .. ((idx - 1) % 6 + 1))
    end
    local function applyOverlay(popup)
        if not popup or not popup.Frame or type(popup.Emotes) ~= "table" then
            return
        end
        for idx, fid in pairs(fakeE) do
            if popup.Emotes[idx] then
                local ok, err = pcall(function()
                    local sf = wheelSlotFrame(popup, idx)
                    local item = ItemSvc:GetItemFromID(fid)
                    ItemSvc:ClearViewport(sf.ViewportFrame)
                    ItemSvc:CreateViewport(item, sf.ViewportFrame)
                    sf.TextLabel.Text = ItemSvc:GetName(item)
                end)
                if not ok and not warnedOverlay then
                    warnedOverlay = true
                    log("Aviso: no se pudo dibujar el visual en la rueda ->", tostring(err))
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function refreshWheel()
        local p = getPopup()
        if not p or type(p.Emotes) ~= "table" then
            return
        end
        pcall(origSetEquipped, p, p.Emotes) -- redibuja lo real
        applyOverlay(p) -- y encima el visual
    end
    local function isLocalEmote(self)
        local char = self.Character
        local lc = LP.Character
        if not char or not lc then
            return false
        end
        if char == lc then
            return true
        end
        local a, b = char:GetAttribute("Tag"), lc:GetAttribute("Tag")
        return a ~= nil and a == b
    end
    local function resolveFake(self)
        local mod = self.EmoteModule
        local modId = mod and mod:GetAttribute("ID")
        if not modId then
            return nil
        end
        if pending and tick() - pending.t < 5 then
            local fid = fakeE[pending.slot]
            if fid and realSlotId(pending.slot) == modId then
                pending = nil
                return fid
            end
        end
        for idx = 1, 12 do
            local fid = fakeE[idx]
            if fid and realSlotId(idx) == modId then
                return fid
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function swapTo(fid)
        if type(firesignal) ~= "function" then
            log("Tu ejecutor no tiene firesignal: no se puede cambiar el emote local")
            return
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        local tag = char and char:GetAttribute("Tag")
        if not tag then
            log("No se encontro el Tag del personaje; no se cambia el emote")
            return
        end
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local tagBuf = buffer.create(2)
            buffer.writeu16(tagBuf, 0, tag)
            firesignal(RS.Events.UpdateCharacterDataRegistry.OnClientEvent, {
                buffer.fromstring("\24\1"),
                fid,
                tagBuf,
            })
        end)
        if not ok then
            log("Fallo el cambio local de emote ->", tostring(err))
        end
    end
    local function slotType(slot)
        if type(slot) ~= "string" or type(origSlotTypeForSlot) ~= "function" then
            return nil
        end
        local ok, st = pcall(origSlotTypeForSlot, slot)
        if ok and KNOWN[st] then
            return st
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function collectEmoteIds()
        local raw, seen = {}, {}
        local items = RS:FindFirstChild("Items")
        local function walk(parent)
            for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Folder") then
                    if child.Name == "Emotes" then
                        for _, e in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
                            if e:IsA("ModuleScript") then
                                local id = e:GetAttribute("ID")
                                if type(id) == "number" and not seen[id] then
                                    seen[id] = true
                                    raw[#raw + 1] = id
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        walk(child)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if items then
            walk(items)
        end
        local filtered = {}
        for _, id in ipairs(raw) do
            local okSlot, st = pcall(Inventory.GetSlotType, id)
            local okReg, entry = pcall(Registry.GetById, id)
            if okSlot and st == "Emote" and okReg and entry and entry.Module then
                filtered[#filtered + 1] = id
            end
        end
        if #filtered == 0 and #raw > 0 then
            log("Aviso: el filtro por slot 'Emote' dejo 0; se usa la lista sin filtrar (", #raw, ")")
            filtered = raw
        end
        table.sort(filtered)
        return filtered, #raw
    end
    local function collectCosmetics()
        local okAll, all = pcall(Registry.GetAll)
        if not okAll or type(all) ~= "table" then
            return nil
        end
        local bytype = emptyByType()
        local typeOf = {}
        local n = 0
        for id in pairs(all) do
            n = n + 1
            if n % 150 == 0 then
                task.wait() -- ceder para no congelar el cliente al leer cientos de modulos
            end
            if type(id) == "number" then
                local ok, st = pcall(Inventory.GetSlotType, id)
                if ok and KNOWN[st] then
                    local list = bytype[st]
                    list[#list + 1] = id
                    typeOf[id] = st
                end
            end
        end
        for _, list in pairs(bytype) do
            table.sort(list)
        end
        return bytype, typeOf, n
    end
    local function substitutePerks(value)
        if type(value) ~= "table" or not (INGAME.Perk and ENABLED.Perk) then
            return value
        end
        local removed, any = {}, false
        for slot in pairs(fakeC) do
            if slotType(slot) == "Perk" then
                any = true
                local okR, rid = pcall(origGetFromSlot, slot)
                if okR and type(rid) == "number" and rid ~= 0 then
                    removed[rid] = true
                end
            end
        end
        if not any then
            return value
        end
        local out, seen = {}, {}
        for _, id in ipairs(value) do
            if not removed[id] then
                out[#out + 1] = id
                seen[id] = true
            end
        end
        for slot, fid in pairs(fakeC) do
            if slotType(slot) == "Perk" and not seen[fid] then
                out[#out + 1] = fid
                seen[fid] = true
            end
        end
        return out
    end
    local function substituteNametag(value)
        local fid = fakeC["NametagSlot"]
        if type(fid) == "number" and INGAME.Nametag and ENABLED.Nametag then
            return fid
        end
        return value
    end
    local function applyPatches()
        patch("useInventory.GetOwnedBySlotType", Inventory, "GetOwnedBySlotType", function(orig)
            return function(st, ...)
                local list = orig(st, ...)
                if type(list) ~= "table" then
                    return list
                end
                local extra
                if st == "Emote" then
                    if not emoteOn then
                        return list
                    end
                    extra = ALL
                elseif cosOn and ENABLED[st] then
                    extra = BYTYPE[st]
                else
                    return list
                end
                local have = {}
                for _, id in ipairs(list) do
                    have[id] = true
                end
                local out = table.clone(list)
                for _, id in ipairs(extra) do
                    if not have[id] then
                        out[#out + 1] = id
                    end
                end
                return out
            end
        end)
        patch("useLoadout.TryEquip", Loadout, "TryEquip", function(orig)
            return function(slot, id, ...)
                local idx = emoteOn and slotIndex(slot) or nil
                if idx then
                    if type(id) == "number" and id ~= 0 then
                        if ALLSET[id] and not owns(id) then
                            if realSlotId(idx) == 0 then
                                local base = pickBase()
                                if not base then
                                    log("No tienes ningun emote real para usar de base en el slot", idx)
                                    return
                                end
                                log("Slot", idx, "vacio: se equipa el emote real base", base)
                                orig(slot, base)
                            end
                            fakeE[idx] = id
                            log("Slot", idx, "-> emote visual", id)
                            refreshWheel()
                            return
                        end
                        local had = fakeE[idx] ~= nil
                        fakeE[idx] = nil
                        local r = orig(slot, id, ...)
                        if had then
                            refreshWheel()
                        end
                        return r
                    end
                    if id == nil or id == 0 then
                        fakeE[idx] = nil
                    end
                    return orig(slot, id, ...)
                end
                local st = cosOn and slotType(slot) or nil
                if st and ENABLED[st] then
                    if type(id) == "number" and id ~= 0 then
                        if TYPE_OF[id] == st and not owns(id) then
                            fakeC[slot] = id
                            log("Slot", slot, "-> visual", id)
                            return
                        end
                        fakeC[slot] = nil
                        return orig(slot, id, ...)
                    end
                    if (id == nil or id == 0) and fakeC[slot] ~= nil then
                        fakeC[slot] = nil
                        log("Slot", slot, "-> visual quitado")
                        return
                    end
                end
                return orig(slot, id, ...)
            end
        end)
        patch("useLoadout.TryUnequip", Loadout, "TryUnequip", function(orig)
            return function(slot, ...)
                if cosOn and fakeC[slot] ~= nil then
                    fakeC[slot] = nil
                    log("Slot", slot, "-> visual quitado")
                    return
                end
                local idx = emoteOn and slotIndex(slot) or nil
                local had = idx and fakeE[idx] ~= nil
                if idx then
                    fakeE[idx] = nil
                end
                local r = orig(slot, ...)
                if had then
                    refreshWheel()
                end
                return r
            end
        end)
        patch("useLoadout.GetEquipped", Loadout, "GetEquipped", function(orig)
            return function(...)
                local real = orig(...)
                if type(real) ~= "table" or (next(fakeE) == nil and next(fakeC) == nil) then
                    return real
                end
                local out = table.clone(real)
                for idx, fid in pairs(fakeE) do
                    local name = "EmoteSlot_" .. idx
                    if out[name] then
                        out[name] = fid
                    end
                end
                for slot, fid in pairs(fakeC) do
                    out[slot] = fid
                end
                return table.freeze(out)
            end
        end)
        patch("useLoadout.IsEquipped", Loadout, "IsEquipped", function(orig)
            return function(id)
                local eq, s = orig(id)
                if eq then
                    return eq, s
                end
                for idx, fid in pairs(fakeE) do
                    if fid == id then
                        return true, "EmoteSlot_" .. idx
                    end
                end
                for slot, fid in pairs(fakeC) do
                    if fid == id then
                        return true, slot
                    end
                end
                return false, nil
            end
        end)
        if emoteOn then
            patch("EmotePopup.SetEquipped", EmotePopup, "SetEquipped", function(orig)
                return function(self, list, ...)
                    local r = orig(self, list, ...)
                    popupInst = self
                    applyOverlay(self)
                    return r
                end
            end)
            patch("EmotePopup.Show", EmotePopup, "Show", function(orig)
                return function(self, ...)
                    local r = orig(self, ...)
                    popupInst = self
                    applyOverlay(self)
                    return r
                end
            end)
            patch("EmotePopup.KeyActivated", EmotePopup, "KeyActivated", function(orig)
                return function(self, slot, ...)
                    if type(slot) == "number" then
                        pending = { slot = slot, t = tick() }
                    end
                    return orig(self, slot, ...)
                end
            end)
            patch("Emote.Activate", EmoteClass, "Activate", function(orig)
                return function(self, preview, p3)
                    local fid = nil
                    if not preview then
                        pcall(function()
                            if isLocalEmote(self) then
                                fid = resolveFake(self)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    if fid then
                        task.defer(swapTo, fid)
                    end
                    return orig(self, preview, p3)
                end
            end)
        end
        if cosOn then
            if RigClass then
                patch("Rig.BuildRig (partida)", RigClass, "BuildRig", function(orig)
                    return function(self, ...)
                        if not self.Built
                            and type(self.Cosmetics) == "table"
                            and self.RigTerm and self.Actions and self.ToolSkins and self.Boombox
                            and next(fakeC) ~= nil
                        then
                            local isLocal = false
                            pcall(function()
                                isLocal = self.Parent ~= nil and self.Parent.Local == true
                            end)
                            if isLocal then
                                local ok, err = pcall(function()
                                    local real = self.Cosmetics
                                    local removed = {}
                                    for slot in pairs(fakeC) do
                                        local okR, rid = pcall(origGetFromSlot, slot)
                                        if okR and type(rid) == "number" and rid ~= 0 then
                                            removed[rid] = true
                                        end
                                    end
                                    local out, seen = {}, {}
                                    for _, id in ipairs(real) do
                                        if not removed[id] then
                                            out[#out + 1] = id
                                            seen[id] = true
                                        end
                                    end
                                    for _, fid in pairs(fakeC) do
                                        if not seen[fid] then
                                            out[#out + 1] = fid
                                            seen[fid] = true
                                        end
                                    end
                                    table.clear(lastBuild)
                                    lastBuild.Real = table.clone(real)
                                    lastBuild.Fake = table.clone(fakeC)
                                    lastBuild.Final = table.clone(out)
                                    self.Cosmetics = out
                                    log(
                                        "BuildRig local: real = {" .. table.concat(real, ",") .. "}",
                                        "| final = {" .. table.concat(out, ",") .. "}"
                                    )
                                end)
                                if not ok then
                                    log("Fallo al cambiar Cosmetics en BuildRig ->", tostring(err))
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return orig(self, ...)
                    end
                end)
            else
                log("PARCHE NO APLICADO (no se pudo cargar Objects.Game.Character.Rig): cosmeticos en partida no funcionan")
                patchResults[#patchResults + 1] = { label = "Rig.BuildRig (partida)", ok = false }
            end
            if DataRegClass then
                patch("DataRegistry.SetTerm (ventajas/etiqueta)", DataRegClass, "SetTerm", function(orig)
                    return function(self, term, value, ...)
                        if (term == "Perks" or term == "Nametag") and next(fakeC) ~= nil then
                            local ok, res = pcall(function()
                                if self.Parent == nil or self.Parent.Local ~= true then
                                    return value
                                end
                                if term == "Perks" then
                                    return substitutePerks(value)
                                end
                                return substituteNametag(value)
                            end)
                            if ok and res ~= nil and res ~= value then
                                log("En partida:", term, "->", type(res) == "table" and ("{" .. table.concat(res, ",") .. "}") or tostring(res))
                                value = res
                            end
                        end
                        return orig(self, term, value, ...)
                    end
                end)
            else
                log("PARCHE NO APLICADO (no se pudo cargar Objects.Game.Character.DataRegistry): ventajas/etiqueta en partida no funcionan")
                patchResults[#patchResults + 1] = { label = "DataRegistry.SetTerm (ventajas/etiqueta)", ok = false }
            end
        end
    end
    function M.Disable()
        gen = gen + 1 -- cancela un Enable que este leyendo listas
        if not active then
            return
        end
        for i = #patches, 1, -1 do
            local p = patches[i]
            pcall(function()
                p.tbl[p.key] = p.orig
            end)
        end
        patches = {}
        table.clear(fakeE)
        table.clear(fakeC)
        table.clear(lastBuild)
        pending = nil
        emoteOn, cosOn = false, false
        active = false
        refreshWheel()
        log("Descargado: todo restaurado")
    end
    local function enableImpl(myGen)
        for _, name in ipairs({ "YY_CosmeticTest", "YY_EmoteTest" }) do
            local ok, t = pcall(function()
                return GENV[name]
            end)
            if ok and type(t) == "table" and type(t.Unload) == "function" then
                pcall(t.Unload)
                log("Se descargo el script de prueba suelto:", name)
            end
        end
        Registry = req("Items.Registry", "Items", "Registry")
        Inventory = req("useInventory", "Shared", "UserData", "ClientHooks", "useInventory")
        Loadout = req("useLoadout", "Shared", "UserData", "ClientHooks", "useLoadout")
        if not (Registry and Inventory and Loadout) then
            log("ABORTADO: falta al menos un modulo necesario. No se aplico ningun parche.")
            return
        end
        RigClass = req("Objects.Game.Character.Rig", "Objects", "Game", "Character", "Rig")
        DataRegClass = req("Objects.Game.Character.DataRegistry", "Objects", "Game", "Character", "DataRegistry")
        PopupService = req("PopupService", "Services", "UI", "PopupService")
        EmotePopup = req("PopupService.Types.Emote", "Services", "UI", "PopupService", "Types", "Emote")
        EmoteClass = req("Objects.Items.Emote", "Objects", "Items", "Emote")
        ItemSvc = req("ClientItemService", "Services", "Items", "ClientItemService")
        local emoteMods = (EmotePopup and EmoteClass and ItemSvc) and true or false
        if not emoteMods then
            log("Aviso: faltan modulos de emotes; Unlock All se aplica solo a cosmeticos/personajes/auras")
        end
        local newAll, newAllSet, rawCount = {}, {}, 0
        if emoteMods then
            newAll, rawCount = collectEmoteIds()
            for _, id in ipairs(newAll) do
                newAllSet[id] = true
            end
            log("Emotes encontrados:", rawCount, "| validos:", #newAll)
        end
        local newBy, newTypeOf, regCount = collectCosmetics() -- puede ceder (task.wait)
        if myGen ~= gen then
            return -- se apago (o se re-encendio) mientras se leian las listas
        end
        if active then
            return
        end
        if not newBy then
            log("Aviso: Registry.GetAll no devolvio una tabla; cosmeticos/personajes/auras desactivados")
            newBy = emptyByType()
            newTypeOf = {}
            regCount = 0
        end
        local cosTotal = 0
        local counts = {}
        for _, n in ipairs(TYPE_NAMES) do
            cosTotal = cosTotal + #newBy[n]
            counts[#counts + 1] = n .. ": " .. #newBy[n]
        end
        log("Items en Registry:", regCount, "|", table.concat(counts, " | "))
        if #newAll == 0 and cosTotal == 0 then
            log("ABORTADO: no se encontro ningun emote ni cosmetico. No se aplico ningun parche.")
            return
        end
        ALL, ALLSET = newAll, newAllSet
        BYTYPE, TYPE_OF = newBy, newTypeOf
        emoteOn = emoteMods and #newAll > 0
        cosOn = cosTotal > 0
        patches, patchResults = {}, {}
        table.clear(fakeE)
        table.clear(fakeC)
        pending = nil
        popupInst = nil
        warnedOverlay = false
        origGetOwned = Inventory.GetOwnedBySlotType
        origOwns = Inventory.Owns
        origIsEquipped = Loadout.IsEquipped
        origGetFromSlot = Loadout.GetEquippedFromSlot
        origSlotTypeForSlot = Loadout.GetSlotTypeForSlot
        origSetEquipped = EmotePopup and EmotePopup.SetEquipped or nil
        applyPatches()
        active = true
        local okCount = 0
        for _, r in ipairs(patchResults) do
            if r.ok then
                okCount = okCount + 1
            end
        end
        log("Listo:", okCount .. "/" .. #patchResults, "parches aplicados")
        if okCount < #patchResults then
            log("Algun parche fallo: ejecuta getgenv().YY_UnlockAll.Status() y revisa cual")
        end
        pcall(function()
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "Unlock All",
                Text = okCount .. "/" .. #patchResults .. " parches aplicados",
                Duration = 5,
            })
        end)
    end
    function M.Enable()
        if active then
            return
        end
        gen = gen + 1
        local myGen = gen
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, err = pcall(enableImpl, myGen)
            if not ok then
                log("FALLO Enable ->", tostring(err))
            end
        end)
    end
    function M.IsEnabled()
        return active
    end
    function M.Status()
        log("---- ESTADO ---- activo:", tostring(active), "| emotes:", tostring(emoteOn), "| cosmeticos:", tostring(cosOn))
        for _, r in ipairs(patchResults) do
            log(r.ok and "OK     " or "FALLO  ", r.label)
        end
        for st in pairs(KNOWN) do
            log("Tipo", st, ENABLED[st] and "activo" or "desactivado", "| items:", #BYTYPE[st])
        end
        local any = false
        for idx = 1, 12 do
            if fakeE[idx] then
                any = true
                log("Emote slot", idx, "real:", realSlotId(idx), "| visual:", fakeE[idx])
            end
        end
        for slot, fid in pairs(fakeC) do
            any = true
            local okReal, real = pcall(Loadout.GetEquippedFromSlot, slot)
            log("Slot", slot, "real:", okReal and real or "?", "| visual:", fid)
        end
        if not any then
            log("Sin slots visuales activos")
        end
    end
    function M.SetType(name, state)
        if not KNOWN[name] then
            log("Tipo desconocido:", tostring(name), "(usa " .. table.concat(TYPE_NAMES, ", ") .. ")")
            return
        end
        ENABLED[name] = state and true or false
        if not ENABLED[name] then
            for slot in pairs(fakeC) do
                if slotType(slot) == name then
                    fakeC[slot] = nil
                end
            end
        end
        log("Tipo", name, ENABLED[name] and "activado" or "desactivado", "(se aplica la proxima vez que abras el selector)")
    end
    function M.SetInGame(name, state)
        if INGAME[name] == nil then
            log("Solo se puede para Perk o Nametag")
            return
        end
        INGAME[name] = state and true or false
        log("En partida:", name, INGAME[name] and "activado" or "desactivado", "(aplica en la proxima actualizacion del servidor)")
    end
    return M
end)()
end

local function getKeyLanguage(localeId)
    local savedMode = nil
    pcall(function()
        if isfile and readfile and isfile("Yin_Yang_Lang.txt") then
            savedMode = string.lower((string.gsub(readfile("Yin_Yang_Lang.txt"), "%s+", "")))
        end
    end)
    if savedMode == "es" or savedMode == "en" then
        return savedMode
    end
    local language = string.lower(tostring(localeId or "en")):match("^(%a+)") or "en"
    return KeyLanguageTranslations[language] and language or "en"
end
local function keyText(key)
    local languageTable = KeyLanguageTranslations[KeyLanguage] or KeyLanguageTranslations.en
    return languageTable[key] or KeyLanguageTranslations.en[key] or key
end
function getKeyVisualTheme()
    return KeyVisualTheme
end
function addYinSweep(strokeObject, borderColors, duration)
    local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, borderColors[1]),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.34, borderColors[2]),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.67, borderColors[3]),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, borderColors[4]),
    })
    gradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.36),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.36),
    })
    gradient.Offset = Vector2.new(-1.5, 0)
    gradient.Parent = strokeObject
    KeyTweenService:Create(
        gradient,
        TweenInfo.new(duration or 1.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, false),
        {Offset = Vector2.new(1.5, 0)}
    ):Play()
    KeyTweenService:Create(
        strokeObject,
        TweenInfo.new((duration or 1.8) + 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true),
        {Transparency = 0.02}
    ):Play()
end
function normalizeAccessKey(value)
    return tostring(value or ""):gsub("%s+", ""):upper()
end
function isAccessKeyFormat(value)
    return value:match("^YINYANG%-%w%w%w%-%w%w%w%-%w%w%w%-%w%w%w%-%w%w%w$") ~= nil
end
function hasKeyCacheSupport()
    return type(isfile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" and type(writefile) == "function" and type(delfile) == "function"
end
function loadCachedKey()
    if not hasKeyCacheSupport() then
        return nil
    end
    local ok, cached = pcall(function()
        if isfile(KEY_CACHE_FILE) then
            return normalizeAccessKey(readfile(KEY_CACHE_FILE))
        end
    end)
    if ok and isAccessKeyFormat(cached or "") then
        return cached
    end
    return nil
end
function saveCachedKey(key)
    if hasKeyCacheSupport() then
        pcall(writefile, KEY_CACHE_FILE, normalizeAccessKey(key))
    end
end
function clearCachedKey()
    if hasKeyCacheSupport() then
        pcall(function()
            if isfile(KEY_CACHE_FILE) then
                delfile(KEY_CACHE_FILE)
            end
        end)
    end
end
function validateAccessKey(value)
    local key = normalizeAccessKey(value)
    if not isAccessKeyFormat(key) then
        return false, "INVALID FORMAT"
    end
    local requestFunction = (syn and syn.request) or http_request or request or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
    local ok, responseBody = pcall(function()
        if requestFunction then
            local response = requestFunction({
                Url = KEY_VALIDATE_URL .. KeyHttpService:UrlEncode(key),
                Method = "GET",
            })
            if not response or tonumber(response.StatusCode) ~= 200 then
                error("HTTP validation failed")
            end
            return response.Body
        end
        return game:HttpGet(KEY_VALIDATE_URL .. KeyHttpService:UrlEncode(key))
    end)
    if not ok or type(responseBody) ~= "string" then
        return false, "SERVER UNAVAILABLE"
    end
    local decodedOk, data = pcall(function()
        return KeyHttpService:JSONDecode(responseBody)
    end)
    if not decodedOk or type(data) ~= "table" then
        return false, "INVALID RESPONSE"
    end
    if data.valid == true then
        return true, "KEY ACCEPTED"
    end
    return false, "INVALID OR EXPIRED KEY"
end

    local sectionIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

    input.ZIndex = 4

    local inputCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    inputCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    inputCorner.Parent = input
    local inputPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    inputPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 50)
    inputPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 16)
    inputPadding.Parent = input
    local inputStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    inputStroke.Color = visualTheme.Accent
    inputStroke.Transparency = 0.22
    inputStroke.Parent = input
    inputStroke.Thickness = 1.15
    addYinSweep(inputStroke, visualTheme.Borders, 1.8)
    local keyIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    keyIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    keyIcon.Image = "rbxassetid://82339297204572"
    keyIcon.ImageColor3 = visualTheme.Text
    keyIcon.ImageTransparency = 0.02
    keyIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 131)
    keyIcon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18)
    keyIcon.ZIndex = 5
    keyIcon.Parent = content
    local keyDivider = Instance.new("Frame")
    keyDivider.BackgroundColor3 = visualTheme.Borders[3]
    keyDivider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
    keyDivider.BorderSizePixel = 0
    keyDivider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 63, 0, 122)
    keyDivider.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 36)
    keyDivider.ZIndex = 5
    keyDivider.Parent = content
    function createActionButton(name, text, position, size, accent)
        local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
        button.Name = name
        button.AutoButtonColor = false
        button.BackgroundColor3 = visualTheme.Header
        button.BorderSizePixel = 0
        button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        button.Text = text
        button.TextColor3 = visualTheme.Text
        button.TextSize = 11
        button.Position = position
        button.Size = size
        button.ZIndex = 4
        button.Parent = content
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        corner.Parent = button
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Color = accent
        stroke.Transparency = 0.18
        stroke.Thickness = 1
        stroke.Parent = button
        addYinSweep(stroke, visualTheme.Borders, name == "CheckKey" and 1.45 or 2.05)
        return button, stroke
    end
    local checkButton, checkStroke = createActionButton("CheckKey", keyText("check"), UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 171), UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 44), visualTheme.Accent)
    local linkButton, linkStroke = createActionButton("GetLink", keyText("link"), UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 231), UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 32), visualTheme.Borders[2])
    linkButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
    linkButton.TextColor3 = visualTheme.TextDim
    linkButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    linkButton.TextSize = 9
    linkStroke.Transparency = 0.28
    local checkIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    checkIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    checkIcon.Image = "rbxassetid://113137557066757"
    checkIcon.ImageColor3 = visualTheme.Text
    checkIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -96, 0, 184)
    checkIcon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18)
    checkIcon.ZIndex = 5
    checkIcon.Parent = content
    local linkIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    linkIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    linkIcon.Image = "rbxassetid://125248903248905"
    linkIcon.ImageColor3 = visualTheme.TextDim
    linkIcon.ImageTransparency = 0.05
    linkIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -92, 0, 238)
    linkIcon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18)
    linkIcon.ZIndex = 5
    linkIcon.Parent = content
    local status = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    status.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    status.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    status.Text = keyText("secure")
    status.TextColor3 = visualTheme.TextDim
    status.TextSize = 8
    status.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    status.Position = UDim2.new(0, 47, 0, 270)
    status.Size = UDim2.new(1, -64, 0, 16)
    status.ZIndex = 4
    status.Parent = content
    local alertIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    alertIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    alertIcon.Image = "rbxassetid://140207627533370"
    alertIcon.ImageColor3 = visualTheme.TextDim
    alertIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 21, 0, 268)
    alertIcon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)
    alertIcon.ZIndex = 5
    alertIcon.Parent = content
    local authorized = false
    local closed = false
    local busy = false
    local currentStatusKind = "secure"
    local function renderKeyStatus(kind)
        if kind == "secure" then return keyText("secure") end
        if kind == "revalidating" then return keyText("revalidating") end
        if kind == "contacting" then return keyText("contacting") end
        if kind == "accepted" then return "✓ " .. keyText("accepted") .. " — " .. keyText("loading") end
        if kind == "link_copied" then return keyText("link_copied") .. " — " .. KEY_PORTAL_URL end
        if kind == "portal" then return keyText("portal") .. ": " .. KEY_PORTAL_URL end
        if kind == "no_file_support" then return keyText("no_file_support") end
        if kind == "invalid_format" then return "✕ " .. keyText("invalid_format") end
        if kind == "server_unavailable" then return "✕ " .. keyText("server_unavailable") end
        if kind == "invalid_response" then return "✕ " .. keyText("invalid_response") end
        if kind == "invalid_expired" then return "✕ " .. keyText("invalid_expired") end
        if kind == "invalid_expired_removed" then return "✕ " .. keyText("invalid_expired") .. " — " .. keyText("saved_removed") end
        return keyText("secure")
    end
    local function setKeyStatus(kind, color)
        currentStatusKind = kind
        status.Text = renderKeyStatus(kind)
        status.TextColor3 = color
    end
    local function refreshKeyLanguage()
        brandSub.Text = keyText("brand_sub")
        title.Text = keyText("title")
        subtitle.Text = keyText("subtitle")
        if busy then
            checkButton.Text = keyText("checking")
        elseif authorized then
            checkButton.Text = "✓  " .. keyText("access_granted")
        else
            checkButton.Text = keyText("check")
        end
        linkButton.Text = keyText("link")
        status.Text = renderKeyStatus(currentStatusKind)
    end
    local function setKeyLanguageFromLocale(localeId)
        KeyLanguage = getKeyLanguage(localeId)
        refreshKeyLanguage()
    end
    pcall(function()
        local systemLocale = LocalizationService.SystemLocaleId
        if systemLocale and systemLocale ~= "" then
            setKeyLanguageFromLocale(systemLocale)
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, translator = pcall(function()
            return LocalizationService:GetTranslatorForPlayerAsync(LocalPlayer)
        end)
        if ok and translator then
            local function updateFromTranslator()
                setKeyLanguageFromLocale(translator.LocaleId)
            end
            updateFromTranslator()
            pcall(function()
                translator:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocaleId"):Connect(updateFromTranslator)
            end)
        end
    end)
    function tryAccess(value, fromCache)
        if busy or closed then
            return
        end
        busy = true
        input.Text = normalizeAccessKey(value)
        checkButton.Text = keyText("checking")
        setKeyStatus(
            fromCache and "revalidating" or "contacting",
            Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 210)
        )
        local valid, message = validateAccessKey(input.Text)
        if valid then
            saveCachedKey(input.Text)
            authorized = true
            getgenv().EvadeBetaKeyValidated = true
            setKeyStatus("accepted", Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235))
            checkButton.Text = "✓  " .. keyText("access_granted")
            checkButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 58)
            task.wait(0.7)
            if gui.Parent then
                gui:Destroy()
            end
        else
            local statusKind =
                message == "INVALID FORMAT" and "invalid_format"
                or message == "SERVER UNAVAILABLE" and "server_unavailable"
                or message == "INVALID RESPONSE" and "invalid_response"
                or message == "INVALID OR EXPIRED KEY" and (fromCache and "invalid_expired_removed" or "invalid_expired")
                or "secure"
            if fromCache and message == "INVALID OR EXPIRED KEY" then
                clearCachedKey()
                input.Text = ""
            end
            setKeyStatus(statusKind, Color3.fromRGB(222, 222, 222))
            checkButton.Text = "✓  " .. keyText("check")
            checkButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(43, 43, 43)
            task.wait(0.35)
            if checkButton.Parent then
                checkButton.BackgroundColor3 = visualTheme.Header
            end
        end
        busy = false
    end
    for _, data in ipairs({{checkButton, checkStroke}, {linkButton, linkStroke}}) do
        data[1].MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if not busy then
                setButtonVisual(data[1], data[2], true)
            end
        end)
        data[1].MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            setButtonVisual(data[1], data[2], false)
        end)
    end
    input.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
        if enterPressed then
            tryAccess(input.Text, false)
        end
    end)
    linkButton.Activated:Connect(function()
        if setclipboard then
            pcall(setclipboard, KEY_PORTAL_URL)
            setKeyStatus("link_copied", Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230))
        else
            setKeyStatus("portal", Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230))
        end
    end)
    close.Activated:Connect(function()
        closed = true
        gui:Destroy()
    end)
    checkButton.Activated:Connect(function()
        tryAccess(input.Text, false)
    end)
    local cachedKey = loadCachedKey()
    if cachedKey then
        input.Text = cachedKey
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.15)
            tryAccess(cachedKey, true)
        end)
    elseif not hasKeyCacheSupport() then
        setKeyStatus("no_file_support", Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 210))
    end
    while not authorized and not closed do
        task.wait()
    end
    return authorized
end

function showSocialPromoUI()
    local visualTheme = getKeyVisualTheme()
    local parent

        parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    end)

    end

    local function buildSocialCard(data)
        local card = Instance.new("Frame")
        card.Name = data.name
        card.BackgroundColor3 = visualTheme.Header
        card.BorderSizePixel = 0
        card.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -7, 1, 0)
        card.ZIndex = 4

        cardCorner.Parent = card

        cardStroke.Color = visualTheme.Borders[3]
        cardStroke.Transparency = 0.55
        cardStroke.Thickness = 1
        cardStroke.Parent = card
        addYinSweep(cardStroke, visualTheme.Borders, 2.4)
        local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        icon.Image = data.icon
        icon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -22, 0, 12)
        icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 44)

        label.TextSize = 12
        label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 60)
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)

        copyButton.AutoButtonColor = false
        copyButton.Name = "CopyLink"
        copyButton.BackgroundColor3 = visualTheme.Background
        copyButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
        copyButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        copyButton.Text = (KeyLanguage == "es") and "COPIAR LINK" or "COPY LINK"
        copyButton.TextColor3 = visualTheme.TextDim
        copyButton.TextSize = 10
        copyButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, -34)
        copyButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 26)
        copyButton.ZIndex = 5
        copyButton.Parent = card
        local copyCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        copyCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        copyCorner.Parent = copyButton
        local copyStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        copyStroke.Color = visualTheme.Borders[2]
        copyStroke.Transparency = 0.35
        copyStroke.Thickness = 1
        copyStroke.Parent = copyButton
        local originalText = copyButton.Text
        copyButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            copyButton.BackgroundColor3 = visualTheme.Header
        end)
        copyButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            copyButton.BackgroundColor3 = visualTheme.Background
        end)
        copyButton.Activated:Connect(function()
            if setclipboard then
                pcall(setclipboard, data.url)
                copyButton.Text = (KeyLanguage == "es") and "COPIADO!" or "COPIED!"
            else
                copyButton.Text = (KeyLanguage == "es") and "SIN SOPORTE" or "NOT SUPPORTED"
            end
            task.delay(1.4, function()
                if copyButton.Parent then
                    copyButton.Text = originalText
                end
            end)
        end)
        return card
    end
    for _, data in ipairs(SOCIAL_LINKS) do
        buildSocialCard(data)
    end
    local continueButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
    continueButton.AutoButtonColor = false
    continueButton.Name = "Continue"
    continueButton.BackgroundColor3 = visualTheme.Header

    continueButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 32)

    end)
    local closed = false
    close.Activated:Connect(function()
        closed = true
    end)
    continueButton.Activated:Connect(function()
        closed = true
    end)
    while not closed do
        task.wait()
    end
    if gui.Parent then
        gui:Destroy()
    end
end
local ENABLE_KEY_SYSTEM_UI = true
if ENABLE_KEY_SYSTEM_UI and not createKeySystemUI() then
    warn("YIN YANG KEY: closed without a valid key")
    return
end
if ENABLE_KEY_SYSTEM_UI then
    showSocialPromoUI()
end
do
    local _destroyed = false
    local function _selfDestruct(reason)
        if _destroyed then return end
        _destroyed = true
        pcall(function()
            local targets = {"SAEKeySystem", "YinYangUI", "YinYangMain"}
            for _, guiParent in ipairs({
                game:GetService("CoreGui"),
                LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui"),
            }) do
                if guiParent then
                    for _, name in ipairs(targets) do
                        local g = guiParent:FindFirstChild(name)
                        if g then g:Destroy() end
                    end
                    for _, child in ipairs(guiParent:GetChildren()) do
                        if child:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                            pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if type(isfile) == "function" and type(delfile) == "function" then
                if isfile(KEY_CACHE_FILE) then
                    delfile(KEY_CACHE_FILE)
                end
            end
        end)
        error("__YY_PROTECTED__" .. (reason or ""), 0)
    end
    pcall(function()
        local _db = debug
        if type(_db) == "table" then
            local _dangerousFns = {
                "getinfo", "getupvalue", "setupvalue", "getlocal",
                "setlocal", "getmetatable", "setmetatable",
                "traceback", "sethook", "getfenv", "setfenv"
            }
            for _, fnName in ipairs(_dangerousFns) do
                local original = _db[fnName]
                if type(original) == "function" then
                    _db[fnName] = function(...)
                        local args = {...}
                        local _isDangerousCall = (
                            fnName == "getinfo" or fnName == "getupvalue" or fnName == "setupvalue"
                            or fnName == "getmetatable" or fnName == "setmetatable"
                            or fnName == "getfenv" or fnName == "setfenv"
                            or fnName == "getlocal" or fnName == "setlocal"
                            or fnName == "sethook"
                        )
                        if _isDangerousCall then
                            _selfDestruct("debug_api_intercepted:" .. fnName)
                        end
                        return original(...)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if type(decompile) == "function" then
            local _origDecompile = decompile
            getgenv().decompile = function(obj, ...)
                if obj and (obj:IsA("LocalScript") or obj:IsA("ModuleScript") or obj:IsA("Script")) then
                    _selfDestruct("decompile_attempt")
                end
                return _origDecompile(obj, ...)
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local _dumpFunctions = {"getscriptbytecode", "dumpstring", "getscripthash"}
        local _genv = type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv() or _G
        for _, fnName in ipairs(_dumpFunctions) do
            if type(_genv[fnName]) == "function" then
                local _orig = _genv[fnName]
                _genv[fnName] = function(...)
                    _selfDestruct("bytecode_dump_intercepted:" .. fnName)
                    return _orig(...)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    local _envSnapshot = {}
    pcall(function()
        if type(getgenv) == "function" then
            local _genv = getgenv()
            for k, _ in pairs(_genv) do
                _envSnapshot[k] = true
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local _isStudio = RunService:IsStudio()
        if _isStudio then
            if not LocalPlayer then
                _selfDestruct("studio_no_player")
            end
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        local _checkInterval = 3
        local _iteraciones = 0
        while not _destroyed do
            _iteraciones = _iteraciones + 1
            task.wait(_checkInterval)
            if _destroyed then break end
            pcall(function()
                if type(decompile) == "function" then
                    local _src = tostring(decompile)
                    if _src:find("native") or _src:find("C function") then
                        _selfDestruct("decompile_restored_native")
                    end
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                if type(getgenv) == "function" then
                    local _genv = getgenv()
                    local _dumpSignals = {
                        "getscriptbytecode", "dumpstring", "getscripthash",
                        "readbytecode", "getfuncinfo", "getprotolist"
                    }
                    for _, fnName in ipairs(_dumpSignals) do
                        if type(_genv[fnName]) == "function" and not _envSnapshot[fnName] then
                            _selfDestruct("injected_dump_fn:" .. fnName)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end
print("\nSERVICES: Initialized successfully")

local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local fullBrightState = {
    Enabled = false,
    Connection = nil,
    Original = nil,
}
local fullBrightHooks = {
    SaveSettings = nil,
}
function captureFullBrightState()
    if fullBrightState.Original then
        return
    end
    local ok, snapshot = pcall(function()
        return {
            Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
            Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
            OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
            ColorShift_Bottom = Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom,
            ColorShift_Top = Lighting.ColorShift_Top,
            GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
            ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
            ExposureCompensation = Lighting.ExposureCompensation,
            FogColor = Lighting.FogColor,
            FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
            FogStart = Lighting.FogStart,
            EnvironmentDiffuseScale = Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale,
            EnvironmentSpecularScale = Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale,
        }
    end)
    if ok and snapshot then
        fullBrightState.Original = snapshot
    end
end
function disconnectFullBrightConnection()
    if fullBrightState.Connection then
        pcall(function()
            fullBrightState.Connection:Disconnect()
        end)
        fullBrightState.Connection = nil
    end
end
function restoreFullBrightState()
    local original = fullBrightState.Original
    if not original then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        Lighting.Brightness = original.Brightness
        Lighting.Ambient = original.Ambient
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = original.OutdoorAmbient
        Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = original.ColorShift_Bottom
        Lighting.ColorShift_Top = original.ColorShift_Top
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = original.GlobalShadows
        Lighting.ClockTime = original.ClockTime
        Lighting.ExposureCompensation = original.ExposureCompensation
        Lighting.FogColor = original.FogColor
        Lighting.FogEnd = original.FogEnd
        Lighting.FogStart = original.FogStart
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = original.EnvironmentDiffuseScale
        Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = original.EnvironmentSpecularScale
    end)
end
function applyFullBright()
    pcall(function()
        if Lighting.GlobalShadows ~= false then
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
        end
        if Lighting.Brightness ~= 1 then
            Lighting.Brightness = 1
        end
        local white = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        if Lighting.Ambient ~= white then
            Lighting.Ambient = white
        end
        if Lighting.OutdoorAmbient ~= white then
            Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = white
        end
        if Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom ~= white then
            Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = white
        end
        if Lighting.ColorShift_Top ~= white then
            Lighting.ColorShift_Top = white
        end
        if Lighting.ClockTime ~= 12 then
            Lighting.ClockTime = 12
        end
        if Lighting.ExposureCompensation ~= 0 then
            Lighting.ExposureCompensation = 0
        end
        if Lighting.FogColor ~= white then
            Lighting.FogColor = white
        end
        if Lighting.FogEnd ~= 1e10 then
            Lighting.FogEnd = 1e10
        end
        if Lighting.FogStart ~= 0 then
            Lighting.FogStart = 0
        end
        if Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale ~= 1 then
            Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 1
        end
        if Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale ~= 1 then
            Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 1
        end
    end)
end
function setFullBrightEnabled(state)
    local enabled = state and true or false
    fullBrightState.Enabled = enabled
    Config.EnableFullBright = enabled
    if enabled then
        captureFullBrightState()
        applyFullBright()
        if not fullBrightState.Connection then
            fullBrightState.Connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not fullBrightState.Enabled then
                    return
                end
                applyFullBright()
            end)
        end
    else
        disconnectFullBrightConnection()
        restoreFullBrightState()
    end
    if fullBrightHooks.SaveSettings then
        pcall(function()
            fullBrightHooks.SaveSettings()
        end)
    end
end

function LoadSettings()
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(SettingsFile) then
            local content = readfile(SettingsFile)
            if content and content ~= "" then
                return HttpService:JSONDecode(content)
            end
        end
        return nil
    end)
    if ok and result then
        if result.EnableTeleportWalk ~= nil then Config.EnableTeleportWalk = result.EnableTeleportWalk end
        if result.TeleportMovementSpeed ~= nil then Config.TeleportMovementSpeed = result.TeleportMovementSpeed end
        if result.EnableEnhancedJump ~= nil then Config.EnableEnhancedJump = result.EnableEnhancedJump end
        if result.JumpHeight ~= nil then Config.JumpHeight = result.JumpHeight end
        if result.EnableInfiniteSlide ~= nil then Config.EnableInfiniteSlide = result.EnableInfiniteSlide end
        if result.InfiniteSlideBoost ~= nil then Config.InfiniteSlideBoost = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(result.InfiniteSlideBoost) or 8), 1, 150) end
        if result.AutoJump ~= nil then Config.AutoJump = result.AutoJump end
        if result.AutoJumpSpeed ~= nil then Config.AutoJumpSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(result.AutoJumpSpeed) or 1.5, 0.5, 8) end
        if result.AutoCrouch ~= nil then Config.AutoCrouch = result.AutoCrouch end
        if result.AutoCrouchSpeed ~= nil then Config.AutoCrouchSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(result.AutoCrouchSpeed) or 1.5, 0.5, 100) end
        if result.EnableJumpAcceleration ~= nil then Config.EnableJumpAcceleration = result.EnableJumpAcceleration end
        if result.JumpAccelerationSpeed ~= nil then Config.JumpAccelerationSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(result.JumpAccelerationSpeed) or 42, 30, 110) end
        if result.EnableGravityMod ~= nil then Config.EnableGravityMod = result.EnableGravityMod end
        if result.GravityScale ~= nil then Config.GravityScale = result.GravityScale end
        if result.EnableFrontalJump ~= nil then Config.EnableFrontalJump = result.EnableFrontalJump end
        if result.FrontalJumpSpeed ~= nil then getgenv().FrontalJumpSpeed = result.FrontalJumpSpeed end
        if result.RampMultiplier ~= nil then getgenv().RampMultiplier = result.RampMultiplier end
        if result.EnableRampMultiplier ~= nil then Config.EnableRampMultiplier = result.EnableRampMultiplier end
        if result.RampMultiplierValue ~= nil then getgenv().RampMultiplierValue = result.RampMultiplierValue end
        if result.EnableFullBright ~= nil then Config.EnableFullBright = result.EnableFullBright end
        if result.EnableAutoTicket ~= nil then Config.EnableAutoTicket = result.EnableAutoTicket end
        if result.EnableVoidEscape ~= nil then Config.EnableVoidEscape = result.EnableVoidEscape end
        if result.EnableAfkMap     ~= nil then Config.EnableAfkMap     = result.EnableAfkMap     end
        if result.EnableGagMap     ~= nil then Config.EnableGagMap     = result.EnableGagMap     end
        if result.EnableAntiAfk    ~= nil then Config.EnableAntiAfk    = result.EnableAntiAfk    end
        if result.EnableWhistleESP ~= nil then Config.EnableWhistleESP = result.EnableWhistleESP end
        if result.EnableReviveAura ~= nil then Config.EnableReviveAura = result.EnableReviveAura end
        if result.EnableAutoSave   ~= nil then Config.EnableAutoSave   = result.EnableAutoSave   end
        if result.EnableEspDown    ~= nil then Config.EnableEspDown    = result.EnableEspDown    end
        if result.EnableAutoCarry ~= nil then Config.EnableAutoCarry = result.EnableAutoCarry end
        if result.CarryRange    ~= nil then Config.CarryRange    = result.CarryRange    end
        if result.CarryInterval ~= nil then Config.CarryInterval = result.CarryInterval end
        if result.EnableAutoForceSpawn ~= nil then Config.EnableAutoForceSpawn = result.EnableAutoForceSpawn end
        if result.EnableAntiBot ~= nil then Config.EnableAntiBot = result.EnableAntiBot end
        if result.AntiBotDistance ~= nil then Config.AntiBotDistance = result.AntiBotDistance end
        if result.AntiBotCooldown ~= nil then Config.AntiBotCooldown = result.AntiBotCooldown end
        if result.EnableAutoTrimp ~= nil then Config.EnableAutoTrimp = result.EnableAutoTrimp end
        if result.AutoTrimpMultiplier ~= nil then Config.AutoTrimpMultiplier = result.AutoTrimpMultiplier end
        if result.AutoTrimpFallSpeed ~= nil then Config.AutoTrimpFallSpeed = result.AutoTrimpFallSpeed end
        if result.EnableTrimpTeleport ~= nil then Config.EnableTrimpTeleport = result.EnableTrimpTeleport end
        if result.EnableSpeedMultiplier ~= nil then Config.EnableSpeedMultiplier = result.EnableSpeedMultiplier end
        if result.SpeedMultiplierValue ~= nil then Config.SpeedMultiplierValue = result.SpeedMultiplierValue end
        if result.EnableEmoteSpeed ~= nil then Config.EnableEmoteSpeed = result.EnableEmoteSpeed end
        if result.EmoteSpeedMultiplier ~= nil then Config.EmoteSpeedMultiplier = result.EmoteSpeedMultiplier end
        if result.EnableLayerJumps ~= nil then Config.EnableLayerJumps = result.EnableLayerJumps end
        if result.LayerJumpsCount ~= nil then Config.LayerJumpsCount = result.LayerJumpsCount end
        if result.EnableImpulseJump ~= nil then Config.EnableImpulseJump = result.EnableImpulseJump end
        if result.ImpulseJumpSpeed ~= nil then Config.ImpulseJumpSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(result.ImpulseJumpSpeed) or 42, 10, 120) end
        if result.EnableBackJump ~= nil then Config.EnableBackJump = result.EnableBackJump end
        if result.BackJumpSpeed ~= nil then getgenv().BackJumpSpeed = result.BackJumpSpeed end
        if result.BackRampMultiplier ~= nil then getgenv().BackRampMultiplier = result.BackRampMultiplier end
        if result.EnableFigure8  ~= nil then Config.EnableFigure8  = result.EnableFigure8 end
        if result.Figure8Speed   ~= nil then Config.Figure8Speed   = math.clamp(tonumber(result.Figure8Speed)  or 1.5, 0.2, 3.0) end
        if result.Figure8Radius  ~= nil then Config.Figure8Radius  = math.clamp(tonumber(result.Figure8Radius) or 8,   3,   25)  end
        if result.EnableLissajous  ~= nil then Config.EnableLissajous  = result.EnableLissajous end
        if result.LissajousSpeed   ~= nil then Config.LissajousSpeed   = math.clamp(tonumber(result.LissajousSpeed)  or 1.5, 0.2, 3.0) end
        if result.LissajousRadius  ~= nil then Config.LissajousRadius  = math.clamp(tonumber(result.LissajousRadius) or 8,   3,   25)  end
        if result.EnableSpiro  ~= nil then Config.EnableSpiro  = result.EnableSpiro end
        if result.SpiroSpeed   ~= nil then Config.SpiroSpeed   = math.clamp(tonumber(result.SpiroSpeed)  or 0.8, 0.1, 2.0)  end
        if result.SpiroRadius  ~= nil then Config.SpiroRadius  = math.clamp(tonumber(result.SpiroRadius) or 12,  5,   30)   end
        if result.SpiroRatio   ~= nil then Config.SpiroRatio   = math.clamp(tonumber(result.SpiroRatio)  or 0.35, 0.1, 0.9) end
        print("SETTINGS: Previous configuration loaded successfully")
    else
        print("SETTINGS: No saved configuration found, using defaults")
    end
end

local LIBRARY_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ProtectPoyect/Lib-Yin/refs/heads/main/Yi"
local LIBRARY_LOAD_ATTEMPTS = 3
local LIBRARY_LOAD_RETRY_WAIT = 1.5
local function notifyLoadStatus(text, duration)
    pcall(function()
        game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
            Title = "Yin Yang",
            Text = text,
            Duration = duration or 6,
        })
    end)
end
local success = false
local lastLoadError = nil
for attempt = 1, LIBRARY_LOAD_ATTEMPTS do
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        loadstring(game:HttpGet(LIBRARY_URL))()
    end)
    if ok and _G.YinYang then
        success = true
        break
    end
    lastLoadError = err
    warn(string.format("[Yin Yang] Intento %d/%d de carga fallido: %s", attempt, LIBRARY_LOAD_ATTEMPTS, tostring(err)))
    if attempt < LIBRARY_LOAD_ATTEMPTS then
        notifyLoadStatus("No se pudo cargar la interfaz, reintentando (" .. attempt .. "/" .. LIBRARY_LOAD_ATTEMPTS .. ")...", 4)
        task.wait(LIBRARY_LOAD_RETRY_WAIT)
    end
end
if not success then
    notifyLoadStatus("No se pudo cargar la interfaz tras " .. LIBRARY_LOAD_ATTEMPTS .. " intentos. Revisa tu conexión y volvé a ejecutar el script.", 10)
    error("ERROR: Failed to load Yin Yang v28 Final — " .. tostring(lastLoadError))
    return
end
print("LIBRARY: Yin Yang v28 Final loaded successfully")
task.wait(0.5)
print("SYSTEM: Initialization completed")
print("\nCreating EVADE v5.2 Beta interface...")
function GetSavedTheme()
    local theme = "Dark"
    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile("Yin_Yang_Config.txt") then
            local content = readfile("Yin_Yang_Config.txt")
            if content and content ~= "" then
                local value = content:match("theme:([^|]+)")
                if value then
                    theme = value
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    return theme
end
local UI = _G.YinYang:CreateWindow("Yin Yang", GetSavedTheme())
task.spawn(function()
    local okAll, errAll = pcall(function()
        if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then return end
        local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
        local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        local createdButtons, currentMouseHold = {}, {}
        for _, n in ipairs({"LookBack", "ADS"}) do
            pcall(function()
                require(ReplicatedStorage.Objects.Game.Actions.ActionTypes[n]).Requirements.Mobile = nil
            end)
        end
        local function sendKey(key, down)
            pcall(function()
                ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Services", 5):WaitForChild("Client", 5)
                    :WaitForChild("KeybindService", 5):WaitForChild("SendKeybindEvent", 5)
                    :Fire({ Key = key, Down = down })
            end)
        end
        local keybindButtonsConfig = {
            { name = "SecondaryButton",   layoutOrder = 999, icon = "rbxassetid://126943351764139", label = "Zoom",              key = "Secondary" },
            { name = "ReloadButton",      layoutOrder = 997, icon = "rbxassetid://78648212535999",  label = "Front View/Reload", key = "Reload" },
            { name = "LeaderboardButton", layoutOrder = 998, icon = "rbxassetid://5107166345",      label = "Leaderboard",       key = "Leaderboard" },
        }
        local function findTargetParent()
            local topbar = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("TopbarStandard")
            local main = topbar and topbar:FindFirstChild("Main")
            local holders = main and main:FindFirstChild("Holders")
            return holders and holders:FindFirstChild("Left") or nil
        end
        local function createFallbackParent()
            local old = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("TopbarStandard")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
            pcall(function() StarterGui:SetCore("TopbarEnabled", false) end)
            local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            screenGui.Name = "TopbarStandard"
            screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = false
            screenGui.ScreenInsets = Enum.ScreenInsets.TopbarSafeInsets
            screenGui.DisplayOrder = 100
            screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            screenGui.Parent = PlayerGui
            local holders = Instance.new("Frame")
            holders.Name = "Holders"
            holders.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            holders.BorderSizePixel = 0
            holders.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
            holders.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -2)
            holders.Parent = screenGui
            local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
            frame.Name = "Main"
            frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
            frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            frame.Parent = holders
            local scrollingFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
            scrollingFrame.Name = "Right"
            scrollingFrame.Parent = frame
            scrollingFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            scrollingFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            scrollingFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
            scrollingFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0)
            scrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
            scrollingFrame.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
            scrollingFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 0
            scrollingFrame.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X
            scrollingFrame.ScrollingEnabled = false
            local uiListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
            uiListLayout.Parent = scrollingFrame
            uiListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 12)
            uiListLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            uiListLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
            uiListLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
            uiListLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
