        end
        local function createKeybindButton(config, parent)
            local Button = Instance.new("Frame")
            Button.Name = config.name
            Button.Parent = parent
            Button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            Button.ClipsDescendants = true
            Button.LayoutOrder = config.layoutOrder
            Button.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 44)
            Button.ZIndex = 20
            local IconButton = Instance.new("Frame")
            IconButton.Name = "IconButton"
            IconButton.Parent = Button
            IconButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            IconButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            IconButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
            IconButton.ClipsDescendants = true
            IconButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            IconButton.ZIndex = 2
            local UICorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            UICorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            UICorner.Parent = IconButton
            local IconImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            IconImage.Parent = IconButton
            IconImage.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            IconImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            IconImage.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            IconImage.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            IconImage.ZIndex = 15
            IconImage.Image = config.icon
            local IconImageRatio = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
            IconImageRatio.Parent = IconImage
            IconImageRatio.DominantAxis = Enum.DominantAxis.Height
            local ClickRegion = Instance.new("TextButton")
            ClickRegion.Parent = IconButton
            ClickRegion.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ClickRegion.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            ClickRegion.ZIndex = 20
            ClickRegion.Text = ""
            local IconOverlay = Instance.new("Frame")
            IconOverlay.Parent = IconButton
            IconOverlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            IconOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 0.925
            IconOverlay.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            IconOverlay.Visible = false
            IconOverlay.ZIndex = 6
            local UICorner_Overlay = Instance.new("UICorner")
            UICorner_Overlay.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            UICorner_Overlay.Parent = IconOverlay
            local function updateVisualState()
                IconOverlay.Visible = currentMouseHold[config.key] or false
            end
            ClickRegion.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
                currentMouseHold[config.key] = true
                sendKey(config.key, true)
                updateVisualState()
            end)
            ClickRegion.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
                if currentMouseHold[config.key] then
                    currentMouseHold[config.key] = false
                    sendKey(config.key, false)
                    updateVisualState()
                end
            end)
            createdButtons[config.key] = { Button = Button, updateVisualState = updateVisualState }
        end
        local function ensureButtons(parent)
            for _, config in ipairs(keybindButtonsConfig) do
                local data = createdButtons[config.key]
                if not (data and data.Button and data.Button.Parent) then
                    local stale = parent:FindFirstChild(config.name)
                    if stale then stale:Destroy() end
                    createKeybindButton(config, parent)
                end
            end
        end
        local targetParent = findTargetParent()
        local waited = 0
        while not targetParent and waited < 20 do
            task.wait(0.5)
            waited = waited + 0.5
            targetParent = findTargetParent()
        end
        if targetParent then
            pcall(function() StarterGui:SetCore("TopbarEnabled", true) end)
        else
            targetParent = createFallbackParent()
        end
        ensureButtons(targetParent)
        while true do
            task.wait(2)
            local needs = false
            for _, config in ipairs(keybindButtonsConfig) do
                local data = createdButtons[config.key]
                if not (data and data.Button and data.Button.Parent and data.Button:IsDescendantOf(PlayerGui)) then
                    needs = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if needs then
                local parent = findTargetParent()
                if not parent then
                    local fb = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("TopbarStandard")
                    parent = fb and fb:FindFirstChild("Right", true) or nil
                end
                if parent then
                    pcall(ensureButtons, parent)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    if not okAll then
        warn("[Botones barra superior] Error: " .. tostring(errAll))
    end
end)
function setupRainbowInversedTitle()
    local PlayerGui = Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    task.wait(0.8)
    if PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Yin") then
        local ScreenGui = PlayerGui.Yin
        local TitleLabel = ScreenGui:FindFirstChild("TitleLabel")
        if TitleLabel then
            print("Creating inverse rainbow effect with two TextLabels...")
            local origText = TitleLabel.Text
            local origSize = TitleLabel.Size
            local origPos = TitleLabel.Position
            local origFont = TitleLabel.Font
            local origTextSize = TitleLabel.TextSize
            TitleLabel.TextTransparency = 1
            local EvadeLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            EvadeLabel.Name = "EvadeLabel"
            EvadeLabel.Text = "EVADE"
            EvadeLabel.Font = origFont
            EvadeLabel.TextSize = origTextSize
            EvadeLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            EvadeLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            EvadeLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 1, 0)
            EvadeLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
            EvadeLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            EvadeLabel.ZIndex = TitleLabel.ZIndex + 1
            EvadeLabel.Parent = TitleLabel.Parent
            local BetaLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            BetaLabel.Name = "BetaLabel"
            BetaLabel.Text = "v5.2 Beta"
            BetaLabel.Font = origFont
            BetaLabel.TextSize = origTextSize
            BetaLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            BetaLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            BetaLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 1, 0)
            BetaLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 0)
            BetaLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            BetaLabel.ZIndex = TitleLabel.ZIndex + 1
            BetaLabel.Parent = TitleLabel.Parent
            print("✅ Labels created: EvadeLabel + BetaLabel")
            local cycleCount = 0
            local rainbowFrameCounter = 0
            local rainbow = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if not EvadeLabel or not EvadeLabel.Parent or not BetaLabel or not BetaLabel.Parent then
                    rainbow:Disconnect()
                    return
                end
                rainbowFrameCounter = rainbowFrameCounter + 1
                if rainbowFrameCounter % 3 ~= 0 then return end
                cycleCount = cycleCount + 1
                local progress = (cycleCount % 120) / 120
                if progress < 0.5 then
                    local t = progress * 2
                    local evadeBrightness = 1 - t
                    EvadeLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(
                        math.floor(255 * evadeBrightness),
                        math.floor(255 * evadeBrightness),
                        math.floor(255 * evadeBrightness)
                    )
                    local betaBrightness = t
                    BetaLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(
                        math.floor(255 * betaBrightness),
                        math.floor(255 * betaBrightness),
                        math.floor(255 * betaBrightness)
                    )
                else
                    local t = (progress - 0.5) * 2
                    local evadeBrightness = t
                    EvadeLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(
                        math.floor(255 * evadeBrightness),
                        math.floor(255 * evadeBrightness),
                        math.floor(255 * evadeBrightness)
                    )
                    local betaBrightness = 1 - t
                    BetaLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(
                        math.floor(255 * betaBrightness),
                        math.floor(255 * betaBrightness),
                        math.floor(255 * betaBrightness)
                    )
                end
            end)
            print("✅ Inverse rainbow effect activated")
        end
    end
end
task.delay(1, setupRainbowInversedTitle)
local ScriptLanguage = "en"
local ScriptTranslator = nil
local ScriptTranslationCache = {}
local function scriptText(spanishText, englishText)
    spanishText = tostring(spanishText or "")
    englishText = tostring(englishText or spanishText)
    if ScriptLanguage == "es" then
        return spanishText
    end
    if ScriptLanguage == "en" then
        return englishText
    end
    local cacheKey = ScriptLanguage .. "\0" .. englishText
    if ScriptTranslationCache[cacheKey] ~= nil then
        return ScriptTranslationCache[cacheKey]
    end
    local translated = nil
    if ScriptTranslator then
        pcall(function()
            translated = ScriptTranslator:Translate(game, englishText)
        end)
    end
    if translated and translated ~= "" and translated ~= englishText then
        ScriptTranslationCache[cacheKey] = translated
        return translated
    end
    ScriptTranslationCache[cacheKey] = englishText
    return englishText
end
local function refreshScriptLanguage()
    local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    local screenGui = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("Yin")
    if not screenGui then
        return
    end
    for _, object in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do
        if object:IsA("TextLabel") or object:IsA("TextButton") or object:IsA("TextBox") then
            local spanishText = object:GetAttribute("TextSpanish")
            local englishText = object:GetAttribute("TextEnglish")
            if spanishText ~= nil and englishText ~= nil then
                object.Text = scriptText(spanishText, englishText)
            end
            local placeholderSpanish = object:GetAttribute("PlaceholderSpanish")
            local placeholderEnglish = object:GetAttribute("PlaceholderEnglish")
            if placeholderSpanish ~= nil and placeholderEnglish ~= nil then
                object.PlaceholderText = scriptText(placeholderSpanish, placeholderEnglish)
            end
        end
    end
end
local function syncScriptLanguage()
    local ok, language = pcall(function()
        return _G.YinYang:GetLanguage()
    end)
    if not (ok and type(language) == "string" and language ~= "") then
        language = "en"
        pcall(function()
            language = string.lower(tostring(LocalizationService.SystemLocaleId)):match("^(%a+)") or "en"
        end)
    end
    ScriptLanguage = language
    ScriptTranslationCache = {}
    refreshScriptLanguage()
end
syncScriptLanguage()
pcall(function()
    _G.YinYang:OnLanguageChanged(syncScriptLanguage)
end)
task.spawn(function()
    local ok, translator = pcall(function()
        return LocalizationService:GetTranslatorForPlayerAsync(LocalPlayer)
    end)
    if ok and translator then
        ScriptTranslator = translator
        ScriptTranslationCache = {}
        refreshScriptLanguage()
    end
end)

function GetAvatarPalette()
    local active = nil
    pcall(function()
        active = UI.AllThemes[UI.CurrentTheme]
    end)
    local fallback = {
        Background = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 18),
        Secondary = Color3.fromRGB(32, 32, 36),
        AccentOff = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 66),
        Text = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 248),
        TextDim = Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 175),
        Stroke = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 160),
        Accent = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240),
    }
    local palette = {}
    for key, value in pairs(fallback) do
        local themed = type(active) == "table" and active[key] or nil
        palette[key] = (typeof(themed) == "Color3") and themed or value
    end
    return palette
end
function AvatarContrastColor(color)
    local luminance = 0.299 * color.R + 0.587 * color.G + 0.114 * color.B
    if luminance > 0.6 then
        return Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
    end
    return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
end
function AvatarAdaptStatusColor(color)
    local background = GetAvatarPalette().Background
    local luminance = 0.299 * background.R + 0.587 * background.G + 0.114 * background.B
    if luminance > 0.55 then
        return Color3.new(color.R * 0.5, color.G * 0.5, color.B * 0.5)
    end
    return color
end
function AvatarCorner(parent, radius)
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
    corner.Parent = parent
end
function createAvatarStroke(parent, transparency)
    local border = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    border.Color = GetAvatarPalette().Stroke
    border.Thickness = 1
    border.Transparency = transparency or 0.55
    pcall(function()
        border.ZIndex = 12
    end)
    border.Parent = parent
    border:SetAttribute("ThemeRole", "Stroke")
    return border
end

AvatarStatus.Name = "AvatarStatus"
AvatarStatus.Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 1, -12)
AvatarStatus.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 6)

AvatarStatus.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center

AvatarStatus:SetAttribute("TextEnglish", "")
local avatarStatusSpanish, avatarStatusEnglish = "", ""
local avatarStatusColor = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
function SetAvatarStatus(spanish, english, color)
    avatarStatusSpanish = spanish
    avatarStatusEnglish = english
    avatarStatusColor = color or Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
    AvatarStatusHolder.Visible = true
    AvatarStatus:SetAttribute("TextSpanish", spanish)
    AvatarStatus:SetAttribute("TextEnglish", english)
    AvatarStatus.Text = scriptText(spanish, english)
    AvatarStatus.TextColor3 = AvatarAdaptStatusColor(avatarStatusColor)
    AvatarStatusIcon.ImageColor3 = AvatarAdaptStatusColor(avatarStatusColor)
end
function GetAvatarVisualHumanoid()
    local rigsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Rigs")
    local visualRig = rigsFolder and rigsFolder:FindFirstChild(Players.LocalPlayer.Name)
    local humanoid = visualRig and visualRig:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return visualRig, humanoid
end
function ApplyAvatarByUsername()
    local username = tostring(AvatarUsernameInput.Text or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    if username == "" then
        SetAvatarStatus("Escribe un nombre de usuario.", "Enter a username.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 135, 135))
        return
    end
    local visualRig, visualHumanoid = GetAvatarVisualHumanoid()
    if not visualRig or not visualHumanoid then
        SetAvatarStatus("No se encontró el rig visual R6.", "The visual R6 rig was not found.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 135, 135))
        return
    end
    if visualHumanoid.RigType ~= Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
        SetAvatarStatus("El rig visual no es R6.", "The visual rig is not R6.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 135, 135))
        return
    end
    SetAvatarStatus("Cargando avatar de " .. username .. "...", "Loading " .. username .. "'s avatar...", Color3.fromRGB(195, 215, 255))
    local okUser, userIdOrError = pcall(function()
        return Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(username)
    end)
    if not okUser then
        SetAvatarStatus("No se encontró ese usuario.", "That user was not found.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 135, 135))
        return
    end
    local okDescription, descriptionOrError = pcall(function()
        return Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(userIdOrError)
    end)
    if not okDescription then
        SetAvatarStatus("No se pudo cargar el avatar.", "The avatar could not be loaded.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 135, 135))
        return
    end
    local okApply, applyError = pcall(function()
        visualHumanoid:ApplyDescriptionReset(descriptionOrError)
    end)
    if not okApply then
        SetAvatarStatus("No se pudo aplicar el avatar.", "The avatar could not be applied.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 135, 135))
        warn("[Avatar] ApplyDescriptionReset error:", applyError)
        return
    end
    SetAvatarStatus(
        "Avatar de " .. username .. " aplicado. Escribe otro nombre para cambiarlo.",
        username .. "'s avatar was applied. Enter another name to change it.",
        Color3.fromRGB(145, 255, 180)
    )
end
AvatarApplyButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(ApplyAvatarByUsername)
AvatarUsernameInput.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
    if enterPressed then
        ApplyAvatarByUsername()
    end
end)

    local textColor = AvatarHeaderTitle.TextColor3
    AvatarHeaderIcon.ImageColor3 = textColor
    AvatarInputIcon.ImageColor3 = textColor
end
RefreshAvatarThemeIcons()
AvatarHeaderTitle:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextColor3"):Connect(RefreshAvatarThemeIcons)
function RefreshAvatarThemeDesign()
    local palette = GetAvatarPalette()
    local onAccent = AvatarContrastColor(palette.Accent)
    AvatarApplyLabel.TextColor3 = onAccent
    AvatarApplyArrow.TextColor3 = onAccent
    AvatarUsernameInput.PlaceholderColor3 = palette.TextDim
    AvatarStatus.TextColor3 = AvatarAdaptStatusColor(avatarStatusColor)
    AvatarStatusIcon.ImageColor3 = AvatarAdaptStatusColor(avatarStatusColor)
end
RefreshAvatarThemeDesign()
AvatarApplyHolder:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundColor3"):Connect(RefreshAvatarThemeDesign)
AvatarDescription:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextColor3"):Connect(RefreshAvatarThemeDesign)

TabAvatar:CreateDivider()
TabAvatar:CreateLabel("PARTES CORPORALES", "CUSTOM BODY PARTS", 12)
local CustomBodyPartState = {
    Saved = {},
    HeadlessFallback = {
        Active = false,
        HeadTransparency = nil,
        FaceTransparency = nil,
    },
}
local CustomBodyParts = {
    Korblox = {
        Spanish = "Pierna derecha Korblox",
        English = "Korblox Right Leg",
        Property = "RightLeg",
        AssetId = 139607718,
    },
    Headless = {
        Spanish = "Cabeza invisible Headless",
        English = "Headless Invisible Head",
        Property = "Head",
        AssetId = 15093053680,
    },
}
function SetHeadlessFallback(visualRig, enabled)
    local head = visualRig and visualRig:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head or not head:IsA("BasePart") then
        return false
    end
    local face = head:FindFirstChild("face")
    if enabled then
        if not CustomBodyPartState.HeadlessFallback.Active then
            CustomBodyPartState.HeadlessFallback.HeadTransparency = head.LocalTransparencyModifier
            CustomBodyPartState.HeadlessFallback.FaceTransparency = face and face:IsA("Decal") and face.Transparency or nil
        end
        head.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
        if face and face:IsA("Decal") then
            face.Transparency = 1
        end
        CustomBodyPartState.HeadlessFallback.Active = true
    else
        head.LocalTransparencyModifier = CustomBodyPartState.HeadlessFallback.HeadTransparency or 0
        if face and face:IsA("Decal") and CustomBodyPartState.HeadlessFallback.FaceTransparency ~= nil then
            face.Transparency = CustomBodyPartState.HeadlessFallback.FaceTransparency
        end
        CustomBodyPartState.HeadlessFallback = {
            Active = false,
            HeadTransparency = nil,
            FaceTransparency = nil,
        }
    end
    return true
end
function ApplyCustomBodyPart(partInfo, enabled)
    local visualRig, visualHumanoid = GetAvatarVisualHumanoid()
    if not visualRig or not visualHumanoid then
        SetAvatarStatus("No se encontró el rig visual R6.", "The visual R6 rig was not found.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 135, 135))
        return false
    end
    if visualHumanoid.RigType ~= Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
        SetAvatarStatus("Las piezas requieren un rig visual R6.", "Custom parts require the visual R6 rig.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 135, 135))
        return false
    end
    local okDescription, descriptionOrError = pcall(function()
        return visualHumanoid:GetAppliedDescription()
    end)
    if not okDescription or not descriptionOrError then
        SetAvatarStatus("No se pudo leer el avatar actual.", "The current avatar description could not be read.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 135, 135))
        warn("[Avatar] GetAppliedDescription error:", descriptionOrError)
        return false
    end
    if enabled then
        if CustomBodyPartState.Saved[partInfo.Property] == nil then
            CustomBodyPartState.Saved[partInfo.Property] = descriptionOrError[partInfo.Property]
        end
        descriptionOrError[partInfo.Property] = partInfo.AssetId
        SetAvatarStatus("Aplicando " .. partInfo.Spanish .. "...", "Applying " .. partInfo.English .. "...", Color3.fromRGB(195, 215, 255))
    else
        descriptionOrError[partInfo.Property] = CustomBodyPartState.Saved[partInfo.Property] or 0
        CustomBodyPartState.Saved[partInfo.Property] = nil
        if partInfo.Property == "Head" and CustomBodyPartState.HeadlessFallback.Active then
            SetHeadlessFallback(visualRig, false)
        end
    end
    local okApply, applyError = pcall(function()
        visualHumanoid:ApplyDescriptionReset(descriptionOrError)
    end)
    if not okApply then
        if enabled and partInfo.Property == "Head" and SetHeadlessFallback(visualRig, true) then
            SetAvatarStatus("Cabeza invisible aplicada localmente.", "Headless fallback applied locally.", Color3.fromRGB(145, 255, 180))
            warn("[Avatar] Headless Dynamic Head fallback used:", applyError)
            return true
        end
        SetAvatarStatus("No se pudo aplicar la pieza.", "The body part could not be applied.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 135, 135))
        warn("[Avatar] ApplyDescriptionReset error:", applyError)
        return false
    end
    SetAvatarStatus(
        enabled and "Pieza aplicada al rig visual." or "Pieza restaurada.",
        enabled and "Body part applied to the visual rig." or "Body part restored.",
        enabled and Color3.fromRGB(145, 255, 180) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
    )
    return true
end
local KorbloxRightLegToggle
local HeadlessHeadToggle
KorbloxRightLegToggle = TabAvatar:CreateToggle(
    CustomBodyParts.Korblox.Spanish,
    CustomBodyParts.Korblox.English,
    false,

        local ok = ApplyCustomBodyPart(CustomBodyParts.Korblox, enabled)
        if not ok and enabled then
            KorbloxRightLegToggle.SetValue(false)
        end
    end
)
HeadlessHeadToggle = TabAvatar:CreateToggle(
    CustomBodyParts.Headless.Spanish,
    CustomBodyParts.Headless.English,
    false,
    function(enabled)
        local ok = ApplyCustomBodyPart(CustomBodyParts.Headless, enabled)
        if not ok and enabled then
            HeadlessHeadToggle.SetValue(false)
        end
    end
)
local HeadlessDeletedToggle
local function RemoveHeadAccessories(visualRig)
    local typeNames = { Hat = true, Hair = true, Face = true, Eyebrow = true, Eyelash = true }
    local attachmentNames = { HatAttachment = true, HairAttachment = true, FaceFrontAttachment = true, FaceCenterAttachment = true }
    local removed = 0
    for _, child in ipairs(visualRig:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Accessory") then
            local isHeadItem = false
            local okType, accessoryType = pcall(function()
                return child.AccessoryType
            end)
            if okType and accessoryType and typeNames[accessoryType.Name] then
                isHeadItem = true
            end
            if not isHeadItem then
                local handle = child:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                local attachment = handle and handle:FindFirstChildOfClass("Attachment")
                if attachment and attachmentNames[attachment.Name] then
                    isHeadItem = true
                end
            end
            if isHeadItem then
                child:Destroy()
                removed = removed + 1
            end
        end
    end
    return removed
end
HeadlessDeletedToggle = TabAvatar:CreateToggle(
    "Headless + Deleted",
    "Headless + Deleted",
    false,
    function(enabled)
        if enabled and HeadlessHeadToggle and HeadlessHeadToggle.GetValue() then
            HeadlessHeadToggle.SetValue(false)
        end
        local ok = ApplyCustomBodyPart(CustomBodyParts.Headless, enabled)
        if not ok then
            if enabled then
                HeadlessDeletedToggle.SetValue(false)
            end
            return
        end
        if enabled then
            local visualRig = GetAvatarVisualHumanoid()
            if visualRig then
                RemoveHeadAccessories(visualRig)
                SetAvatarStatus(
                    "Headless aplicada y accesorios de la cabeza eliminados.",
                    "Headless applied and head accessories removed.",
                    Color3.fromRGB(145, 255, 180)
                )
            end
        end
    end
)
end
print("\nConfiguring Move tab...")
local lastSpeedSelf, lastSpeedState, lastSpeedValue = nil, nil, nil
getgenv().YYSpeedRestoreRequested = false
getgenv().YYRestoreSpeedMultiplier = nil
do
    local FakeTrimp = {
        Enabled = false,
        MovementType = "Camera",
        CameraDirection = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),
        SmoothAcceleration = true,
        SpeedAcceleration = 9,
        MaxSpeed = 99,
        State = { MoveDir = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) }
    }
    local currentSpeed = 0
    local currentDir = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
    local oldUpdate = nil
    local DaraTrimpToggle = nil
    local movementOk, Movement = pcall(function()
        return require(ReplicatedStorage.Objects.Game.Character.Client.Movement)
    end)
    local trimpReady = movementOk and type(Movement) == "table" and Movement.Update ~= nil
    local function FakeTimpUpdate(self, dt)
        oldUpdate(self, dt)
        if not FakeTrimp.Enabled then
            currentSpeed = 0
            return
        end
        local hrp = self.Character and self.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = self.Character and self.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not humanoid then return end
        local moveDir = Vector3.new()
        if FakeTrimp.MovementType == "Camera" then
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam then
                local lookDir = cam.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(FakeTrimp.CameraDirection)
                moveDir = Vector3.new(lookDir.X, 0, lookDir.Z).Unit
            end
        else
            moveDir = humanoid.MoveDirection
        end
        if moveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
            FakeTrimp.State.MoveDir = moveDir.Unit
        end
        currentDir = FakeTrimp.State.MoveDir
        local targetSpeed = FakeTrimp.MaxSpeed * 90
        if FakeTrimp.SmoothAcceleration then
            currentSpeed = currentSpeed + (targetSpeed - currentSpeed) * math.min(FakeTrimp.SpeedAcceleration * dt, 1)
        else
            currentSpeed = targetSpeed
        end
        local newVelocity = currentDir * currentSpeed
        self.DataRegistry:Set("Velocity", newVelocity)
        local horizontal = Vector3.new(newVelocity.X, 0, newVelocity.Z) / 90
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(horizontal.X, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, horizontal.Z)
    end
    if trimpReady and not oldUpdate then
        oldUpdate = Movement.Update
        Movement.Update = FakeTimpUpdate
    end
    DaraTrimpToggle = TabMove:CreateFloatingToggle(
        "Dara Trimp",
        "Dara Trimp",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            if not trimpReady then
                if enabled and DaraTrimpToggle then DaraTrimpToggle.SetValue(false) end
                warn("[Dara Trimp] No disponible: no se encontro ReplicatedStorage.Objects.Game.Character.Client.Movement en este juego")
                return
            end
            FakeTrimp.Enabled = enabled
            if not enabled then currentSpeed = 0 end
        end,
        {
            imageId = "rbxassetid://77466337396355",
            name = "Darks",
            description = "Thanks to one of the creators of dara hub for contributing with us!"
        }
    )
    TabMove:CreateSlider(
        "Speed",
        1, 200, FakeTrimp.MaxSpeed,
        function(value)
            FakeTrimp.MaxSpeed = tonumber(value) or 99
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateSlider(
        "Acceleration",
        1, 30, FakeTrimp.SpeedAcceleration,
        function(value)
            FakeTrimp.SpeedAcceleration = tonumber(value) or 9
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateToggle(
        "Aceleración suave",
        "Smooth acceleration",
        FakeTrimp.SmoothAcceleration,
        function(enabled)
            FakeTrimp.SmoothAcceleration = enabled
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateToggle(
        "Dirección por movimiento (en vez de cámara)",
        "Move direction (instead of camera)",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            FakeTrimp.MovementType = enabled and "Humanoid" or "Camera"
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateDivider()
    UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
        if gpe then return end
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q then
            if not trimpReady then return end
            FakeTrimp.Enabled = not FakeTrimp.Enabled
            if not FakeTrimp.Enabled then currentSpeed = 0 end
            if DaraTrimpToggle then DaraTrimpToggle.SetValue(FakeTrimp.Enabled) end
            game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "Fake Trimp",
                Text = FakeTrimp.Enabled and "ON" or "OFF",
                Duration = 2
            })
        end
    end)
end
TabMove:CreateCombinedToggle("Combinado (prueba)", "Combined (test)")
TabMove:CreateDivider()
do
    local DARA_DEFAULT_FRICTION = 5           -- dara: defaultMovementValues.Friction
    local autoJumpEnabled = false             -- dara: autoJumpEnabled
    local autoJumpType = "Simulation"         -- dara: autoJumpType
    local bhopHoldEnabled = false             -- dara: bhopHoldEnabled
    local accelerationMethod = "Acceleration" -- dara: accelerationMethod
    local accelerationValue = -0.2            -- dara: accelerationValue
    local autoAccelerationEnabled = false     -- dara: autoAccelerationEnabled
    local maxAcceleration = 3                 -- dara: maxAcceleration
    local minAcceleration = -1                -- dara: minAcceleration
    local maxAutoAccelSpeed = 70              -- dara: maxAutoAccelSpeed
    local AutowallHop = false                 -- dara: AutowallHop
    local lastFriction = nil                  -- dara: lastFriction (compartido por bhop y slide, como en dara)
    local movementHooked = false
    local movementOldUpdate = nil
    local movementCharacterService = nil
    local DaraBhopToggle = nil
    local DaraBhopHoldToggle = nil
    local DaraAutoWallHopToggle = nil
    local function movementValueSet(movementType, value)
        if not firesignal then return end
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local tag = character and character:GetAttribute("Tag")
        if not tag then return end
        pcall(function()
            firesignal(ReplicatedStorage.Events.CharacterTask.OnClientEvent, tag, "ModifyMovement", { movementType, value })
        end)
    end
    local function getCurrentSpeed(character)
        if not character then return 0 end
        local hrp = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return 0 end
        for _, child in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("LinearVelocity") and child.Name:find("LinearVelocity") then
                local pv = child.PlaneVelocity
                return (pv.X * pv.X + pv.Y * pv.Y) ^ 0.5
            end
        end
        local velocity = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        return (velocity.X * velocity.X + velocity.Z * velocity.Z) ^ 0.5
    end
    local function movementGetCharacterService()
        if movementCharacterService then return movementCharacterService end
        local ok, svc = pcall(function()
            local services = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Services")
            local asset = services and services:FindFirstChild("Asset")
            local module = asset and asset:FindFirstChild("CharacterService")
            return module and require(module) or nil
        end)
        if ok and svc then movementCharacterService = svc end
        return movementCharacterService
    end
    local function updateBhopEnabled()
        local shouldEnable = (autoJumpEnabled or bhopHoldEnabled) and accelerationMethod == "Acceleration"
        movementValueSet("BhopEnabled", shouldEnable)
    end
    local function movementStep(self)
        local humanoid = self.Character and self.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid then return end
        local state = nil
        local svc = movementGetCharacterService()
        if svc then
            local charData = svc:GetCharacterFromPlayer(LocalPlayer)
            local movementData = charData and charData.Movement
            state = movementData and movementData.State
        end
        if not state then
            state = self.State
        end
        local grounded = self.DataRegistry:Get("Grounded")
        local desiredFriction = nil
        local isInfSlide = (state == "Slide" or state == "EmotingSlide" or state == "CarrySlide")
        if isInfSlide then
            if Config.EnableInfiniteSlide then
                desiredFriction = -Config.InfiniteSlideBoost
            end
        end
        if desiredFriction == nil and accelerationMethod == "Acceleration" then
            local isBhopActive = autoJumpEnabled or (bhopHoldEnabled and humanoid.Jump == true)
            if isBhopActive and not grounded then
                local currentSpeed = getCurrentSpeed(self.Character)
                if autoAccelerationEnabled then
                    if currentSpeed > maxAutoAccelSpeed then
                        desiredFriction = maxAcceleration
                    elseif currentSpeed < maxAutoAccelSpeed then
                        desiredFriction = minAcceleration
                    else
                        desiredFriction = accelerationValue
                    end
                else
                    desiredFriction = accelerationValue
                end
            end
        end
        if desiredFriction ~= lastFriction then
            if desiredFriction ~= nil then
                movementValueSet("Friction", desiredFriction)
            else
                movementValueSet("Friction", DARA_DEFAULT_FRICTION)
            end
            lastFriction = desiredFriction
        end
        if (state == "WallrunRight" or state == "WallrunLeft") and AutowallHop then
            self:AttemptJump()
        end
        if autoJumpEnabled and grounded then
            if autoJumpType == "Realistic" then
                self:AttemptJump()
            else
                humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
            end
        end
        if bhopHoldEnabled and grounded and humanoid.Jump == true then
            self:AttemptJump()
        end
    end
    local function movementEnsureHook()
        if movementHooked then return true end
        local ok, Movement = pcall(function()
            return require(ReplicatedStorage.Objects.Game.Character.Client.Movement)
        end)
        if not ok or type(Movement) ~= "table" or Movement.Update == nil then return false end
        movementOldUpdate = Movement.Update
        Movement.Update = function(self, ...)
            movementOldUpdate(self, ...)
            pcall(movementStep, self)
        end
        movementHooked = true
        return true
    end
    getgenv().YY_DaraMovement = { EnsureHook = movementEnsureHook }
    local function movementTryEnable(toggleRef)
        if not firesignal or not movementEnsureHook() then
            if toggleRef and toggleRef.SetValue then toggleRef.SetValue(false) end
            warn("[Bunny Hop] No disponible: falta firesignal o ReplicatedStorage.Objects.Game.Character.Client.Movement en este juego")
            return false
        end
        return true
    end
    DaraBhopToggle = TabMove:CreateFloatingToggle(
        "Bunny Hop",
        "Bunny Hop",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            if enabled and not movementTryEnable(DaraBhopToggle) then return end
            autoJumpEnabled = enabled
            updateBhopEnabled()
        end,
        {
            imageId = "rbxassetid://77466337396355",
            name = "Darks",
            description = "Thanks to one of the creators of dara hub for contributing with us!"
        }
    )
    DaraBhopHoldToggle = TabMove:CreateToggle(
        "Bhop Mantener Salto",
        "Bhop Hold Jump",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            if enabled and not movementTryEnable(DaraBhopHoldToggle) then return end
            bhopHoldEnabled = enabled
            updateBhopEnabled()
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateToggle(
        "Bhop con aceleración",
        "Bhop Acceleration Mode",
        true,
        function(enabled)
            accelerationMethod = enabled and "Acceleration" or "No Acceleration"   -- dara: SetAccelerationMethod
            updateBhopEnabled()
            if not (autoJumpEnabled or bhopHoldEnabled) then
                movementValueSet("Friction", DARA_DEFAULT_FRICTION)
                lastFriction = nil
            end
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateToggle(
        "Auto salto realista",
        "Auto Jump Realistic",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            autoJumpType = enabled and "Realistic" or "Simulation"
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateSlider(
        "Bhop Acceleration",
        -3, 0, accelerationValue,
        function(value)
            local n = tonumber(value)
            if n then
                accelerationValue = n
            end
        end
    )
    DaraAutoWallHopToggle = TabMove:CreateToggle(
        "Auto Wall Hop",
        "Auto Wall Hop",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            if enabled and not movementTryEnable(DaraAutoWallHopToggle) then return end
            AutowallHop = enabled
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateToggle(
        "Auto aceleración (Legit)",
        "Auto Acceleration (Legit)",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            autoAccelerationEnabled = enabled
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateSlider(
        "Max Acceleration",
        -3, 10, maxAcceleration,
        function(value)
            local n = tonumber(value)
            if n then
                maxAcceleration = n
            end
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateSlider(
        "Min Acceleration",
        -3, 10, minAcceleration,
        function(value)
            local n = tonumber(value)
            if n then
                minAcceleration = n
            end
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateSlider(
        "Max Speed",
        1, 300, maxAutoAccelSpeed,
        function(value)
            local n = tonumber(value)
            if n and n > 0 then
                maxAutoAccelSpeed = n
            end
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateDivider()
end
do
    local BounceHeight = 190             -- dara: BounceHeight
    local DaraSuperBounceToggle = nil
    local function triggerSuperBounce()
        if not LocalPlayer.Character then return end
        local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        local rootPart = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if humanoid and rootPart then
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
            rootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(rootPart.Velocity.X, BounceHeight, rootPart.Velocity.Z)
        end
    end
    DaraSuperBounceToggle = TabMove:CreateFloatingToggle(
        "Super Bounce",
        "Super Bounce",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            if not enabled then return end
            triggerSuperBounce()
            task.defer(function()
                if DaraSuperBounceToggle then DaraSuperBounceToggle.SetValue(false) end
            end)
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateSlider(
        "Bounce Height",
        10, 500, BounceHeight,
        function(value)
            BounceHeight = tonumber(value) or 50
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateDivider()
end
do
    local BounceMultiplier = 5          -- dara: BounceMultiplier
    local FallSpeedThreshold = 69       -- dara: FallSpeedThreshold
    local EdgeTrimpEnabled = false
    local LastFloorMaterial = Enum.Material.Air
    local LastPosition = Vector3.new()
    local WasFalling = false
    local EdgeDetected = false
    local EdgeCharacter = nil
    local EdgeHumanoid = nil
    local EdgeRootPart = nil
    local edgeTrimpConnection = nil
    local charAddedConnection = nil
    local function handleCharacterAdded(NewCharacter)
        EdgeCharacter = NewCharacter
        EdgeHumanoid = EdgeCharacter:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
        EdgeRootPart = EdgeCharacter:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        LastPosition = EdgeRootPart.Position
        WasFalling = false
        EdgeDetected = false
    end
    TabMove:CreateFloatingToggle(
        "Edge Trimp",
        "Edge Trimp",
        false,
        function(State)
            EdgeTrimpEnabled = State
            if State then
                if LocalPlayer.Character then
                    handleCharacterAdded(LocalPlayer.Character)
                end
                charAddedConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(handleCharacterAdded)
                edgeTrimpConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if not (EdgeCharacter and EdgeHumanoid and EdgeRootPart) then return end
                    local CurrentPosition = EdgeRootPart.Position
                    local Velocity = (CurrentPosition - LastPosition) / RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    LastPosition = CurrentPosition
                    local CurrentFloorMaterial = EdgeHumanoid.FloorMaterial
                    local IsFalling = EdgeHumanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall or EdgeHumanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping
                    if CurrentFloorMaterial ~= LastFloorMaterial and CurrentFloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air and not IsFalling then
                        EdgeDetected = true
                    else
                        EdgeDetected = false
                    end
                    if EdgeDetected and EdgeHumanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
                        local FallVelocity = Velocity.Y
                        if FallVelocity < -FallSpeedThreshold then
                            local BounceVelocity = math.abs(FallVelocity) * BounceMultiplier
                            EdgeRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(EdgeRootPart.Velocity.X, BounceVelocity, EdgeRootPart.Velocity.Z)
                        end
                    end
                    LastFloorMaterial = CurrentFloorMaterial
                    WasFalling = IsFalling
                end)
            else
                if edgeTrimpConnection then
                    edgeTrimpConnection:Disconnect()
                    edgeTrimpConnection = nil
                end
                if charAddedConnection then
                    charAddedConnection:Disconnect()
                    charAddedConnection = nil
                end
            end
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateSlider(
        "Bounce Height Multiplier",
        1, 20, BounceMultiplier,
        function(value)
            local Num = tonumber(value)
            if Num and Num > 0 then
                BounceMultiplier = Num
            end
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateSlider(
        "Falling Threshold",
        10, 200, FallSpeedThreshold,
        function(value)
            local Num = tonumber(value)
            if Num and Num > 0 then
                FallSpeedThreshold = Num
            end
        end
    )
    TabMove:CreateDivider()
end

TabMove:CreateDivider()

    Config.EnableSpeedMultiplier = state
    print(state and "✓ Speed Multiplier ENABLED" or "X Speed Multiplier DISABLED")
    if state then
        getgenv().YYSpeedRestoreRequested = false
        getgenv().YYSpeedApplyRequested = true
        if getgenv().YYApplySpeedMultiplier then
            getgenv().YYApplySpeedMultiplier()
        end
    else
        getgenv().YYSpeedRestoreRequested = true
        if getgenv().YYRestoreSpeedMultiplier then
            getgenv().YYRestoreSpeedMultiplier()
        end
    end
    SaveSettings()
end)

do
    local DaraInfiniteSlideToggle = nil
    local daraMovement = getgenv().YY_DaraMovement   -- publicado por el bloque de Bunny Hop (mas arriba): hook unico de Movement.Update, igual que dara
    local function slideEnsureHook()
        return daraMovement ~= nil and daraMovement.EnsureHook() == true
    end
    DaraInfiniteSlideToggle = TabMove:CreateFloatingToggle("Slide Inf", "Inf Slide", Config.EnableInfiniteSlide, function(state)
        if state and not (firesignal and slideEnsureHook()) then
            Config.EnableInfiniteSlide = false
            if DaraInfiniteSlideToggle then DaraInfiniteSlideToggle.SetValue(false) end
            warn("[Infinite Slide] No disponible: falta firesignal o ReplicatedStorage.Objects.Game.Character.Client.Movement en este juego")
            return
        end
        Config.EnableInfiniteSlide = state
        print(state and "✓ Infinite Slide ENABLED" or "X Infinite Slide DISABLED")
        SaveSettings()
    end)
    if Config.EnableInfiniteSlide and not (firesignal and slideEnsureHook()) then
        Config.EnableInfiniteSlide = false
        if DaraInfiniteSlideToggle and DaraInfiniteSlideToggle.SetValue then DaraInfiniteSlideToggle.SetValue(false) end
        warn("[Infinite Slide] No disponible: falta firesignal o ReplicatedStorage.Objects.Game.Character.Client.Movement en este juego")
    end
    TabMove:CreateSlider(
        "Slide Speed",
        1, 150, Config.InfiniteSlideBoost,
        function(value)
            Config.InfiniteSlideBoost = math.clamp(math.floor(value), 1, 150)
            print("✓ Infinite Slide speed: " .. Config.InfiniteSlideBoost)
            SaveSettings()
        end
    )
end

function setupBetaNewRainbow()
    local PlayerGui = Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    task.wait(1.2)
    if PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Yin") then
        local ScreenGui = PlayerGui.Yin
        for _, child in pairs(ScreenGui:GetDescendants()) do
            if child:IsA("TextLabel") and child.Text:find("Frontal Jump") then
                local parentFrame = child.Parent
                if parentFrame then
                    local BetaNewLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    BetaNewLabel.Name = "BetaLabel"
                    BetaNewLabel.Text = "Beta"
                    BetaNewLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    BetaNewLabel.TextSize = 12
                    BetaNewLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    BetaNewLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
                    BetaNewLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 1, 0)
                    BetaNewLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.7, 0, 0, 0)
                    BetaNewLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
                    BetaNewLabel.ZIndex = child.ZIndex + 2
                    BetaNewLabel.Parent = parentFrame
                    print("™ Creating Yellow-Black rainbow effect for Beta...")
                    local cycleCount = 0
                    local betaFrameCounter = 0
                    local rainbowBetaNew = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                        if not BetaNewLabel or not BetaNewLabel.Parent then
                            rainbowBetaNew:Disconnect()
                            return
                        end
                        betaFrameCounter = betaFrameCounter + 1
                        if betaFrameCounter % 3 ~= 0 then return end
                        cycleCount = cycleCount + 1
                        local progress = (cycleCount % 120) / 120
                        if progress < 0.5 then
                            local t = progress * 2
                            local brightness = 1 - t
                            BetaNewLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(
                                math.floor(255 * brightness),
                                math.floor(255 * brightness),
                                0
                            )
                        else
                            local t = (progress - 0.5) * 2
                            local brightness = t
                            BetaNewLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(
                                math.floor(255 * brightness),
                                math.floor(255 * brightness),
                                0
                            )
                        end
                    end)
                    print("✓ Yellow-Black rainbow effect activated for Beta")
                    return
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
task.delay(1.5, setupBetaNewRainbow)
local AfkMapSpawnCFrame  = nil
local AfkMapRespawnConn  = nil
local AntiAfkConn        = nil
local AFk_MAP_FILE       = "yin_yang_map.json"
local AFK_MAP_URL        = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Yinyangzx/yin_map/refs/heads/main/yin_yang_map.json"
function loadAfkMap()
    if isfile then
        local ok, raw = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, AFK_MAP_URL)
        if ok and raw and raw ~= "" then
            pcall(writefile, AFk_MAP_FILE, raw)
        end
    end
    if not isfile or not isfile(AFk_MAP_FILE) then return false, "Archivo no encontrado" end
    local ok, raw = pcall(readfile, AFk_MAP_FILE)
    if not ok then return false, "Error leyendo archivo" end
    local ok2, data = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(raw) end)
    if not ok2 or not data or not data.parts then return false, "Archivo inválido" end
    local old = workspace:FindFirstChild("YinYangAfkMap")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
    folder.Name = "YinYangAfkMap"
    folder.Parent = workspace
    local spawnCFrame = nil
    for _, p in ipairs(data.parts) do
        pcall(function()
            local part = Instance.new(p.t or "Part")
            part.Size = Vector3.new(p.sx, p.sy, p.sz)
            part.CFrame = CFrame.new(p.px, p.py, p.pz) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(p.rx), math.rad(p.ry), math.rad(p.rz))
            part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(p.cr, p.cg, p.cb)
            pcall(function() part.Material = Enum.Material:GetEnumItems()[p.m] or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic end)
            part.Transparency = p.tr or 0
            part.Anchored = p.an == 1
            part.CanCollide = p.ca == 1
            part.CastShadow = false
            part.Parent = folder
            if part:IsA("SpawnLocation") then
                spawnCFrame = part.CFrame
            end
        end)
    end
    AfkMapSpawnCFrame = (spawnCFrame or CFrame.new(0, 10, 0)) + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
    return true, data.count or #data.parts
end
function setAfkMapEnabled(state)
    Config.EnableAfkMap = state
    if state then
        if AfkMapSpawnCFrame then
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then hrp.CFrame = AfkMapSpawnCFrame end
        end
        if AfkMapRespawnConn then AfkMapRespawnConn:Disconnect() end
        AfkMapRespawnConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.wait(0.5)
            if Config.EnableAfkMap and AfkMapSpawnCFrame then
                local c = LocalPlayer.Character
                local h = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if h then h.CFrame = AfkMapSpawnCFrame end
            end
        end)
    else
        if AfkMapRespawnConn then AfkMapRespawnConn:Disconnect() AfkMapRespawnConn = nil end
        AfkMapSpawnCFrame = nil
        local old = workspace:FindFirstChild("YinYangAfkMap")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
    end
    SaveSettings()
end
do -- bloque de locales: libera 5 variables locales del script principal
local GagMapCachedScript  = nil
local GagMapSpawnCFrame   = nil
local GagMapRespawnConn   = nil
local GAG_MAP_URL         = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Yinyangzx/yin_map/refs/heads/main/Map.lua"
local GAG_MAP_FOLDER      = "__YY_GagMap"
function loadGagMap()
    if not GagMapCachedScript then
        local ok, raw = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, GAG_MAP_URL)
        if not ok or not raw or raw == "" then
            return false, "Error descargando el mapa"
        end
        GagMapCachedScript = raw
        print("[GagMap] Descargado y guardado en caché (" .. #raw .. " bytes)")
    else
        print("[GagMap] Usando caché en memoria (sin re-descarga)")
    end
    local fn, compileErr = loadstring(GagMapCachedScript)
    if not fn then return false, "Error compilando: " .. tostring(compileErr) end
    local old = workspace:FindFirstChild(GAG_MAP_FOLDER)
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
    folder.Name = GAG_MAP_FOLDER
    folder.Parent = workspace
    local beforeSet = {}
    for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
        beforeSet[child] = true
    end
    local ok2, err2 = pcall(fn)
    if not ok2 then
        folder:Destroy()
        return false, "Error ejecutando: " .. tostring(err2)
    end
    local spawnCFrame = nil
    local count = 0
    for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
        if not beforeSet[child] and child ~= folder then
            if child:IsA("SpawnLocation") then
                spawnCFrame = child.CFrame
            end
            child.Parent = folder
            count = count + 1
        end
    end
    GagMapSpawnCFrame = (spawnCFrame or CFrame.new(-104.5, 0.5, -13.5)) + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
    return true, count
end
function setGagMapEnabled(state)
    Config.EnableGagMap = state
    if state then
        if GagMapSpawnCFrame then
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then hrp.CFrame = GagMapSpawnCFrame end
        end
        if GagMapRespawnConn then GagMapRespawnConn:Disconnect() end
        GagMapRespawnConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.wait(0.5)
            if Config.EnableGagMap and GagMapSpawnCFrame then
                local c = LocalPlayer.Character
                local h = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if h then h.CFrame = GagMapSpawnCFrame end
            end
        end)
    else
        if GagMapRespawnConn then GagMapRespawnConn:Disconnect() GagMapRespawnConn = nil end
        GagMapSpawnCFrame = nil
        local old = workspace:FindFirstChild(GAG_MAP_FOLDER)
        if old then old:Destroy() end
    end
    SaveSettings()
end
end -- fin del bloque de locales
function setAntiAfkEnabled(state)
    Config.EnableAntiAfk = state
    if state then
        if AntiAfkConn then return end
        AntiAfkConn = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if math.floor(os.clock()) % 60 == 0 then
                pcall(function()
                    game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendMouseWheelEvent(0, 0, true, game)
                end)
            end
        end)
    else
        if AntiAfkConn then AntiAfkConn:Disconnect() AntiAfkConn = nil end
    end
    SaveSettings()
end
local playerEspRigService = nil
local playerEspWhistleTemplate = nil
local playerEspEntries = {}
local playerEspConnections = {}
local playerEspAccumulator = 0
local PLAYER_ESP_WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local PLAYER_ESP_RED = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
local PLAYER_ESP_REFRESH = 0.10
pcall(function()
    local services = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Services", 5)
    local asset = services and services:WaitForChild("Asset", 5)
    local rigModule = asset and asset:WaitForChild("RigService", 5)
    if rigModule then
        playerEspRigService = require(rigModule)
    end
end)
pcall(function()
    local objects = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Objects", 5)
    local gameFolder = objects and objects:WaitForChild("Game", 5)
    local characterFolder = gameFolder and gameFolder:WaitForChild("Character", 5)
    local actionVisuals = characterFolder and characterFolder:WaitForChild("ActionVisuals", 5)
    local actions = actionVisuals and actionVisuals:WaitForChild("Actions", 5)
    local whistle = actions and actions:WaitForChild("Whistle", 5)
    playerEspWhistleTemplate = whistle and whistle:FindFirstChild("WhistleHighlight")
end)
function playerEspDisconnect(connection)
    if connection then
        pcall(function()
            connection:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
end
function playerEspSetVisible(entry, visible)
    if entry.highlight then
        entry.highlight.Enabled = visible and Config.EnableWhistleESP
    end
    if entry.billboard then
        entry.billboard.Enabled = visible and Config.EnableWhistleESP
    end
end
function playerEspApplyColor(entry)
    if not entry or not entry.highlight then
        return
    end
    local color = entry.downed and PLAYER_ESP_RED or PLAYER_ESP_WHITE
    pcall(function()
        entry.highlight.FillColor = color
        entry.highlight.OutlineColor = color
    end)
end
function playerEspClearVisual(entry)
    if not entry then
        return
    end
    playerEspDisconnect(entry.died)
    entry.died = nil
    if entry.highlight then
        pcall(function()
            entry.highlight:Destroy()
        end)
        entry.highlight = nil
    end
    if entry.billboard then
        pcall(function()
            entry.billboard:Destroy()
        end)
        entry.billboard = nil
    end
    entry.label = nil
    entry.root = nil
    entry.visualRoot = nil
    entry.visualModel = nil
    entry.rigData = nil
    entry.downed = false
    entry.humanoid = nil
    entry.character = nil
end
function playerEspDestroyEntry(player)
    local entry = playerEspEntries[player]
    if not entry then
        return
    end
    playerEspDisconnect(entry.characterAdded)
    playerEspDisconnect(entry.characterRemoving)
    playerEspClearVisual(entry)
    playerEspEntries[player] = nil
end
function playerEspGetRigData(character)
    if not playerEspRigService or not character then
        return nil
    end
    local tag = character:GetAttribute("Tag")
    if not tag then
        return nil
    end
    local ok, rig = pcall(function()
        return playerEspRigService:GetRigFromTag(tag)
    end)
    if ok and rig and rig.Model and rig.Model:IsA("Model") then
        return rig
    end
    return nil
end
function playerEspResolveVisual(player, character)
    local rigData = playerEspGetRigData(character)
    local visualModel = rigData and rigData.Model or nil
    if visualModel then
        local visualRoot = visualModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true)
            or visualModel.PrimaryPart
            or visualModel:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        if visualRoot then
            return visualModel, visualRoot, rigData
        end
    end
    local playersFolder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Players")
    visualModel = playersFolder and playersFolder:FindFirstChild(player.Name)
    if visualModel and visualModel:IsA("Model") then
        local visualRoot = visualModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true)
            or visualModel.PrimaryPart
            or visualModel:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        if visualRoot then
            return visualModel, visualRoot, nil
        end
    end
    local characterRoot = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    return character, characterRoot, nil
end
function playerEspIsDowned(rigData, character)
    if rigData and rigData.DataRegistry then
        local okDowned, downed = pcall(function()
            return rigData.DataRegistry:Get("Downed")
        end)
        if okDowned and downed == true then
            return true
        end
        local okState, state = pcall(function()
            return rigData.DataRegistry:Get("State")
        end)
        if okState and state == "Downed" then
            return true
        end
    end
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return humanoid ~= nil and humanoid.Health <= 0
end
function playerEspCreateEntry(player, character)
    if player == LocalPlayer then
        return
    end
    local entry = playerEspEntries[player]
    if not entry then
        entry = {}
        playerEspEntries[player] = entry
    else
        playerEspClearVisual(entry)
    end
    local characterRoot = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not character or not characterRoot or not humanoid then
        return
    end
    local visualModel, visualRoot, rigData = playerEspResolveVisual(player, character)
    if not visualModel or not visualRoot then
        return
    end
    local downed = playerEspIsDowned(rigData, character)
    local oldHighlight = character:FindFirstChild("Beta_PlayerESP_WhistleHighlight")
    if oldHighlight then
        oldHighlight:Destroy()
    end
    local oldBillboard = visualRoot:FindFirstChild("Beta_PlayerESP_Label")
    if oldBillboard then
        oldBillboard:Destroy()
    end
    local highlight
    if playerEspWhistleTemplate then
        highlight = playerEspWhistleTemplate:Clone()
        highlight.Name = "Beta_PlayerESP_WhistleHighlight"
        highlight.Adornee = visualModel
        highlight.Enabled = Config.EnableWhistleESP
        highlight.Parent = character
    else
        highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
        highlight.Name = "Beta_PlayerESP_WhistleHighlight_Fallback"
        highlight.Adornee = visualModel
        highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        highlight.FillTransparency = 1
        highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
        highlight.Enabled = Config.EnableWhistleESP
        highlight.Parent = character
    end
    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "Beta_PlayerESP_Label"
    billboard.Adornee = visualRoot
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.LightInfluence = 0
    pcall(function()
        billboard.MaxDistance = math.huge
    end)
    billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 360, 0, 42)
    billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.8, 0)
    billboard.Enabled = Config.EnableWhistleESP
    billboard.Parent = visualRoot
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = "Info"
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.TextColor3 = PLAYER_ESP_WHITE
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    label.TextSize = 18
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    label.Text = player.DisplayName
    label.Parent = billboard
    entry.character = character
    entry.root = characterRoot
    entry.visualModel = visualModel
    entry.visualRoot = visualRoot
    entry.rigData = rigData
    entry.downed = downed
    entry.humanoid = humanoid
    entry.highlight = highlight
    entry.billboard = billboard
    entry.label = label
    playerEspApplyColor(entry)
    entry.died = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
        if playerEspEntries[player] == entry then
            entry.downed = true
            playerEspApplyColor(entry)
        end
    end)
end
function playerEspAttachPlayer(player)
    if player == LocalPlayer then
        return
    end
    playerEspDestroyEntry(player)
    local entry = {
        characterAdded = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
            task.defer(function()
                if Config.EnableWhistleESP then
                    local root = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
                    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if root and humanoid then
                        playerEspCreateEntry(player, character)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end),
        characterRemoving = player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
            local current = playerEspEntries[player]
            if current then
                playerEspClearVisual(current)
            end
        end),
    }
    playerEspEntries[player] = entry
    if player.Character then
        playerEspCreateEntry(player, player.Character)
    end
end
function playerEspAttachAll()
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer then
            playerEspAttachPlayer(player)
        end
    end
end
function playerEspRefresh()
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and not playerEspEntries[player] then
            playerEspAttachPlayer(player)
        end
    end
    local localCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
    local localRoot = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local observerPosition = localRoot and localRoot.Position or (camera and camera.CFrame.Position)
    if not observerPosition then
        return
    end
    for player, entry in pairs(playerEspEntries) do
        if not player.Parent then
            playerEspDestroyEntry(player)
        elseif entry.character and entry.character.Parent and entry.root and entry.root.Parent then
            local visualModel, visualRoot, rigData = playerEspResolveVisual(player, entry.character)
            if visualModel ~= entry.visualModel or visualRoot ~= entry.visualRoot then
                playerEspCreateEntry(player, entry.character)
                entry = playerEspEntries[player]
            elseif entry then
                entry.rigData = rigData or entry.rigData
            end
            if entry and entry.visualRoot and entry.visualRoot.Parent then
                entry.downed = playerEspIsDowned(entry.rigData, entry.character)
                playerEspApplyColor(entry)
                local distance = (entry.visualRoot.Position - observerPosition).Magnitude
                playerEspSetVisible(entry, Config.EnableWhistleESP and distance <= math.huge)
                if entry.label then
                    entry.label.Text = string.format("%s  |  %d m", player.DisplayName, math.floor(distance + 0.5))
                end
            end
        else
            playerEspSetVisible(entry, false)
        end
    end
end
function playerEspSetEnabled(state)
    Config.EnableWhistleESP = state and true or false
    if Config.EnableWhistleESP then
        playerEspAttachAll()
    else
        for player in pairs(playerEspEntries) do
            playerEspDestroyEntry(player)
        end
    end
    SaveSettings()
end
table.insert(playerEspConnections, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
    if Config.EnableWhistleESP then
        playerEspAttachPlayer(player)
    end
end))
table.insert(playerEspConnections, Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
    playerEspDestroyEntry(player)
end))
table.insert(playerEspConnections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
    playerEspAccumulator = playerEspAccumulator + deltaTime
    if playerEspAccumulator >= PLAYER_ESP_REFRESH then
        playerEspAccumulator = 0
        if Config.EnableWhistleESP then
            playerEspRefresh()
        end
    end
end))
print("\nConfiguring Map tab...")

end)

    SaveSettings()

    print(state and "✓ Void Escape ENABLED" or "X Void Escape DISABLED")

    else
        setGagMapEnabled(false)
        print("X Grow a Garden DISABLED")
    end
end)
TabMap:CreateDivider()

    print(state and "✓ Anti AFK ENABLED" or "X Anti AFK DISABLED")
end)
TabMap:CreateDivider()
TabMap:CreateLabel("ESP DE JUGADORES", "PLAYER ESP", 12)
TabMap:CreateDivider()
TabMap:CreateFloatingToggle("ESP de Jugadores", "Player ESP", Config.EnableWhistleESP, function(state)
    playerEspSetEnabled(state)
    print(state and "✓ Player ESP ENABLED" or "X Player ESP DISABLED")

print("✓ MAP: Tab completed")

    task.defer(function()
        playerEspSetEnabled(true)
    end)
