local removeWallsBackup     = {}
local removeWallsWatcher    = nil
local removeWallsParent     = nil
local function getInvisPartsContainer()
    local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    local container = map and map:FindFirstChild("InvisParts")
    if not container then
        container = workspace:FindFirstChild("InvisParts", true)
    end
    return container
end
local function removeWallsDestroyParts(container)
    local parts = container:GetDescendants()
    for _, obj in ipairs(parts) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            pcall(function()
                local clone = obj:Clone()
                clone:SetAttribute("__RW_ParentPath", obj.Parent:GetFullName())
                table.insert(removeWallsBackup, clone)
            end)
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
end
local function removeWallsWatchNew(container)
    if removeWallsWatcher then
        pcall(function() removeWallsWatcher:Disconnect() end)
        removeWallsWatcher = nil
    end
    removeWallsWatcher = container.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if not Config.EnableRemoveWalls then return end
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            task.defer(function()
                if not Config.EnableRemoveWalls then return end
                pcall(function()
                    local clone = obj:Clone()
                    clone:SetAttribute("__RW_ParentPath", obj.Parent:GetFullName())
                    table.insert(removeWallsBackup, clone)
                end)
                pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
            end)
        end
    end)
end
local function setRemoveWallsEnabled(state)
    Config.EnableRemoveWalls = state
    if state then
        removeWallsBackup = {}
        local container = getInvisPartsContainer()
        if not container then
            warn("[RemoveWalls] No se encontró InvisParts en el workspace.")
            Config.EnableRemoveWalls = false
            return
        end
        removeWallsParent = container
        print("[RemoveWalls] Encontrado en: " .. container:GetFullName())
        removeWallsDestroyParts(container)
        removeWallsWatchNew(container)
        print("[RemoveWalls] Activo — " .. #removeWallsBackup .. " partes eliminadas y guardadas")
    else
        if removeWallsWatcher then
            pcall(function() removeWallsWatcher:Disconnect() end)
            removeWallsWatcher = nil
        end
        local restored = 0
        if removeWallsParent and removeWallsParent.Parent then
            for _, clone in ipairs(removeWallsBackup) do
                pcall(function()
                    local newPart = clone:Clone()
                    newPart.Parent = removeWallsParent
                    restored = restored + 1
                end)
            end
        end
        removeWallsBackup = {}
        removeWallsParent = nil
        print("[RemoveWalls] Desactivado — " .. restored .. " partes restauradas")
    end
end
print("OK REMOVE WALLS: Logic loaded")
print("\nConfiguring Extra tab...")
do
    local DaraItemLimitToggle = nil
    DaraItemLimitToggle = TabExtra:CreateFloatingToggle(
        "Dara Item limit",
        "Dara Item limit",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            if not enabled then return end
            local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
            for i = 1, 4 do
                local a = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Shared")
                if a then a = a:FindFirstChild("HUD") end
                if a then a = a:FindFirstChild("Interactors") end
                if a then a = a:FindFirstChild("Popups") end
                if a then a = a:FindFirstChild("Usables") end
                if a then a = a:FindFirstChild("Wheel") end
                if a then
                    local b = a:FindFirstChild("Item" .. i)
                    if b then b = b:FindFirstChild("Icon") end
                    if b then b = b:FindFirstChild("TooMany") end
                    if b then b:Destroy() end
                end
            end
            for i = 1, 4 do
                local a = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Misc")
                if a then a = a:FindFirstChild("UI") end
                if a then a = a:FindFirstChild("Shared") end
                if a then a = a:FindFirstChild("HUD") end
                if a then a = a:FindFirstChild("Interactors") end
                if a then a = a:FindFirstChild("Popups") end
                if a then a = a:FindFirstChild("Usables") end
                if a then a = a:FindFirstChild("Wheel") end
                if a then
                    local b = a:FindFirstChild("Item" .. i)
                    if b then b = b:FindFirstChild("Icon") end
                    if b then b = b:FindFirstChild("TooMany") end
                    if b then b:Destroy() end
                end
            end
        end,
        {
            imageId = "rbxassetid://77466337396355",
            name = "Darks",
            description = "Thanks to one of the creators of dara hub for contributing with us!"
        }
    )
    local DaraToolDilayToggle = nil
    DaraToolDilayToggle = TabExtra:CreateFloatingToggle(
        "Dara Tool dilay",
        "Dara Tool dilay",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            if not enabled then return end
            local toolsFolder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Tools")
            if not toolsFolder then
                if DaraToolDilayToggle then DaraToolDilayToggle.SetValue(false) end
                warn("[Dara Tool dilay] No disponible: no se encontro ReplicatedStorage.Tools en este juego")
                return
            end
            local function setFastShoot(data)
                if type(data) ~= "table" then return end
                for key, value in pairs(data) do
                    if key == "Cooldown" and type(value) == "number" then
                        data[key] = 1/65536
                    elseif type(value) == "table" then
                        setFastShoot(value)
                    end
                end
            end
            for _, tool in pairs(toolsFolder:GetChildren()) do
                if tool:IsA("ModuleScript") then
                    local success, toolData = pcall(require, tool)
                    if success and type(toolData) == "table" and toolData.Tasks then
                        for _, task in ipairs(toolData.Tasks) do
                            for _, func in ipairs(task.Functions or {}) do
                                for _, activation in ipairs(func.Activations or {}) do
                                    for _, method in ipairs(activation.Methods or {}) do
                                        if method.Info then setFastShoot(method.Info) end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            for _, autoFunc in ipairs(task.AutomaticFunctions or {}) do
                                for _, method in ipairs(autoFunc.Methods or {}) do
                                    if method.Info then setFastShoot(method.Info) end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end,
        {
            imageId = "rbxassetid://77466337396355",
            name = "Darks",
            description = "Thanks to one of the creators of dara hub for contributing with us!"
        }
    )
    local DaraCameraConnection = nil
    local DaraCameraStretchHorizontal = 0.80
    local DaraCameraStretchVertical = 0.80
    TabExtra:CreateFloatingToggle(
        "Dara Camera",
        "Dara Camera",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            if DaraCameraConnection then
                DaraCameraConnection:Disconnect()
                DaraCameraConnection = nil
            end
            if not enabled then return end
            DaraCameraConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if not Camera then return end
                Camera.CFrame = Camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, DaraCameraStretchHorizontal, 0, 0, 0, DaraCameraStretchVertical, 0, 0, 0, 1)
            end)
        end,
        {
            imageId = "rbxassetid://77466337396355",
            name = "Darks",
            description = "Thanks to one of the creators of dara hub for contributing with us!"
        }
    )
    TabExtra:CreateSlider(
        "Stretch Horizontal",
        0.1, 2, DaraCameraStretchHorizontal,
        function(value)
            DaraCameraStretchHorizontal = tonumber(value) or 0.80
        end
    )
    TabExtra:CreateSlider(
        "Stretch Vertical",
        0.1, 2, DaraCameraStretchVertical,
        function(value)
            DaraCameraStretchVertical = tonumber(value) or 0.80
        end
    )
    local DaraColaInfoOk, DaraColaInfoRaw = pcall(function()
        local colaModule = require(ReplicatedStorage.Tools.Cola)
        return colaModule.Tasks[1].AutomaticFunctions[2].Methods[1].Info
    end)
    local DaraColaInfo = DaraColaInfoOk and type(DaraColaInfoRaw) == "table" and DaraColaInfoRaw or nil
    local DaraColaReady = DaraColaInfo ~= nil
        and getrawmetatable ~= nil and setreadonly ~= nil and newcclosure ~= nil
        and getnamecallmethod ~= nil and firesignal ~= nil
    local DaraColaDuration = tonumber(DaraColaInfo and DaraColaInfo.Duration) or 10
    local DaraColaSpeed = tonumber(DaraColaInfo and DaraColaInfo.SpeedBoost) or 15
    local DaraColaHooked = false
    local DaraColaMeta = nil
    local DaraColaOldNamecall = nil
    local DaraColaEndlessEnabled = false   -- dara: endlessColaEnabled
    local DaraColaBoosterEnabled = false   -- dara: modifyColaEnabled
    local function DaraColaVignette()
        local duration = DaraColaBoosterEnabled and DaraColaDuration or DaraColaInfo.Duration
        firesignal(
            ReplicatedStorage.Events.SharedUISetType.OnClientEvent,
            "Vignette",
            DaraColaInfo.Color,
            duration,
            true
        )
    end
    local function DaraColaSpeedBoost(Type)
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if character then
            local tag = character:GetAttribute("Tag")
            if tag then
                local speed = DaraColaBoosterEnabled and DaraColaSpeed or DaraColaInfo.SpeedBoost
                local duration = DaraColaBoosterEnabled and DaraColaDuration or DaraColaInfo.Duration
                firesignal(
                    ReplicatedStorage.Events.CharacterTask.OnClientEvent,
                    tag,
                    "Movement",
                    {
                        SpeedChange = {
                            Value = speed,
                            Type = Type or "Cola",
                            Length = duration
                        }
                    }
                )
            end
        end
    end
    local function DaraColaApplyHook()
        if DaraColaHooked then return end
        local toolAction = ReplicatedStorage.Events.ToolAction
        DaraColaMeta = getrawmetatable(game)
        DaraColaOldNamecall = DaraColaMeta.__namecall
        local oldNamecall = DaraColaOldNamecall
        setreadonly(DaraColaMeta, false)
        DaraColaMeta.__namecall = newcclosure(function(self, ...)
            local method = getnamecallmethod()
            local args = {...}
            if self == toolAction and (method == "FireServer" or method == "fireServer") then
                for i, v in ipairs(args) do
                    if typeof(v) == "buffer" then
                        local content = buffer.tostring(v)
                        if content == "\1\1\1" and DaraColaEndlessEnabled then
                            return
                        end
                        if content == "\1\2\1" then
                            if DaraColaEndlessEnabled then
                                DaraColaSpeedBoost()
                                task.wait()
                                DaraColaVignette()
                                return
                            elseif DaraColaBoosterEnabled then
                                task.spawn(function()
                                    DaraColaSpeedBoost("FakeCola")
                                    DaraColaVignette()
                                end)
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return oldNamecall(self, ...)
        end)
        setreadonly(DaraColaMeta, true)
        DaraColaHooked = true
    end
    local function DaraColaRemoveHook()
        if not DaraColaHooked then return end
        setreadonly(DaraColaMeta, false)
        DaraColaMeta.__namecall = DaraColaOldNamecall
        setreadonly(DaraColaMeta, true)
        DaraColaHooked = false
    end
    local function DaraColaCheckHookStatus()
        if DaraColaEndlessEnabled or DaraColaBoosterEnabled then
            return pcall(DaraColaApplyHook)
        end
        pcall(DaraColaRemoveHook)
        return true
    end
    local DaraColaToggle = nil
    DaraColaToggle = TabExtra:CreateFloatingToggle(
        "Dara Cola",
        "Dara Cola",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            if not DaraColaReady then
                if enabled and DaraColaToggle then DaraColaToggle.SetValue(false) end
                warn("[Dara Cola] No disponible: falta ReplicatedStorage.Tools.Cola o alguna funcion del executor (getrawmetatable, setreadonly, newcclosure, getnamecallmethod, firesignal)")
                return
            end
            DaraColaEndlessEnabled = enabled
            local ok, err = DaraColaCheckHookStatus()
            if not ok then
                DaraColaEndlessEnabled = false
                if DaraColaToggle then DaraColaToggle.SetValue(false) end
                warn("[Dara Cola] No se pudo activar: " .. tostring(err))
            end
        end,
        {
            imageId = "rbxassetid://77466337396355",
            name = "Darks",
            description = "Thanks to one of the creators of dara hub for contributing with us!"
        }
    )
    local DaraColaBoosterToggle = nil
    DaraColaBoosterToggle = TabExtra:CreateFloatingToggle(
        "Cola Booster",
        "Cola Booster",
        false,
        function(enabled)
            if not DaraColaReady then
                if enabled and DaraColaBoosterToggle then DaraColaBoosterToggle.SetValue(false) end
                warn("[Cola Booster] No disponible: falta ReplicatedStorage.Tools.Cola o alguna funcion del executor (getrawmetatable, setreadonly, newcclosure, getnamecallmethod, firesignal)")
                return
            end
            DaraColaBoosterEnabled = enabled
            local ok, err = DaraColaCheckHookStatus()
            if not ok then
                DaraColaBoosterEnabled = false
                if DaraColaBoosterToggle then DaraColaBoosterToggle.SetValue(false) end
                warn("[Cola Booster] No se pudo activar: " .. tostring(err))
            end
        end
    )
    TabExtra:CreateSlider(
        "Cola Duration",
        0.5, 60, math.clamp(DaraColaDuration, 0.5, 60),
        function(value)
            DaraColaDuration = tonumber(value) or DaraColaDuration
        end
    )
    TabExtra:CreateSlider(
        "Cola Speed",
        0.1, 10, math.clamp(DaraColaSpeed, 0.1, 10),
        function(value)
            DaraColaSpeed = tonumber(value) or DaraColaSpeed
        end
    )
    TabExtra:CreateDivider()
end
TabExtra:CreateLabel("MODIFY STATISTICS", "MODIFY STATISTICS", 14)
TabExtra:CreateDivider()
do
local function buildModifyStatistics()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    local FIELDS = {
        { key = "Level",     label = scriptText("Nivel", "Level") },
        { key = "XP",        label = scriptText("XP actual", "Current XP") },
        { key = "Tokens",    label = scriptText("Dinero (Tokens)", "Money (Tokens)") },
        { key = "Points",    label = scriptText("Puntos", "Points") },
        { key = "Survivals", label = scriptText("Victorias", "Wins") },
        { key = "Streak",    label = scriptText("Racha", "Streak") },
    }
    local overrides = {}
    local active = false
    local alive = true
    local cur = nil
    local conns = {}
    local realCache = { Level = 1, XP = 0, Tokens = 0, Points = 0, Survivals = 0, Streak = 0 }
    local survNote = ""
    local function comma(n)
        local s = tostring(math.floor(n))
        local r = s:reverse():gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1,")
        r = r:reverse():gsub("^,", "")
        return r
    end
    local function reqXP(lvl)
        return math.floor(lvl ^ 1.5 * 1.5 + 10)
    end
    local function findPath(root, names)
        local o = root
        for _, n in ipairs(names) do
            o = o and o:FindFirstChild(n)
        end
        return o
    end
    local function getHook(name)
        local ok, m = pcall(function()
            local x = RS
            for _, n in ipairs({ "Shared", "UserData", "ClientHooks", name }) do
                x = x:FindFirstChild(n)
                if not x then error("falta " .. n) end
            end
            return require(x)
        end)
        if ok then return m end
        return nil
    end
    local function realValues()
        local r = { Level = 1, XP = 0, Tokens = 0, Points = 0, Survivals = 0, Streak = 0 }
        local p = getHook("useProgression")
        if p then
            local ok, t = pcall(p.Get)
            if ok and type(t) == "table" then
                r.Level = t.Level or 1
                r.XP = t.XP or 0
            end
        end
        local c = getHook("useCurrency")
        if c then
            local ok, t = pcall(c.All)
            if ok and type(t) == "table" then
                r.Tokens = t.Tokens or 0
                r.Points = t.Points or 0
            end
        end
        local n = getHook("useCounters")
        if n then
            local ok, t = pcall(n.All)
            if ok and type(t) == "table" then
                r.Survivals = t.Survivals or 0
                r.Streak = t.Streak or 0
            end
        end
        return r
    end
    local function resolve()
        local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        local menu = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("Menu")
        local bottom = findPath(menu, { "Views", "Default", "Bottom" })
        if not bottom then return nil end
        local t = {
            bottom = bottom,
            levelText = findPath(bottom, { "Level", "Level" }),
            xpText = findPath(bottom, { "Level", "Experience" }),
            bar = findPath(bottom, { "Level", "Bar" }),
            tokens = findPath(bottom, { "Left", "Tokens", "Body", "Amount" }),
            points = findPath(bottom, { "Left", "Points", "Body", "Amount" }),
            streak = findPath(bottom, { "Left", "Streak", "Body", "Amount" }),
            streakFrame = findPath(bottom, { "Left", "Streak" }),
            surv = findPath(bottom, { "Left", "Survivals", "Body", "Amount" }),
            survFrame = findPath(bottom, { "Left", "Survivals" }),
        }
        survNote = ""
        if not t.surv and t.survFrame then
            for _, d in ipairs(t.survFrame:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("TextLabel") and d.Name == "Amount" then
                    t.surv = d
                    survNote = " (ruta alterna: " .. d.Name .. ")"
                    break
                end
            end
            if not t.surv then
                for _, d in ipairs(t.survFrame:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("TextLabel") and d.Name ~= "Cash" and d.Text ~= "+" and d.Text ~= "" then
                        t.surv = d
                        survNote = " (ruta alterna: " .. d.Name .. ")"
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return t
    end
    local function setText(o, s)
        if o and o.Parent and o.Text ~= s then
            o.Text = s
        end
    end
    local function setBar(bar, ratio)
        if bar and bar.Parent then
            local s = bar.Size
            if math.abs(s.X.Scale - ratio) > 1e-4 then
                bar.Size = UDim2.new(ratio, s.X.Offset, s.Y.Scale, s.Y.Offset)
            end
        end
    end
    local function enforceAll()
        if not cur or not active then return end
        local ov = overrides
        if ov.Level or ov.XP then
            local lvl = ov.Level or realCache.Level
            local xp = ov.XP or realCache.XP
            local req = reqXP(lvl)
            if ov.Level then setText(cur.levelText, "LEVEL " .. lvl) end
            setText(cur.xpText, xp .. "/" .. req)
            setBar(cur.bar, math.clamp(xp / req, 0, 1))
        end
        if ov.Tokens then setText(cur.tokens, comma(ov.Tokens)) end
        if ov.Points then setText(cur.points, comma(ov.Points)) end
        if ov.Survivals then setText(cur.surv, comma(ov.Survivals)) end
        if ov.Streak then
            setText(cur.streak, comma(ov.Streak))
            local f = cur.streakFrame
            if f and f.Parent then
                local want = ov.Streak > 0
                if f.Visible ~= want then f.Visible = want end
            end
        end
    end
    local function writeReal()
        if not cur then return end
        local r = realCache
        local req = reqXP(r.Level)
        setText(cur.levelText, "LEVEL " .. r.Level)
        setText(cur.xpText, r.XP .. "/" .. req)
        setBar(cur.bar, math.clamp(r.XP / req, 0, 1))
        setText(cur.tokens, comma(r.Tokens))
        setText(cur.points, comma(r.Points))
        setText(cur.surv, comma(r.Survivals))
        setText(cur.streak, comma(r.Streak))
        local f = cur.streakFrame
        if f and f.Parent then
            local want = r.Streak > 0
            if f.Visible ~= want then f.Visible = want end
        end
    end
    local function disconnectAll()
        for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
            pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
        end
        conns = {}
    end
    local function watch(o, prop)
        if o then
            conns[#conns + 1] = o:GetPropertyChangedSignal(prop):Connect(enforceAll)
        end
    end
    local function rebind(r)
        disconnectAll()
        cur = r
        if not r then return end
        watch(r.levelText, "Text")
        watch(r.xpText, "Text")
        watch(r.bar, "Size")
        watch(r.tokens, "Text")
        watch(r.points, "Text")
        watch(r.surv, "Text")
        watch(r.streak, "Text")
        watch(r.streakFrame, "Visible")
    end
    local function getParent()
        local ok, h = pcall(function() return gethui and gethui() end)
        if ok and h then return h end
        local ok2, cg = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        if ok2 and cg then return cg end
        return lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end
    local function softStroke(o, tr)
        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        s.Color = Color3.fromRGB(120, 125, 145)
        s.Thickness = 1
        s.Transparency = tr or 0.42
        s.Parent = o
        return s
    end
    local parent = getParent()
    local old = parent:FindFirstChild("YinYangModifyStatistics")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "YinYangModifyStatistics"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.Parent = parent
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(250, 322)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 60)
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 10)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Active = true
    frame.Parent = gui
    Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    softStroke(frame, 0.35)
    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 28)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.BorderSizePixel = 0
    title.Text = "  Modify Statistics"
    title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240)
    title.TextSize = 12
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    title.Parent = frame
    local subtitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    subtitle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 92, 0, 28)
    subtitle.Position = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 0)
    subtitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    subtitle.BorderSizePixel = 0
    subtitle.Text = scriptText("solo visual", "visual only")
    subtitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 155, 170)
    subtitle.TextSize = 10
    subtitle.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    subtitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    subtitle.Parent = frame
    local titleLine = Instance.new("Frame")
    titleLine.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 1)
    titleLine.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 28)
    titleLine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 125, 145)
    titleLine.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
    titleLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
    titleLine.Parent = frame
    local boxes = {}
    for idx, f in ipairs(FIELDS) do
        local y = 34 + (idx - 1) * 30
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(98, 26)
        lbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, y)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = f.label
        lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
        lbl.TextSize = 11
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        lbl.Parent = frame
        local tb = Instance.new("TextBox")
        tb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(130, 26)
        tb.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(110, y)
        tb.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        tb.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
        tb.BorderSizePixel = 0
        tb.Text = ""
        tb.PlaceholderText = "real: ..."
        tb.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        tb.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240)
        tb.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 135)
        tb.TextSize = 12
        tb.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        tb.Parent = frame
        Instance.new("UICorner", tb).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        softStroke(tb, 0.6)
        boxes[f.key] = tb
    end
    local bApply = Instance.new("TextButton")
    bApply.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(112, 28)
    bApply.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 218)
    bApply.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 26)
    bApply.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    bApply.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bApply.Text = scriptText("Aplicar", "Apply")
    bApply.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 232, 212)
    bApply.TextSize = 12
    bApply.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    bApply.Parent = frame
    Instance.new("UICorner", bApply).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    softStroke(bApply, 0.5)
    local bReset = Instance.new("TextButton")
    bReset.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(112, 28)
    bReset.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(128, 218)
    bReset.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 26)
    bReset.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    bReset.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bReset.Text = scriptText("Quitar", "Remove")
    bReset.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 212, 208)
    bReset.TextSize = 12
    bReset.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    bReset.Parent = frame
    Instance.new("UICorner", bReset).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    softStroke(bReset, 0.5)
    local status = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    status.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 64)
    status.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 252)
    status.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    status.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
    status.BorderSizePixel = 0
    status.TextWrapped = true
    status.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    status.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(185, 192, 202)
    status.TextSize = 10
    status.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    status.Text = scriptText(
        "Listo. Escribe los valores que quieras (vacio = no tocar) y toca Aplicar. Funciona en el menu principal.",
        "Ready. Type the values you want (empty = leave untouched) and tap Apply. Works in the main menu.")
    status.Parent = frame
    Instance.new("UICorner", status).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    softStroke(status, 0.6)
    local function setStatus(s)
        status.Text = s
    end
    local function refreshPlaceholders()
        for _, f in ipairs(FIELDS) do
            boxes[f.key].PlaceholderText = "real: " .. tostring(realCache[f.key])
        end
    end
    local function bindReport(r)
        local function ok(o) return o and "OK" or "NO" end
        return scriptText(
            "Enlace: Nivel %s | XP %s | Barra %s\nTokens %s | Points %s | Racha %s\nVictorias %s%s",
            "Link: Level %s | XP %s | Bar %s\nTokens %s | Points %s | Streak %s\nWins %s%s"):format(
            ok(r.levelText), ok(r.xpText), ok(r.bar),
            ok(r.tokens), ok(r.points), ok(r.streak),
            ok(r.surv), survNote)
    end
    bApply.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local newOv = {}
        for _, f in ipairs(FIELDS) do
            local t = boxes[f.key].Text:gsub(",", ""):gsub("%s", "")
            if t ~= "" then
                local n = tonumber(t)
                if not n then
                    setStatus(scriptText("Valor invalido en: ", "Invalid value in: ") .. f.label)
                    return
                end
                n = math.max(0, math.floor(n))
                if f.key == "Level" then n = math.max(1, n) end
                newOv[f.key] = n
            end
        end
        local r = resolve()
        if not r then
            setStatus(scriptText(
                "No encuentro PlayerGui.Menu.Views.Default.Bottom. Abre el menu principal y repite.",
                "Can't find PlayerGui.Menu.Views.Default.Bottom. Open the main menu and try again."))
            return
        end
        realCache = realValues()
        refreshPlaceholders()
        overrides = newOv
        active = next(newOv) ~= nil
        rebind(r)
        writeReal()
        enforceAll()
        setStatus((active
            and scriptText("Aplicado (solo visual).\n", "Applied (visual only).\n")
            or scriptText("Sin valores: se muestran los reales.\n", "No values: real ones are shown.\n"))
            .. bindReport(r))
    end)
    bReset.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        overrides = {}
        active = false
        realCache = realValues()
        refreshPlaceholders()
        local r = resolve()
        if r then
            if not cur or cur.bottom ~= r.bottom then cur = r end
            writeReal()
        end
        disconnectAll()
        for _, f in ipairs(FIELDS) do boxes[f.key].Text = "" end
        setStatus(scriptText("Quitado: se muestran los valores reales.", "Removed: real values are shown."))
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while alive do
            task.wait(0.5)
            pcall(function()
                realCache = realValues()
                if active then
                    local r = resolve()
                    if r and (not cur or r.bottom ~= cur.bottom or not cur.bottom.Parent) then
                        rebind(r)
                    end
                    enforceAll()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        realCache = realValues()
        refreshPlaceholders()
    end)
    local dragging, dragStart, startPos
    title.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = i.Position
            startPos = frame.Position
            i.Changed:Connect(function()
                if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
            end)
        end
    end)
    local dragConn = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local d = i.Position - dragStart
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end
    end)
    return function()
        alive = false
        overrides = {}
        active = false
        pcall(function()
            realCache = realValues()
            writeReal()
        end)
        disconnectAll()
        pcall(function() dragConn:Disconnect() end)
        gui:Destroy()
    end
end
local closeModifyStatistics = nil
TabExtra:CreateFloatingToggle("Modify Statistics", "Modify Statistics", false, function(state)
    if state then
        if closeModifyStatistics then
            pcall(closeModifyStatistics)
            closeModifyStatistics = nil
        end
        local ok, res = pcall(buildModifyStatistics)
        if ok and type(res) == "function" then
            closeModifyStatistics = res
            print("[ModifyStatistics] ENABLED")
        else
            warn("[ModifyStatistics] No se pudo abrir: " .. tostring(res))
        end
    else
        if closeModifyStatistics then
            pcall(closeModifyStatistics)
            closeModifyStatistics = nil
        end
        print("[ModifyStatistics] DISABLED")
    end
end)
end

TabExtra:CreateLabel("INTERRUPTOR DE LAG", "LAG SWITCH", 14)
TabExtra:CreateDivider()
TabExtra:CreateFloatingButton("Interruptor de Lag", "Lag Switch", function()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
        pcall(function()
            hum.JumpPower = Config.JumpHeight * 1.5
            hum.UseJumpPower = true
        end)
    end
    local start = os.clock()
    while os.clock() - start < 1.0 do end
    task.delay(5, function()
        local c = LocalPlayer.Character
        local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if h then
            pcall(function()
                h.JumpPower = Config.JumpHeight
                h.UseJumpPower = true
            end)
        end
        print("[LagSwitch] Jump reset")
    end)
    print("[LagSwitch] Fired - 1.5x jump activo por 5s")
end)
TabExtra:CreateDivider()
do
    local startNoFog, stopNoFog
    local originalFogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd
    local originalAtmospheres = {}
    for _, v in pairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
    if v:IsA("Atmosphere") then
    table.insert(originalAtmospheres, v)
    end
    end
    function startNoFog()
    originalFogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd
    Lighting.FogEnd = 1000000
    for _, v in pairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
    if v:IsA("Atmosphere") then
    v:Destroy()
    end
    end
    end
    function stopNoFog()
    Lighting.FogEnd = originalFogEnd
    for _, atmosphere in pairs(originalAtmospheres) do
    if not atmosphere.Parent then
    local newAtmosphere = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
    for _, prop in pairs({"Density", "Offset", "Color", "Decay", "Glare", "Haze"}) do
    if atmosphere[prop] then
    newAtmosphere[prop] = atmosphere[prop]
    end
    end
    newAtmosphere.Parent = Lighting
    end
    end
    end
    TabExtra:CreateFloatingToggle("Sin Niebla", "No Fog", false, function(state)
        if state then
            startNoFog()
        else
            stopNoFog()
        end
    end)
end
do
    local createTimerGUI, destroyTimerGUI, ensureTimerGUI, GetRoundTitle, SetTime, SetSpecialRound, SetStatus, SetTimerVisible, SetSpecialRoundVisible, CheckGameTimerVisibility, StartGameTimerCheck, StopGameTimerCheck, UpdateFromAttributes, StartAttributeMonitor, StopAttributeMonitor, handleTimerToggles
    local ServerStateRegistryService = nil
    local function resolveServerState()
        if ServerStateRegistryService then return ServerStateRegistryService end
        local services = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Services")
        local data = services and services:FindFirstChild("Data")
        local module = data and data:FindFirstChild("ServerStateRegistryService")
        if not module then return nil end
        local ok, result = pcall(require, module)
        if ok then
            ServerStateRegistryService = result
        end
        return ServerStateRegistryService
    end

    if not StatusLabel then

    StatusLabel.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
    StatusLabel.Text = "ROUND ACTIVE"
    StatusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(165, 194, 255)
    StatusLabel.TextScaled = true
    StatusLabel.TextSize = 14
    StatusLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.95
    StatusLabel.TextWrapped = true
    StatusLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    StatusLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1

    function destroyTimerGUI()
    if TimerGUI then
    StopGameTimerCheck()
    StopAttributeMonitor()
    TimerGUI.ScreenGui:Destroy()
    TimerGUI = nil
    end
    TimerEnabled = false
    SpecialRoundEnabled = false
    end
    function ensureTimerGUI()
    if not TimerGUI then
    TimerGUI = createTimerGUI()
    end
    end
    function GetRoundTitle(roundName)
    if not roundName or roundName == "" then
    return ""
    end
    local specialRoundsFolder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Info")
    if not specialRoundsFolder then return roundName end
    specialRoundsFolder = specialRoundsFolder:FindFirstChild("SpecialRounds")
    if not specialRoundsFolder then return roundName end
    local roundModule = specialRoundsFolder:FindFirstChild(roundName)
    if not roundModule then return roundName end
    local success, moduleData = pcall(function()
    return require(roundModule)
    end)
    if success and moduleData and moduleData.Title then
    return moduleData.Title
    end
    return roundName
    end
    function SetTime(seconds)
    if not TimerGUI then return end
    if type(seconds) == "number" then
    local minutes = math.floor(seconds / 60)
    local remainingSeconds = math.floor(seconds % 60)
    TimerGUI.TimeDisplay.Text = string.format("%d:%02d", minutes, remainingSeconds)
    if seconds <= 5 then
    TimerGUI.TimeDisplay.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(215, 100, 100)
    TimerGUI.StatusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(215, 100, 100)
    if TimerGUI.MainTimerStroke then
    TimerGUI.MainTimerStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 138, 138)
    end
    if TimerGUI.MainTimerImage then
    TimerGUI.MainTimerImage.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 138, 138)
    end
    else
    TimerGUI.TimeDisplay.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(165, 194, 255)
    TimerGUI.StatusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(165, 194, 255)
    if TimerGUI.MainTimerStroke then
    TimerGUI.MainTimerStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(165, 194, 255)
    end
    if TimerGUI.MainTimerImage then
    TimerGUI.MainTimerImage.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(21, 21, 21)
    end
    end
    else
    TimerGUI.TimeDisplay.Text = tostring(seconds)
    end
    end
    function SetSpecialRound(roundName)
    if not TimerGUI then return end
    if roundName and roundName ~= "" then
    CurrentSpecialRoundName = roundName
    local roundTitle = GetRoundTitle(roundName)
    TimerGUI.SpecialRoundLabel.Text = roundTitle
    TimerGUI.SpecialRound.Visible = SpecialRoundEnabled
    else
    CurrentSpecialRoundName = ""
    TimerGUI.SpecialRound.Visible = false
    end
    end
    function SetStatus(text)
    if not TimerGUI then return end
    TimerGUI.StatusLabel.Text = text:upper()
    end
    function SetTimerVisible(visible)
    TimerEnabled = visible
    OriginalTimerVisible = visible
    if CheckingGameTimer then
    CheckGameTimerVisibility()
    else
    if TimerGUI then
    TimerGUI.MainTimer.Visible = visible
    end
    end
    end
    function SetSpecialRoundVisible(visible)
    SpecialRoundEnabled = visible
    if TimerGUI and visible and CurrentSpecialRoundName ~= "" then
    TimerGUI.SpecialRound.Visible = true
    elseif TimerGUI then
    TimerGUI.SpecialRound.Visible = false
    end
    end
    function CheckGameTimerVisibility()
    if not TimerEnabled or not TimerGUI then
    if TimerGUI then TimerGUI.MainTimer.Visible = false end
    return
    end
    local hud = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Shared")
    if not hud then
    TimerGUI.MainTimer.Visible = OriginalTimerVisible
    return
    end
    hud = hud:FindFirstChild("HUD")
    if not hud then
    TimerGUI.MainTimer.Visible = OriginalTimerVisible
    return
    end
    hud = hud:FindFirstChild("Overlay")
    if not hud then
    TimerGUI.MainTimer.Visible = OriginalTimerVisible
    return
    end
    hud = hud:FindFirstChild("Default")
    if not hud then
    TimerGUI.MainTimer.Visible = OriginalTimerVisible
    return
    end
    hud = hud:FindFirstChild("RoundOverlay")
    if not hud then
    TimerGUI.MainTimer.Visible = OriginalTimerVisible
    return
    end
    hud = hud:FindFirstChild("Round")
    if not hud then
    TimerGUI.MainTimer.Visible = OriginalTimerVisible
    return
    end
    local roundTimer = hud:FindFirstChild("RoundTimer")
    if not roundTimer then
    TimerGUI.MainTimer.Visible = OriginalTimerVisible
    return
    end
    if roundTimer.Visible then
    TimerGUI.MainTimer.Visible = false
    else
    TimerGUI.MainTimer.Visible = OriginalTimerVisible
    end
    end
    function StartGameTimerCheck()
    if CheckingGameTimer then return end
    CheckingGameTimer = true
    spawn(function()
    while CheckingGameTimer do
    CheckGameTimerVisibility()
    wait(0.1)
    end
    end)
    end
    function StopGameTimerCheck()
    CheckingGameTimer = false
    if TimerGUI then
    TimerGUI.MainTimer.Visible = OriginalTimerVisible
    end
    end
    function UpdateFromAttributes()
    if not resolveServerState() then return end
    local timerValue = ServerStateRegistryService.Registry.Time
    local specialRoundValue = ServerStateRegistryService.Registry.SpecialRound
    local roundStatus = ServerStateRegistryService.Registry.RoundStatus
    if timerValue and timerValue ~= -1 then
    SetTime(timerValue)
    end
    if roundStatus == 2 then
    SetStatus("Round Active")
    elseif roundStatus == 1 or 0 then
    SetStatus("Intermission")
    else
    SetStatus("nil")
    end
    if specialRoundValue and specialRoundValue ~= false then
    SetSpecialRound(tostring(specialRoundValue))
    else
    SetSpecialRound("")
    end
    end
    function StartAttributeMonitor()
    StopAttributeMonitor()
    if not resolveServerState() then
    warn("[Timer] ServerStateRegistryService no disponible")
    return
    end
    local lastTimer = ServerStateRegistryService.Registry.Time
    local lastRoundStatus = ServerStateRegistryService.Registry.RoundStatus
    local lastSpecialRound = ServerStateRegistryService.Registry.SpecialRound
    _attributeConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    local currentTimer = ServerStateRegistryService.Registry.Time
    local currentRoundStatus = ServerStateRegistryService.Registry.RoundStatus
    local currentSpecialRound = ServerStateRegistryService.Registry.SpecialRound
    if currentTimer ~= lastTimer or currentRoundStatus ~= lastRoundStatus or currentSpecialRound ~= lastSpecialRound then
    lastTimer = currentTimer
    lastRoundStatus = currentRoundStatus
    lastSpecialRound = currentSpecialRound
    UpdateFromAttributes()
    end
    end)
    UpdateFromAttributes()
    end
    function StopAttributeMonitor()
    if _attributeConnection then
    _attributeConnection:Disconnect()
    _attributeConnection = nil
    end
    if _attributeConnection2 then
    _attributeConnection2:Disconnect()
    _attributeConnection2 = nil
    end
    if _attributeConnection3 then
    _attributeConnection3:Disconnect()
    _attributeConnection3 = nil
    end
    end
    function handleTimerToggles()
    local timerOn = TimerEnabled
    local specialOn = SpecialRoundEnabled
    if not timerOn and not specialOn then
    destroyTimerGUI()
    else
    ensureTimerGUI()
    if timerOn then
    SetTimerVisible(true)
    StartAttributeMonitor()
    StartGameTimerCheck()
    end
    if specialOn then
    SetSpecialRoundVisible(true)
    end
    end
    end
    TabExtra:CreateFloatingToggle("Temporizador", "Timer", false, function(state)
        TimerEnabled = state
        handleTimerToggles()
    end)
    TabExtra:CreateFloatingToggle("Ronda Especial", "Special Round", false, function(state)
        SpecialRoundEnabled = state
        handleTimerToggles()
    end)
end
do
    local useCurrency, useProgression, useEvent
    local formatNumber, updateFrontFrameXSize, createResourceRow, updateCurrencyUI, updateProgressionUI, updateEventUI, DisableScanData, EnableScanData
    local PlayerStatsEnabled = false
    local PlayerStats = nil
    local textLabels = {}
    local frontFrames = {}
    local observeConnections = {}
    function formatNumber(num)
    local str = tostring(math.floor(num))
    local formatted = ""
    local length = #str
    for i = 1, length do
    formatted = formatted .. str:sub(i, i)
    if (length - i) % 3 == 0 and i ~= length then
    formatted = formatted .. ","
    end
    end
    return formatted
    end
    function updateFrontFrameXSize(frame, textLabel)
    if not frame or not textLabel then return end
    local textBounds = textLabel.TextBounds
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, textBounds.X + 20, 1, 0)
    end
    function createResourceRow(name, defaultValue, textColor, iconAssetId, iconColor, layoutOrder, leftColumn)
    local container = Instance.new("Frame")
    container.Name = name
    container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    container.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    container.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder
    container.Parent = leftColumn

    background.Name = "Background"
    background.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    background.Image = "rbxassetid://196969716"
    background.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(21, 21, 21)
    background.ImageTransparency = 0.4
    background.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    background.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    background.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1.25, 1.25)
    background.ZIndex = 1
    background.Parent = container
    local bgCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    bgCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    bgCorner.Parent = background

    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    icon.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. iconAssetId
    if iconColor then
    icon.ImageColor3 = iconColor

    icon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)

    icon.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.8, 0.8)

    leftEffect.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
    leftEffect.Size = UDim2.new(2, 0, 1, 0)

    clipFront.BackgroundTransparency = 1

    clipFront.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)

    clipFront.Parent = container
    local frontEffect = Instance.new("Frame")
    frontEffect.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    frontEffect.BackgroundTransparency = 0.9
    frontEffect.Position = UDim2.new(-1, 0, 0, 0)
    frontEffect.Size = UDim2.new(2, 0, 1, 0)
    frontEffect.ZIndex = 2
    frontEffect.Parent = clipFront
    local frontCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    frontCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    frontCorner.Parent = frontEffect
    frontFrames[name] = {
    frame = clipFront,
    effect = frontEffect,
    textLabel = textLabel
    }
    task.spawn(function()
    task.wait()
    updateFrontFrameXSize(clipFront, textLabel)
    end)
    return textLabel
    end
    function updateCurrencyUI()
    local allCurrencies = useCurrency.All()
    if textLabels["Tokens"] and allCurrencies["Tokens"] then
    textLabels["Tokens"].Text = formatNumber(allCurrencies["Tokens"])
    end
    if textLabels["Points"] and allCurrencies["Points"] then
    textLabels["Points"].Text = formatNumber(allCurrencies["Points"])
    end
    if textLabels["Survivals"] and allCurrencies["Survivals"] then
    textLabels["Survivals"].Text = formatNumber(allCurrencies["Survivals"])
    end
    if textLabels["Streak"] and allCurrencies["Streak"] then
    textLabels["Streak"].Text = formatNumber(allCurrencies["Streak"])
    end
    for _, data in pairs(frontFrames) do
    if data.frame and data.textLabel then
    updateFrontFrameXSize(data.frame, data.textLabel)
    end
    end
    end
    function updateProgressionUI()
    if not PlayerStats then return end
    local level = useProgression.Level()
    local xp = useProgression.XP()
    local requiredXP = useProgression.RequiredXP() or (level * 100)
    local progress = useProgression.Progress() or 0
    PlayerStats.levelText.Text = "LEVEL " .. tostring(level)
    PlayerStats.expText.Text = formatNumber(xp) .. "/" .. formatNumber(requiredXP)
    PlayerStats.fill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(progress, 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
    end
    function updateEventUI()
    if textLabels["Tickets"] then
    textLabels["Tickets"].Text = formatNumber(useEvent.Balance())
    local data = frontFrames["Tickets"]
    if data and data.frame and data.textLabel then
    updateFrontFrameXSize(data.frame, data.textLabel)
    end
    end
    end
    function DisableScanData()
    PlayerStatsEnabled = false
    for _, disconnect in pairs(observeConnections) do
    if type(disconnect) == "function" then disconnect() end
    end
    observeConnections = {}
    if PlayerStats then
    PlayerStats.ScreenGui:Destroy()
    PlayerStats = nil
    end
    textLabels = {}
    frontFrames = {}
    end
    function EnableScanData()
    local okHooks, hooksErr = pcall(function()
    local hooks = ReplicatedStorage.Shared.UserData.ClientHooks
    useCurrency = require(hooks.useCurrency)
    useProgression = require(hooks.useProgression)
    useEvent = require(hooks.useEvent)
    end)
    if not okHooks then
    warn("[PlayerStats] No se pudieron cargar los hooks: " .. tostring(hooksErr))
    return
    end
    if PlayerStats then
    DisableScanData()
    end

    for name, data in pairs(frontFrames) do
    if data.textLabel then

    TabExtra:CreateFloatingToggle("Mostrar Stats", "Show Stats", false, function(state)
        if state then

local EmoteState = {
    enabled  = true,
    emoteIdx = nil,
}
local activeVisualTrack = nil
local activeVisualTrackRestarting = false
local activeProps       = {}
local activeSound       = nil
local activeSoundKillerConn = nil
local substituting      = false
local emoteGeneration  = 0
local emoteConnections  = {}
local emoteLifecycleConnections = {}
local roundCleanupConnections = {}
local inEvadeEmote      = false
function disconnectEmoteConnections(list)
    for _, connection in ipairs(list) do
        pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
    end
    table.clear(list)
end
local emoteModuleCache = {}
function buildEmoteCache()
    local items = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Items")
    if not items then return end
    local roots = {}
    local packs = items:FindFirstChild("ItemPacks")
    if packs then table.insert(roots, packs) end
    local baseItems = items:FindFirstChild("BaseItems")
    if baseItems then table.insert(roots, baseItems) end
    for _, emote in ipairs(EMOTES) do
        local acceptedNames = {[emote.emoteName] = true}
        if emote.emoteName == "Broom" then
            acceptedNames.BroomOfDoom = true
            acceptedNames["Broom of Doom"] = true
        end
        for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
            local found = false
            for _, inst in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                if inst.ClassName == "ModuleScript" and acceptedNames[inst.Name] then
                    local hasEmoteModel = inst:FindFirstChild("CharacterClassic", true)
                        or inst:FindFirstChild("EmoteModel", true)
                    if emote.emoteName ~= "Broom" or hasEmoteModel then
                        emoteModuleCache[emote.emoteKey or emote.emoteName] = inst
                        found = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if found then break end
        end
    end
    if not emoteModuleCache.Broom then
        for _, inst in ipairs(ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants()) do
            if inst.ClassName == "ModuleScript"
            and (inst.Name == "Broom" or inst.Name == "BroomOfDoom" or inst.Name == "Broom of Doom") then
                local hasEmoteModel = inst:FindFirstChild("CharacterClassic", true)
                    or inst:FindFirstChild("EmoteModel", true)
                if hasEmoteModel then
                    emoteModuleCache.Broom = inst
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
function cleanupActiveEmote()
    emoteGeneration = emoteGeneration + 1
    if activeVisualTrack then
        local track = activeVisualTrack
        activeVisualTrack = nil
        activeVisualTrackRestarting = false
        pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end)
    end
    for _, obj in ipairs(activeProps) do
        pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
    end
    activeProps = {}
    if activeSound then
        pcall(function() activeSound:Stop(); activeSound:Destroy() end)
        activeSound = nil
    end
    if activeSoundKillerConn then
        pcall(function() activeSoundKillerConn:Disconnect() end)
        activeSoundKillerConn = nil
    end
end
function resetEmoteRuntime()
    cleanupActiveEmote()
    inEvadeEmote = false
    substituting = false
end
function bindEmoteCharacterLifecycle(char)
    disconnectEmoteConnections(emoteLifecycleConnections)
    if not char then return end
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        table.insert(emoteLifecycleConnections, humanoid.Died:Connect(resetEmoteRuntime))
    end
    table.insert(emoteLifecycleConnections, char.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
        if not parent then
            resetEmoteRuntime()
        end
    end))
end
function setupRoundCleanupWatchers()
    disconnectEmoteConnections(roundCleanupConnections)
    local updateMapBound = false
    local stateBound = false
    task.spawn(function()
        for _ = 1, 90 do
            local events = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Events")
            local updateMap = events and events:FindFirstChild("UpdateMap")
            if not updateMapBound and updateMap and updateMap:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                updateMapBound = true
                table.insert(roundCleanupConnections, updateMap.OnClientEvent:Connect(resetEmoteRuntime))
            end
            local services = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Services")
            local data = services and services:FindFirstChild("Data")
            local registry = data and data:FindFirstChild("ServerStateRegistryService")
            local changedEvent = registry and registry:FindFirstChild("ChangedEvent")
            if not stateBound and changedEvent and changedEvent:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                stateBound = true
                table.insert(roundCleanupConnections, changedEvent.Event:Connect(function(key, value)
                    if (key == "MapLoading" and value == true)
                    or (key == "Voting" and value == true) then
                        resetEmoteRuntime()
                    end
                end))
            end
            if updateMapBound and stateBound then break end
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end)
end
function rebindRockinExternalJointPart(part, clonedEM, rig)
    if not part or clonedEM:IsAncestorOf(part) then
        return part
    end
    local rigPart = rig:FindFirstChild(part.Name, true)
    if rigPart and not clonedEM:IsAncestorOf(rigPart) then
        return rigPart
    end
    return part
end
function getRelativeInstancePath(inst, root)
    if not inst or not root then return nil end
    local ok, instFull = pcall(function() return inst:GetFullName() end)
    local okRoot, rootFull = pcall(function() return root:GetFullName() end)
    if not ok or not okRoot then return nil end
    local prefix = rootFull .. "."
    if instFull:sub(1, #prefix) ~= prefix then return nil end
    return instFull:sub(#prefix + 1)
end
function findGhoulishJointPart(part, sourceModel, clonedModel, rig)
    if not part then return nil end
    if clonedModel:IsAncestorOf(part) then return part end
    local relativePath = getRelativeInstancePath(part, sourceModel)
    if relativePath then
        local clonePart = clonedModel
        for segment in string.gmatch(relativePath, "[^%.]+") do
            clonePart = clonePart:FindFirstChild(segment)
            if not clonePart then break end
        end
        if clonePart and clonePart:IsA("BasePart") then
            return clonePart
        end
    end
    local rigPart = rig:FindFirstChild(part.Name, true)
    if rigPart and not clonedModel:IsAncestorOf(rigPart) then
        return rigPart
    end
    return part
end
local GHOULISH_ROOT_OFFSET = CFrame.new(0, -4.1, 3)
function spawnEmoteProps(emote, rig)
    local cacheKey = emote.emoteKey or emote.emoteName
    local mod = emoteModuleCache[cacheKey]
    if not mod and emote.emoteName == "Broom" then
        buildEmoteCache()
        mod = emoteModuleCache[cacheKey]
    end
    if not mod then return end
    local cc, emoteModel
    if emote.selectionVariant then
        local selection = mod:FindFirstChild("Selection")
        local variant = selection and selection:FindFirstChild(emote.selectionVariant)
        cc = variant and variant:FindFirstChild("CharacterClassic")
        emoteModel = cc and cc:FindFirstChild("EmoteModel")
    end
    if not emoteModel then
        cc = mod:FindFirstChild("CharacterClassic")
        emoteModel = cc and cc:FindFirstChild("EmoteModel")
    end
    if not emoteModel and emote.emoteName == "Broom" then
        for _, inst in ipairs(mod:GetDescendants()) do
            if inst.Name == "EmoteModel" then
                emoteModel = inst
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if not emoteModel and not emote.selectionVariants then return end
    local propsParent = rig
    if emote.emoteName == "SolarSlayer" then
        local solarFolder = rig:FindFirstChild("__YY_SolarSlayerProps")
        if not solarFolder then
            solarFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
            solarFolder.Name = "__YY_SolarSlayerProps"
            solarFolder.Parent = rig
        end
        propsParent = solarFolder
    end
    if emote.selectionVariants and emote.emoteName == "SolarSlayer" then
        local selection = mod:FindFirstChild("Selection")
        if selection then
            for _, variantId in ipairs(emote.selectionVariants) do
                local variant = selection:FindFirstChild(variantId)
                local variantCC = variant and variant:FindFirstChild("CharacterClassic")
                local variantModel = variantCC and variantCC:FindFirstChild("EmoteModel")
                if variantModel then
                    local combinedProp = variantModel:Clone()
                    combinedProp.Parent = propsParent
                    table.insert(activeProps, combinedProp)
                    for _, prop in ipairs(combinedProp:GetDescendants()) do
                        if prop:IsA("BasePart") then
                            pcall(function()
                                prop.CanCollide = false
                                prop.CanTouch = false
                                prop.CanQuery = false
                                prop.Massless = true
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                    for _, motor in ipairs(combinedProp:GetDescendants()) do
                        if motor:IsA("Motor6D") or motor:IsA("Weld") or motor:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
                            local sourcePart0 = motor.Part0
                            if sourcePart0 and not combinedProp:IsAncestorOf(sourcePart0) then
                                local rigPart = rig:FindFirstChild(sourcePart0.Name)
                                if rigPart and rigPart:IsA("BasePart") then motor.Part0 = rigPart end
                            end
                            local sourcePart1 = motor.Part1
                            if sourcePart1 and not combinedProp:IsAncestorOf(sourcePart1) then
                                local rigPart = rig:FindFirstChild(sourcePart1.Name)
                                if rigPart and rigPart:IsA("BasePart") then motor.Part1 = rigPart end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
    local clonedEM = emoteModel:Clone()
    clonedEM.Parent = propsParent
    table.insert(activeProps, clonedEM)
    if emote.emoteName == "SolarSlayer"
    or emote.emoteName == "GhoulishGalleon"
    or emote.emoteName == "Broom" then
        for _, prop in ipairs(clonedEM:GetDescendants()) do
            if prop:IsA("BasePart") then
                pcall(function() prop.CanCollide = false end)
                pcall(function() prop.CanTouch = false end)
                pcall(function() prop.CanQuery = false end)
                pcall(function() prop.Massless = true end)
            end
        end
    end
    if emote.emoteName == "GhoulishGalleon" then
        local rigRoot = rig:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true)
            or rig:FindFirstChild("Torso", true)
        local rootMotor = nil
        local hull = nil
        for _, joint in ipairs(clonedEM:GetDescendants()) do
            if joint:IsA("Motor6D")
            and joint.Name == "Hull"
            and joint.Parent == clonedEM then
                rootMotor = joint
            end
            if joint:IsA("BasePart") and joint.Name == "Hull" then
                hull = hull or joint
            end
        end
        local targetCFrame = hull and hull.CFrame or nil
        if rootMotor and rigRoot then
            local okC0, c0 = pcall(function() return rootMotor.C0 end)
            local okC1, c1 = pcall(function() return rootMotor.C1 end)
            if okC0 and okC1 and typeof(c0) == "CFrame" and typeof(c1) == "CFrame" then
                targetCFrame = rigRoot.CFrame
                    * GHOULISH_ROOT_OFFSET
                    * c0
                    * c1:Inverse()
            end
        end
        if hull and targetCFrame then
            pcall(function() hull.CFrame = targetCFrame end)
        end
        for _, joint in ipairs(clonedEM:GetDescendants()) do
            if joint ~= rootMotor
            and (joint:IsA("Motor6D") or joint:IsA("Weld")) then
                joint.Part0 = findGhoulishJointPart(joint.Part0, emoteModel, clonedEM, rig)
                joint.Part1 = findGhoulishJointPart(joint.Part1, emoteModel, clonedEM, rig)
            end
        end
        if rootMotor then
            rootMotor:Destroy()
        end
        if rigRoot and hull then
            local follow = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
            follow.Name = "__YY_GhoulishFollow"
            follow.Part0 = rigRoot
            follow.Part1 = hull
            follow.Parent = rigRoot
            table.insert(activeProps, follow)
        end
        return
    end
    for _, motor in ipairs(clonedEM:GetDescendants()) do
        if motor:IsA("Motor6D")
        or ((emote.emoteName == "SolarSlayer" or emote.emoteName == "Broom")
        and (motor:IsA("Weld") or motor:IsA("WeldConstraint"))) then
            local sourcePart0 = motor.Part0
            if sourcePart0 and not clonedEM:IsAncestorOf(sourcePart0) then
                local rigPart = rig:FindFirstChild(sourcePart0.Name)
                if rigPart and rigPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                    motor.Part0 = rigPart
                end
            end
            local sourcePart1 = motor.Part1
            if sourcePart1 and not clonedEM:IsAncestorOf(sourcePart1) then
                local rigPart = rig:FindFirstChild(sourcePart1.Name)
                if rigPart and rigPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                    motor.Part1 = rigPart
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
function playEmoteSound(emote, rig)
    if emote.soundId == "" then return end
    local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
    snd.SoundId = emote.soundId
    snd.Volume  = 0.5
    snd.Looped  = false
    snd.Parent  = rig
    snd:Play()
    activeSound = snd
end
function onEmoteAnimPlayed(anim, track)
    if not EmoteState.enabled or not EmoteState.emoteIdx then return end
    if substituting then return end
    local id = track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId
    if not id then return end
    if IDLE_IDS[id] then
        local selectedEmote = EmoteState.emoteIdx and EMOTES[EmoteState.emoteIdx]
        if selectedEmote
        and selectedEmote.emoteName == "GhoulishGalleon"
        and inEvadeEmote then
            return
        end
        if activeVisualTrack then task.spawn(cleanupActiveEmote) end
        return
    end
    if not inEvadeEmote then return end
    local emote = EMOTES[EmoteState.emoteIdx]
    if not emote or emote.animId == "" then return end
    if emote.animId == id then return end
    substituting = true
    cleanupActiveEmote()
    local visualEpoch = emoteGeneration
    pcall(function()
        local rig = anim.Parent.Parent
        for _, snd in ipairs(rig:GetDescendants()) do
            if snd:IsA("Sound") and snd.IsPlaying then snd:Stop() end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if activeSoundKillerConn then
            activeSoundKillerConn:Disconnect()
            activeSoundKillerConn = nil
        end
        local playersFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Players")
        local charModel = playersFolder and playersFolder:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name)
        if charModel then
            local hrp = charModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local soundPos = hrp and hrp:FindFirstChild("SoundPos")
            if soundPos then
                for _, snd in ipairs(soundPos:GetChildren()) do
                    if snd:IsA("Sound") and snd.Name == "EmoteSound" then
                        pcall(function() snd:Stop() end)
                    end
                end
            end
            activeSoundKillerConn = charModel.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(inst)
                if inst:IsA("Sound") and inst.Name == "EmoteSound" then
                    task.defer(function()
                        pcall(function() inst:Stop() end)
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local rig = anim.Parent.Parent
        for _, obj in ipairs(rig:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("Model") and obj ~= rig:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                obj:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end)
    local fake = Instance.new("Animation")
    fake.AnimationId = emote.animId
    local ok, visualTrack = pcall(function()
        return anim:LoadAnimation(fake)
    end)
    if ok and visualTrack then
        if visualEpoch ~= emoteGeneration or not inEvadeEmote then
            pcall(function() visualTrack:Stop(0); fake:Destroy() end)
            task.defer(function() substituting = false end)
            return
        end
        activeVisualTrack = visualTrack
        visualTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
        visualTrack.Looped = true
        visualTrack:Play()
        if visualEpoch ~= emoteGeneration or not inEvadeEmote then
            if activeVisualTrack == visualTrack then activeVisualTrack = nil end
            pcall(function() visualTrack:Stop(0); fake:Destroy() end)
            task.defer(function() substituting = false end)
            return
        end
        pcall(function()
            local rig = anim.Parent.Parent
            spawnEmoteProps(emote, rig)
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local rig = anim.Parent.Parent
            playEmoteSound(emote, rig)
        end)
        visualTrack.Stopped:Connect(function()
            if activeVisualTrack ~= visualTrack then
                pcall(function() fake:Destroy() end)
                return
            end
            if not inEvadeEmote or visualEpoch ~= emoteGeneration then
                activeVisualTrack = nil
                pcall(function() fake:Destroy() end)
                return
            end
            if activeVisualTrackRestarting then return end
            activeVisualTrackRestarting = true
            task.defer(function()
                activeVisualTrackRestarting = false
                if activeVisualTrack ~= visualTrack
                    or not inEvadeEmote
                    or visualEpoch ~= emoteGeneration then
                    return
                end
                pcall(function()
                    visualTrack.Looped = true
                    visualTrack.TimePosition = 0
                    visualTrack:Play(0)
                end)
            end)
        end)
    end
    task.defer(function() substituting = false end)
end
function connectEmoteRig(humAnim, acAnim)
    for _, c in ipairs(emoteConnections) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    emoteConnections = {}
    if humAnim then
        emoteConnections[#emoteConnections+1] = humAnim.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
            onEmoteAnimPlayed(humAnim, track)
        end)
    end
    if acAnim then
        emoteConnections[#emoteConnections+1] = acAnim.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
            onEmoteAnimPlayed(acAnim, track)
        end)
    end
end
function getEmoteRigAnimators()
    local rigs = workspace:FindFirstChild("Rigs")
    if not rigs then return nil, nil end
    local rig = rigs:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name)
    if not rig then return nil, nil end
    local hum     = rig:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local humAnim = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    local ac      = rig:FindFirstChildOfClass("AnimationController")
    local acAnim  = ac and ac:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    return humAnim, acAnim
end
buildEmoteCache()
task.spawn(function()
    local humAnim, acAnim = getEmoteRigAnimators()
    if not humAnim and not acAnim then
        local rigs = workspace:WaitForChild("Rigs", 10)
        if not rigs then return end
        local rig = rigs:WaitForChild(LocalPlayer.Name, 10)
        if not rig then return end
        local hum = rig:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        humAnim = hum and hum:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
        local ac = rig:WaitForChild("AnimationController", 5)
        acAnim  = ac and ac:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
    end
    pcall(connectEmoteRig, humAnim, acAnim)
end)
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    bindEmoteCharacterLifecycle(char)
    task.wait(1)
    local humAnim, acAnim = getEmoteRigAnimators()
    if humAnim or acAnim then pcall(connectEmoteRig, humAnim, acAnim) end
end)
LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
    resetEmoteRuntime()
    disconnectEmoteConnections(emoteLifecycleConnections)
end)
bindEmoteCharacterLifecycle(LocalPlayer.Character)
setupRoundCleanupWatchers()
