AllDesign = {}
AllDesign.TabIcons = {
    Senju = {
        All = "rbxassetid://139112968149605",
        Emote = "rbxassetid://101452871224485",
    },
}
AllDesign.DefaultTabIcons = {
    All = "rbxassetid://125111822154519",
    Emote = "rbxassetid://97339999657987",
}
function AllDesign.CurrentIcon(key)
    local themeName = nil
    pcall(function()
        themeName = UI.CurrentTheme
    end)
    local themed = AllDesign.TabIcons[themeName]
    if type(themed) == "table" and themed[key] then
        return themed[key]
    end
    return AllDesign.DefaultTabIcons[key]
end
function AllDesign.Card(page, name, height, transparency)
    local card = Instance.new("Frame")
    card.Name = name
    card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
    card.BackgroundColor3 = GetAvatarPalette().Secondary
    card.BackgroundTransparency = transparency
    card.BorderSizePixel = 0
    card.ZIndex = 10
    card.Parent = page
    card:SetAttribute("ThemeRole", "Secondary")
    AvatarCorner(card, 10)
    local stroke = createAvatarStroke(card, 0.55)
    return card, stroke
end
function AllDesign.Label(parent, spanish, english, textSize, font, role, position, size)
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Position = position
    label.Size = size
    label.Text = scriptText(spanish, english)
    label.TextColor3 = GetAvatarPalette()[role]
    label.TextSize = textSize
    label.Font = font
    label.TextWrapped = true
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    label.ZIndex = 12
    label.Parent = parent
    label:SetAttribute("ThemeTextRole", role)
    label:SetAttribute("IgnoreTextEffect", true)
    label:SetAttribute("TextSpanish", spanish)
    label:SetAttribute("TextEnglish", english)
    return label
end
function AllDesign.Header(page, titleSpanish, titleEnglish, descSpanish, descEnglish, iconKey)
    iconKey = iconKey or "All"
    local palette = GetAvatarPalette()
    local header = AllDesign.Card(page, "AllDesignHeader", 78, 0.78)
    local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
    bar.Name = "AllDesignAccentBar"
    bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 44)
    bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -22)
    bar.BackgroundColor3 = palette.Accent
    bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bar.ZIndex = 11
    bar.Parent = header
    bar:SetAttribute("ThemeRole", "Accent")
    AvatarCorner(bar, 999)
    local iconBox = Instance.new("Frame")
    iconBox.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 44)
    iconBox.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 17)
    iconBox.BackgroundColor3 = palette.AccentOff
    iconBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    iconBox.ZIndex = 11
    iconBox.Parent = header
    iconBox:SetAttribute("ThemeRole", "AccentOff")
    AvatarCorner(iconBox, 12)
    createAvatarStroke(iconBox, 0.45)
    local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28)
    icon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8)
    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    icon.Image = AllDesign.CurrentIcon(iconKey)
    icon.ImageColor3 = palette.Text
    icon.ZIndex = 12
    icon.Parent = iconBox
    icon:SetAttribute("ThemeImageRole", "Text")
    local title = AllDesign.Label(header, titleSpanish, titleEnglish, 15, Enum.Font.GothamBlack, "Text",
        UDim2.fromOffset(74, 12), UDim2.new(1, -112, 0, 20))
    title.TextWrapped = false
    title.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    title.ZIndex = 11
    local spark = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    spark.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20)
    spark.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 10)
    spark.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    spark.Text = "✦"
    spark.TextColor3 = palette.Accent
    spark.TextSize = 16
    spark.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    spark.ZIndex = 12
    spark.Parent = header
    spark:SetAttribute("ThemeTextRole", "Accent")
    spark:SetAttribute("IgnoreTextEffect", true)
    local description = AllDesign.Label(header, descSpanish, descEnglish, 11, Enum.Font.GothamMedium, "TextDim",
        UDim2.fromOffset(74, 34), UDim2.new(1, -88, 0, 36))
    description.ZIndex = 11
    local function refreshIcon()
        task.defer(function()
            if icon and icon.Parent then
                icon.Image = AllDesign.CurrentIcon(iconKey)
            end
        end)
    end
    header:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundColor3"):Connect(refreshIcon)
    bar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundColor3"):Connect(refreshIcon)
    description:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextColor3"):Connect(refreshIcon)
    return header
end
function AllDesign.Field(page, titleSpanish, titleEnglish, placeholderSpanish, placeholderEnglish)
    local palette = GetAvatarPalette()
    local order = 1
    for _, child in ipairs(page:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == "AllDesignField" then order = order + 1 end
    end
    local card, stroke = AllDesign.Card(page, "AllDesignField", 58, 0.7)
    stroke.Transparency = 0.45
    local badge = Instance.new("Frame")
    badge.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28)
    badge.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 15)
    badge.BackgroundColor3 = palette.AccentOff
    badge.BorderSizePixel = 0
    badge.ZIndex = 11
    badge.Parent = card
    badge:SetAttribute("ThemeRole", "AccentOff")
    AvatarCorner(badge, 8)
    local number = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    number.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    number.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    number.Text = tostring(order)
    number.TextColor3 = palette.Text
    number.TextSize = 13
    number.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    number.ZIndex = 12
    number.Parent = badge
    number:SetAttribute("ThemeTextRole", "Text")
    number:SetAttribute("IgnoreTextEffect", true)
    local caption = AllDesign.Label(card, titleSpanish, titleEnglish, 10, Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextDim",
        UDim2.fromOffset(52, 8), UDim2.new(1, -64, 0, 14))
    caption.TextWrapped = false
    caption.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    local input = Instance.new("TextBox")
    input.Size = UDim2.new(1, -64, 0, 28)
    input.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 24)
    input.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    input.BorderSizePixel = 0
    input.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    input.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    input.PlaceholderText = scriptText(placeholderSpanish, placeholderEnglish)
    input.PlaceholderColor3 = caption.TextColor3
    input.Text = ""
    input.TextColor3 = palette.Text
    input.TextSize = 14
    input.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    input.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    input.ZIndex = 12
    input.Parent = card
    input:SetAttribute("ThemeTextRole", "Text")
    input:SetAttribute("PlaceholderSpanish", placeholderSpanish)
    input:SetAttribute("PlaceholderEnglish", placeholderEnglish)
    input.Focused:Connect(function()
        local active = GetAvatarPalette()
        stroke.Color = active.Accent
        stroke.Thickness = 1.5
        stroke.Transparency = 0.05
        badge.BackgroundColor3 = active.Accent
        number.TextColor3 = AvatarContrastColor(active.Accent)
    end)
    input.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local active = GetAvatarPalette()
        stroke.Color = active.Stroke
        stroke.Thickness = 1
        stroke.Transparency = 0.45
        badge.BackgroundColor3 = active.AccentOff
        number.TextColor3 = active.Text
    end)
    caption:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextColor3"):Connect(function()
        input.PlaceholderColor3 = caption.TextColor3
    end)
    return input
end
function AllDesign.Button(page, spanish, english, callback, primary)
    local palette = GetAvatarPalette()
    local restTransparency = primary and 0.12 or 0.7
    local hoverTransparency = primary and 0.04 or 0.55
    local pressTransparency = 0.4
    local holder = AllDesign.Card(page, primary and "AllDesignPrimaryButton" or "AllDesignSecondaryButton",
        primary and 44 or 38, restTransparency)
    if primary then
        holder.BackgroundColor3 = palette.Accent
        holder:SetAttribute("ThemeRole", "Accent")
    end
    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    button.BorderSizePixel = 0
    button.AutoButtonColor = false
    button.Text = ""
    button.ZIndex = 11
    button.Parent = holder
    local label = AllDesign.Label(holder, spanish, english, primary and 14 or 12,
        primary and Enum.Font.GothamBlack or Enum.Font.GothamBold, "Text",
        UDim2.fromOffset(31, 0), UDim2.new(1, -62, 1, 0))
    label.TextWrapped = false
    label.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    local arrow = nil
    if primary then
        label:SetAttribute("ThemeTextRole", nil)
        arrow = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        arrow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28)
        arrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0.5, -14)
        arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        arrow.Text = "›"
        arrow.TextSize = 28
        arrow.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        arrow.ZIndex = 12
        arrow.Parent = holder
        arrow:SetAttribute("IgnoreTextEffect", true)
        local function repaintOnAccent()
            local onAccent = AvatarContrastColor(GetAvatarPalette().Accent)
            label.TextColor3 = onAccent
            arrow.TextColor3 = onAccent
        end
        repaintOnAccent()
        holder:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundColor3"):Connect(repaintOnAccent)
    end
    button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        KeyTweenService:Create(holder, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {BackgroundTransparency = hoverTransparency}):Play()
    end)
    button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        KeyTweenService:Create(holder, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {BackgroundTransparency = restTransparency}):Play()
    end)
    button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
        KeyTweenService:Create(holder, TweenInfo.new(0.08), {BackgroundTransparency = pressTransparency}):Play()
        task.delay(0.16, function()
            KeyTweenService:Create(holder, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {BackgroundTransparency = restTransparency}):Play()
        end)
    end)
    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        callback()
    end)
    return holder
end
function AllDesign.Status(page, spanish, english)
    local holder = AllDesign.Card(page, "AllDesignStatus", 50, 0.78)
    local neutral = Color3.fromRGB(175, 185, 205)
    local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    icon.Name = "StatusIcon"
    icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24)
    icon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 13)
    icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    icon.Image = "rbxassetid://88658875299906"
    icon.ImageColor3 = AvatarAdaptStatusColor(neutral)
    icon.ZIndex = 12
    icon.Parent = holder
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = "AllDesignStatusText"
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 1, -12)
    label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 6)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = scriptText(spanish, english)
    label.TextColor3 = AvatarAdaptStatusColor(neutral)
    label.TextSize = 11
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    label.TextWrapped = true
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    label.ZIndex = 12
    label.Parent = holder
    label:SetAttribute("IgnoreTextEffect", true)
    label:SetAttribute("TextSpanish", spanish)
    label:SetAttribute("TextEnglish", english)
    label:SetAttribute("StatusColor", neutral)
    holder:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundColor3"):Connect(function()
        local stored = label:GetAttribute("StatusColor")
        if typeof(stored) == "Color3" then
            label.TextColor3 = AvatarAdaptStatusColor(stored)
            icon.ImageColor3 = AvatarAdaptStatusColor(stored)
        end
    end)
    return label
end
function AllDesign.SetStatus(label, text, color)
    if not (label and label.Parent) then return end
    local adapted = AvatarAdaptStatusColor(color)
    label:SetAttribute("TextSpanish", nil)
    label:SetAttribute("TextEnglish", nil)
    label:SetAttribute("StatusColor", color)
    label.Text = text
    label.TextColor3 = adapted
    local icon = label.Parent:FindFirstChild("StatusIcon")
    if icon then icon.ImageColor3 = adapted end
end
function setupEmoteCarousel()
local EmoteCarousel = nil
local EmotePages = {
    PC = nil,
    Mobile = nil,
}
local EmoteMobileGridController = nil
local EmoteActivePage = nil
local EmoteCarouselAnimating = false
local EmoteCarouselHeightUpdating = false
local EmoteCarouselSwipe = {
    input = nil,
    start = nil,
    threshold = 36,
}
local function trackEmoteInstance(inst)
    if type(inst) == "table" then
        inst = inst.Holder
    end
    return inst
end
local function ensureEmoteCarousel()
    if EmoteCarousel and EmoteCarousel.Parent then
        return EmoteCarousel
    end
    EmoteCarousel = Instance.new("Frame")
    EmoteCarousel.Name = "EmoteCarousel"
    EmoteCarousel.Parent = TabEmote.Page
    EmoteCarousel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 430)
    EmoteCarousel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    EmoteCarousel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    EmoteCarousel.ClipsDescendants = true
    EmoteCarousel.LayoutOrder = -900
    EmoteCarousel.ZIndex = 8
    return EmoteCarousel
end
local function updateEmoteCarouselHeight(page)
    if EmoteCarouselHeightUpdating then return end
    if not page or not page.Parent then return end
    EmoteCarouselHeightUpdating = true
    task.defer(function()
        if page and page.Parent then
            local layout = page:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
            if layout then
                local contentHeight = layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 8
                local h = math.max(430, contentHeight)
                if page.Size.Y.Offset ~= h then
                    page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h)
                end
                if EmoteActivePage == page and EmoteCarousel and EmoteCarousel.Parent
                    and EmoteCarousel.Size.Y.Offset ~= h then
                    EmoteCarousel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h)
                end
            end
        end
        EmoteCarouselHeightUpdating = false
    end)
end
local function makeEmoteCarouselPage(name)
    local viewport = ensureEmoteCarousel()
    local page = Instance.new("Frame")
    page.Name = name or "EmoteCarouselContent"
    page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 430)
    page.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    page.BorderSizePixel = 0
    page.ZIndex = 9
    page.ClipsDescendants = false
    page.Parent = viewport
    local pageLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    pageLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pageLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    pageLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
    pageLayout.Parent = page
    pageLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
        updateEmoteCarouselHeight(page)
    end)
    task.defer(function()
        updateEmoteCarouselHeight(page)
    end)
    return page
end
local function clearMobileEmoteSelection()
    EmoteState.emoteIdx = nil
    cleanupActiveEmote()
    if EmoteMobileGridController and EmoteMobileGridController.SetSelected then
        pcall(function() EmoteMobileGridController.SetSelected(nil) end)
    end
end
local function clearEmotePages()
    for _, page in pairs(EmotePages) do
        if page and page.Parent then
            pcall(function() page:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    EmotePages.PC = nil
    EmotePages.Mobile = nil
    EmoteMobileGridController = nil
    EmoteActivePage = nil
    EmoteCarouselHeightUpdating = false
end

        "DynastyDrumming", "FireworkBlast", "ClassicJeep", "RainingTacos", "SkateboardStroll", "TurkeyJockey",

    AllDesign.Button(page, "List of emotes", "List of emotes", function()
        if not emoteListPanel or not emoteListPanel.Parent then return end
        emoteListPanel.Visible = not emoteListPanel.Visible
        if emoteListPanel.Visible and emoteListScroll then
            emoteListScroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        end
    end, false)
    emoteListPanel = AllDesign.Card(page, "PCEmoteListPanel", 220, 0.7)
    emoteListPanel.Visible = false
    emoteListScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    emoteListScroll.Name = "PCEmoteListScroll"
    emoteListScroll.Parent = emoteListPanel
    emoteListScroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
    emoteListScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -8)
    emoteListScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1

    emoteListScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4

        row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        row.ZIndex = 12
        row:SetAttribute("ThemeRole", "Accent")
        row:SetAttribute("ThemeTextRole", "Text")
        local rowPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        rowPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 12)
        rowPadding.Parent = row
        local rowCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        rowCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        rowCorner.Parent = row
        row.MouseEnter:Connect(function() row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.78 end)
        row.MouseLeave:Connect(function() row.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end)
        row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            sourceEmoteName = emoteListName
            sourceEmoteInput.Text = emoteListName
            emoteListPanel.Visible = false
            applyVisualEmote()
        end)
    end
    function findEmoteFolder(name)
        local rep = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local cleanName = trimName(name)
        if cleanName == "" then return nil end
        local folder = rep:FindFirstChild(cleanName, true)
        if folder then return folder end
        local compactName = cleanName:gsub("%s+", "")
        if compactName ~= cleanName then
            folder = rep:FindFirstChild(compactName, true)
        end
        return folder
    end
    function applyVisualEmote()
        local targetName = trimName(targetEmoteName)
        local sourceName = trimName(sourceEmoteName)
        if targetName == "" or sourceName == "" then
            setStatus("Faltan los dos nombres de emote.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 185, 115))
            return
        end
        local targetFolder = findEmoteFolder(targetName)
        local sourceFolder = findEmoteFolder(sourceName)
        if not targetFolder then
            setStatus("No se encontró el emote actual: " .. targetName, Color3.fromRGB(255, 125, 125))
            return
        end
        if not sourceFolder then
            setStatus("No se encontró el emote visual: " .. sourceName, Color3.fromRGB(255, 125, 125))
            return
        end
        if targetFolder == sourceFolder then
            setStatus("El emote actual y el visual deben ser diferentes.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 185, 115))
            return
        end
        local clones = {}
        for _, item in ipairs(sourceFolder:GetChildren()) do
            local ok, clonedItem = pcall(function() return item:Clone() end)
            if not ok or not clonedItem then
                for _, pending in ipairs(clones) do pcall(function() pending:Destroy() end) end
                setStatus("No se pudo preparar el emote visual.", Color3.fromRGB(255, 125, 125))
                return
            end
            table.insert(clones, clonedItem)
        end
        for _, item in ipairs(targetFolder:GetChildren()) do pcall(function() item:Destroy() end) end
        for _, clonedItem in ipairs(clones) do clonedItem.Parent = targetFolder end
        setStatus("Aplicado: " .. targetName .. " → " .. sourceName, Color3.fromRGB(150, 255, 190))
        print("✅ PC VISUAL EMOTE: " .. targetName .. " → " .. sourceName)
    end
    AllDesign.Button(page, "APLICAR EMOTE VISUAL", "APPLY VISUAL EMOTE", applyVisualEmote, true)
    statusLabel = AllDesign.Status(page, "Listo para aplicar un cambio visual.", "Ready to apply a visual change.")
    TabEmote:ResetContentContainer()
    for index, child in ipairs(page:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
            child.LayoutOrder = index
        end
    end
    UI.PCEmoteChanger = true
    updateEmoteCarouselHeight(page)
    return page
end
getgenv().YYIsPCEmotePlatform = function()
    local detector = UI and UI.DetectPlatformType
    if type(detector) == "function" then
        local ok, result = pcall(detector)
        if ok then return result == "PC" end
    end
    local isPC = false
    pcall(function()
        local userInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local preferredInput = userInputService.PreferredInput
        if preferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.KeyboardAndMouse then
            isPC = true
            return
        elseif preferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.Touch then
            return
        end
        local hasKeyboard = userInputService.KeyboardEnabled
        local hasMouse = userInputService.MouseEnabled
        local hasTouch = userInputService.TouchEnabled
        if hasKeyboard and hasMouse and not hasTouch then
            isPC = true
            return
        end
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local viewportWidth = camera and camera.ViewportSize.X or 0
        isPC = viewportWidth >= 1080
    end)
    return isPC
end
getgenv().YYBuildMobileEmoteChanger = function()
    EmoteState.enabled = true
    local page = EmotePages.Mobile
    if page and page.Parent then
        return page
    end
    page = makeEmoteCarouselPage("MobileEmoteCarouselContent")
    EmotePages.Mobile = page
    TabEmote:SetContentContainer(page)
    trackEmoteInstance(TabEmote:CreateLabel("Emotes", "Emotes", 16))
    EmoteMobileGridController = TabEmote:CreateImageGrid(nil, nil, EmoteGridItems, function(item, index, isSelected)
        if isSelected then
            EmoteState.emoteIdx = emoteIndexByKey[item.emoteKey]
        else
            EmoteState.emoteIdx = nil
            cleanupActiveEmote()
        end
    end, true)
    TabEmote:ResetContentContainer()
    updateEmoteCarouselHeight(page)
    return page
end
local emoteContentMode = nil
local function setEmoteContentMode(mode, direction)
    if EmoteCarouselAnimating then return end
    local newPage = EmotePages[mode]
    if not newPage or not newPage.Parent then return end
    local oldPage = EmoteActivePage
    if oldPage == newPage and emoteContentMode == mode then
        return
    end
    emoteContentMode = mode
    EmoteState.enabled = mode == "Mobile"
    if mode == "PC" then
        clearMobileEmoteSelection()
    end
    updateEmoteCarouselHeight(newPage)
    if not oldPage or not oldPage.Parent then
        newPage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        EmoteActivePage = newPage
        return
    end
    local incomingStart = direction and direction > 0 and 1 or -1
    local outgoingEnd = direction and direction > 0 and -1 or 1
    newPage.Position = UDim2.new(incomingStart, 0, 0, 0)
    EmoteCarouselAnimating = true
    KeyTweenService:Create(oldPage, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Position = UDim2.new(outgoingEnd, 0, 0, 0)
    }):Play()
    local tween = KeyTweenService:Create(newPage, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    })
    tween:Play()
    EmoteActivePage = newPage
    tween.Completed:Connect(function()
        EmoteCarouselAnimating = false
    end)
end
clearEmotePages()
local initialPC = getgenv().YYIsPCEmotePlatform()
getgenv().YYBuildPCEmoteChanger()
getgenv().YYBuildMobileEmoteChanger()
setEmoteContentMode(initialPC and "PC" or "Mobile")
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
    if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
    local page = TabEmote.Page
    if not page.Visible then return end
    local pos = input.Position
    local ap, as = page.AbsolutePosition, page.AbsoluteSize
    if pos.X < ap.X or pos.X > ap.X + as.X or pos.Y < ap.Y or pos.Y > ap.Y + as.Y then return end
    EmoteCarouselSwipe.input = input
    EmoteCarouselSwipe.start = input.Position
end)
UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input ~= EmoteCarouselSwipe.input or not EmoteCarouselSwipe.start then return end
    local delta = input.Position - EmoteCarouselSwipe.start
    EmoteCarouselSwipe.input = nil
    EmoteCarouselSwipe.start = nil
    if math.abs(delta.X) < EmoteCarouselSwipe.threshold
        or math.abs(delta.X) <= math.abs(delta.Y) * 1.20
        or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
    local direction = delta.X < 0 and 1 or -1
    local nextMode = delta.X < 0 and "PC" or "Mobile"
    setEmoteContentMode(nextMode, direction)
end)
end
setupEmoteCarousel()
print("✓ EMOTE: Tab completed")
print("\n✓ OK: INTERFACE FULLY CONFIGURED")
print("\nLoading movement functions...")
function applyTeleportWalk()
    if not Config.EnableTeleportWalk and not Config.EnableTrimpTeleport then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char or not char.PrimaryPart then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    if hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
        pcall(function()
            char:TranslateBy(hum.MoveDirection * Config.TeleportMovementSpeed * 0.1)
        end)
    end
end
function applyEnhancedJump()
    if not Config.EnableEnhancedJump then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char or not char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then return end
    local hum = char.Humanoid
    if hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    if hum:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
        pcall(function()
            hum.JumpPower = Config.JumpHeight
            hum.UseJumpPower = true
        end)
    end
end
function applyAutoJump()
    if not Config.AutoJump then return end
    local now = tick()
    if now < autoJumpNextTime then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    if hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running then
        pcall(function()
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
            local jumpsPerSecond = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.AutoJumpSpeed) or 1.5, 0.5, 8)
            autoJumpNextTime = now + (1 / jumpsPerSecond)
        end)
    else
        autoJumpNextTime = math.min(autoJumpNextTime, now)
    end
end
local crouchToggleFn  = nil
local crouchResolving = false
local function resolveCrouchToggleAsync()
    if crouchToggleFn or crouchResolving then return end
    crouchResolving = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, btn = pcall(function()
            return LocalPlayer.PlayerGui
                :WaitForChild("Game",        5)
                :WaitForChild("HUD",         5)
                :WaitForChild("Mobile",      5)
                :WaitForChild("Right",       5)
                :WaitForChild("Mobile",      5)
                :WaitForChild("CrouchButton",5)
        end)
        if ok and btn then
            local conns = getconnections(btn.Activated)
            if not conns or #conns == 0 then
                conns = getconnections(btn.MouseButton1Click)
            end
            if conns and #conns > 0 and conns[1].Function then
                crouchToggleFn = conns[1].Function
                print("✓ Auto Crouch: CrouchButton resuelto correctamente")
            else
                warn("[AutoCrouch] sin conexiones accesibles en CrouchButton")
            end
        else
            warn("[AutoCrouch] CrouchButton no encontrado")
        end
        crouchResolving = false
    end)
end
function applyAutoCrouch()
    if not Config.AutoCrouch then return end
    if not crouchToggleFn then
        resolveCrouchToggleAsync()
        return
    end
    local now = tick()
    if now < autoCrouchNextTime then return end
    local ok = pcall(crouchToggleFn)
    if ok then
        local togglesPerSecond = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.AutoCrouchSpeed) or 1.5, 0.5, 100)
        autoCrouchNextTime = now + (1 / togglesPerSecond)
    end
end
do -- bloque de locales: libera 6 variables locales del script principal
local LAYER_JUMPS_DEFAULT_CAP = 1    -- dara: defaultMovementValues.JumpCap
local layerJumpsActive = false
local layerJumpsWarned = false
local layerJumpsRespawnToken = 0
local function layerJumpsSend(value)
    if not firesignal then return false end
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local tag = character and character:GetAttribute("Tag")
    if not tag then return false end
    return (pcall(function()
        firesignal(ReplicatedStorage.Events.CharacterTask.OnClientEvent, tag, "ModifyMovement", { "JumpCap", value })
    end))
end
local function layerJumpsReapplyAfterRespawn()
    layerJumpsActive = false
    layerJumpsRespawnToken = layerJumpsRespawnToken + 1
    local token = layerJumpsRespawnToken
    if not Config.EnableLayerJumps then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        for _ = 1, 20 do
            if token ~= layerJumpsRespawnToken or not Config.EnableLayerJumps then return end
            if layerJumpsSend(Config.LayerJumpsCount) then
                layerJumpsActive = true
                return
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    layerJumpsReapplyAfterRespawn()
    lastSpeedSelf, lastSpeedState, lastSpeedValue = nil, nil, nil
    getgenv().YYSpeedRestoreRequested = false
    if Config.EnableSpeedMultiplier then
        getgenv().YYSpeedApplyRequested = true
        task.delay(0.75, function()
            if getgenv().YYApplySpeedMultiplier then getgenv().YYApplySpeedMultiplier() end
        end)
    end
end)
function applyLayerJumps()
    if Config.EnableLayerJumps then
        if not firesignal then
            if not layerJumpsWarned then
                layerJumpsWarned = true
                warn("[Layer Jumps] No disponible: falta firesignal en este executor")
            end
            return
        end
        if layerJumpsSend(Config.LayerJumpsCount) then
            layerJumpsActive = true
        end
    elseif layerJumpsActive then
        if layerJumpsSend(LAYER_JUMPS_DEFAULT_CAP) then
            layerJumpsActive = false
        end
    end
end
if Config.EnableLayerJumps then
    layerJumpsReapplyAfterRespawn()
end
end -- fin del bloque de locales
local impulseCurrentSpeed = 42
local impulseWasAir = false
local activeImpulseBV = nil
function applyImpulseJump()
    if not Config.EnableImpulseJump then
        impulseWasAir = false
        impulseCurrentSpeed = Config.ImpulseJumpSpeed
        if activeImpulseBV then activeImpulseBV:Destroy() activeImpulseBV = nil end
        return
    end
    local baseSpeed = Config.ImpulseJumpSpeed
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    if not root or not humanoid then return end
    if humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
    local isAir = humanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
    if impulseWasAir and not isAir then
        impulseCurrentSpeed = baseSpeed
        impulseAirAccumulator = 0
        if activeImpulseBV then activeImpulseBV:Destroy() activeImpulseBV = nil end
    end
    impulseWasAir = isAir
    if not isAir then return end
    local lookDir = camera.CFrame.LookVector
    lookDir = Vector3.new(lookDir.X, 0, lookDir.Z)
    if lookDir.Magnitude ~= 0 then lookDir = lookDir.Unit end
    if not activeImpulseBV or not activeImpulseBV.Parent or activeImpulseBV.Parent ~= root then
        if activeImpulseBV then pcall(function() activeImpulseBV:Destroy() end) end
        activeImpulseBV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        activeImpulseBV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(4e5, 0, 4e5)
        activeImpulseBV.P = 1250
        activeImpulseBV.Parent = root
    end
    activeImpulseBV.Velocity = lookDir * impulseCurrentSpeed
end
function applyGravityModification()
    if not Config.EnableGravityMod then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char or not char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or not char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return end
    local hum = char.Humanoid
    local rootPart = char.HumanoidRootPart
    if hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    pcall(function()
        local velocity = rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if velocity.Y < 0 then
            rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                velocity.X,
                velocity.Y * Config.GravityScale,
                velocity.Z
            )
        end
    end)
end
function ActivateFrontalJump()
    if inEmote then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if char then
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if root and humanoid then
            if Config.EnableJumpAcceleration then
                humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                lastJumpTime = tick()
                return
            end
            local humanoidState = humanoid:GetState()
            local isOnGround = (humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed or humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
            if isOnGround then
                local lookDir = camera.CFrame.LookVector
                lookDir = Vector3.new(lookDir.X, 0, lookDir.Z)
                if lookDir.Magnitude ~= 0 then
                    lookDir = lookDir.Unit
                end
                local rayOriginFront = root.Position
                local rayDirectionFront = lookDir * 10
                _rpFrontal.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
                local raycastResultFront = Workspace:Raycast(rayOriginFront, rayDirectionFront, _rpFrontal)
                if raycastResultFront and not Config.EnableJumpAcceleration then
                    rampLocked = true
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                    task.delay(0.1, function()
                        if char and root then
                            local jumpBoost = getgenv().FrontalJumpSpeed * getgenv().RampMultiplier * 0.8
                            pcall(function()
                                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X,
                                    jumpBoost,
                                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z
                                )
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                else
                    if not rampLocked then
                        humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                    end
                end
                lastJumpTime = tick()
            end
        end
    end
end
function applyFrontalJump()
    if not Config.EnableFrontalJump and not Config.EnableJumpAcceleration then
        if activeBV then pcall(function() activeBV:Destroy() end) end
        activeBV = nil
        return
    end
    local deltaTime = tick() - lastTick
    lastTick = tick()
    local baseSpeed = Config.EnableJumpAcceleration and math.clamp(tonumber(Config.JumpAccelerationSpeed) or 42, 30, 110) or getgenv().FrontalJumpSpeed
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if char then
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if root and humanoid then
            local isAir = humanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
            local humanoidState = humanoid:GetState()
            local isOnGround = (humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed or humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) and not isAir
            local hitObject = false
            if wasAir and isOnGround then
                hitObject = true
                currentSpeed = baseSpeed
                airAccumulator = 0
                rampLocked = false
            end
            wasAir = isAir
            if isAir then
                local lookDir = camera.CFrame.LookVector
                lookDir = Vector3.new(lookDir.X, 0, lookDir.Z)
                if lookDir.Magnitude ~= 0 then
                    lookDir = lookDir.Unit
                end
                if not activeBV or not activeBV.Parent or activeBV.Parent ~= root then
                    if activeBV then pcall(function() activeBV:Destroy() end) end
                    activeBV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                    activeBV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(4e5, 0, 4e5)
                    activeBV.P = 1250
                    activeBV.Parent = root
                end
                activeBV.Velocity = lookDir * currentSpeed
            else
                airAccumulator = airAccumulator + deltaTime
                while airAccumulator >= 0.04 do
                    airAccumulator = airAccumulator - 0.04
                    currentSpeed = math.min(baseSpeed + 10, currentSpeed + 0.3)
                end
                local lookDir = camera.CFrame.LookVector
                lookDir = Vector3.new(lookDir.X, 0, lookDir.Z)
                if lookDir.Magnitude ~= 0 then
                    lookDir = lookDir.Unit
                end
                local impulsePower = currentSpeed
                if not Config.EnableJumpAcceleration then
                    local rayOriginFront = root.Position
                    local rayDirectionFront = lookDir * 8
                    _rpFrontal.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
                    local raycastResultFront = Workspace:Raycast(rayOriginFront, rayDirectionFront, _rpFrontal)
                    if raycastResultFront then
                        impulsePower = currentSpeed * getgenv().RampMultiplier
                    end
                end
                if not activeBV or not activeBV.Parent or activeBV.Parent ~= root then
                    if activeBV then pcall(function() activeBV:Destroy() end) end
                    activeBV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                    activeBV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(4e5, 0, 4e5)
                    activeBV.P = 1250
                    activeBV.Parent = root
                end
                activeBV.Velocity = lookDir * impulsePower
                currentSpeed = math.max(baseSpeed, currentSpeed - 2.5 * deltaTime)
                if tick() - lastJumpTime >= jumpInterval then
                    ActivateFrontalJump()
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
local rampMultPrevState = nil
function applyRampMultiplier()
    if not Config.EnableRampMultiplier then
        rampMultPrevState = nil
        return
    end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    if not root or not humanoid then return end
    local state = humanoid:GetState()
    local justJumped = (state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping and rampMultPrevState ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
    rampMultPrevState = state
    local moveDir = humanoid.MoveDirection
    if moveDir.Magnitude < 0.05 then return end
    _rpRamp.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
    local hit = Workspace:Raycast(root.Position, moveDir.Unit * 8, _rpRamp)
    if not hit then return end
    if justJumped then
        local boost = humanoid.WalkSpeed * getgenv().RampMultiplierValue * 0.8
        pcall(function()
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X,
                boost,
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z
            )
        end)
    elseif humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
        local currentVel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local boosted = moveDir.Unit * humanoid.WalkSpeed * getgenv().RampMultiplierValue
        pcall(function()
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(boosted.X, currentVel.Y, boosted.Z)
        end)
    end
end
function ActivateBackJump()
    if inEmote then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if char then
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if root and humanoid then
            local humanoidState = humanoid:GetState()
            local isOnGround = (humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed or humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
            if isOnGround then
                local lookDir = camera.CFrame.LookVector
                local backDir = Vector3.new(-lookDir.X, 0, -lookDir.Z)
                if backDir.Magnitude ~= 0 then
                    backDir = backDir.Unit
                end
                local rayOriginBack = root.Position
                local rayDirectionBack = backDir * 10
                _rpBack.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
                local raycastResultBack = Workspace:Raycast(rayOriginBack, rayDirectionBack, _rpBack)
                if raycastResultBack then
                    backRampLocked = true
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                    task.delay(0.1, function()
                        if char and root then
                            local jumpBoost = getgenv().BackJumpSpeed * getgenv().BackRampMultiplier * 0.8
                            pcall(function()
                                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X,
                                    jumpBoost,
                                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z
                                )
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                else
                    if not backRampLocked then
                        humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                    end
                end
                backLastJumpTime = tick()
            end
        end
    end
end
function applyBackJump()
    if not Config.EnableBackJump then
        if backActiveBV then pcall(function() backActiveBV:Destroy() end) end
        backActiveBV = nil
        return
    end
    local deltaTime = tick() - backLastTick
    backLastTick = tick()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if char then
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if root and humanoid then
            local isAir = humanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
            local humanoidState = humanoid:GetState()
            local isOnGround = (humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed or humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) and not isAir
            if backWasAir and isOnGround then
                backCurrentSpeed = getgenv().BackJumpSpeed
                backAirAccumulator = 0
                backRampLocked = false
            end
            backWasAir = isAir
            local lookDir = camera.CFrame.LookVector
            local backDir = Vector3.new(-lookDir.X, 0, -lookDir.Z)
            if backDir.Magnitude ~= 0 then
                backDir = backDir.Unit
            end
            if isAir then
                if not backActiveBV or not backActiveBV.Parent or backActiveBV.Parent ~= root then
                    if backActiveBV then pcall(function() backActiveBV:Destroy() end) end
                    backActiveBV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                    backActiveBV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(4e5, 0, 4e5)
                    backActiveBV.P = 1250
                    backActiveBV.Parent = root
                end
                backActiveBV.Velocity = backDir * backCurrentSpeed
            else
                backAirAccumulator = backAirAccumulator + deltaTime
                while backAirAccumulator >= 0.04 do
                    backAirAccumulator = backAirAccumulator - 0.04
                    backCurrentSpeed = math.min(getgenv().BackJumpSpeed + 10, backCurrentSpeed + 0.3)
                end
                local rayOriginBack = root.Position
                local rayDirectionBack = backDir * 8
                _rpBack.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
                local raycastResultBack = Workspace:Raycast(rayOriginBack, rayDirectionBack, _rpBack)
                local impulsePower = backCurrentSpeed
                if raycastResultBack then
                    impulsePower = backCurrentSpeed * getgenv().BackRampMultiplier
                end
                if not backActiveBV or not backActiveBV.Parent or backActiveBV.Parent ~= root then
                    if backActiveBV then pcall(function() backActiveBV:Destroy() end) end
                    backActiveBV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                    backActiveBV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(4e5, 0, 4e5)
                    backActiveBV.P = 1250
                    backActiveBV.Parent = root
                end
                backActiveBV.Velocity = backDir * impulsePower
                backCurrentSpeed = math.max(getgenv().BackJumpSpeed, backCurrentSpeed - 2.5 * deltaTime)
                if tick() - backLastJumpTime >= backJumpInterval then
                    ActivateBackJump()
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
print("✓ MOVEMENT: Functions loaded successfully")
print("✓ FULLBRIGHT: Function loaded successfully")
local AutoTicketContexts = nil
function FindAutoTicketContexts()
    if AutoTicketContexts then return AutoTicketContexts end
    local ok, gcTable = pcall(getgc, true)
    if not ok or not gcTable then return nil end
    for i = 1, #gcTable do
        local v = gcTable[i]
        if type(v) == "table" then
            local ok2, first = pcall(function() return v[1] end)
            if ok2 and type(first) == "table" then
                local ok3, hasIds = pcall(rawget, first, "CollectableIDs")
                if ok3 and hasIds then
                    AutoTicketContexts = v
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return AutoTicketContexts
end
function GetServerPing()
    local ok, ping = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue() / 1000
    end)
    return (ok and ping) or 0.1
end
function SmoothTp(part, targetCFrame, duration)
    if not part or not part.Parent then return end
    duration = duration or 0.3
    local startCFrame = part.CFrame
    local startTime = tick()
    while tick() - startTime < duration do
        if not Config.EnableAutoTicket then return end
        if not part or not part.Parent then return end
        local alpha = math.clamp((tick() - startTime) / duration, 0, 1)
        pcall(function() part.CFrame = startCFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, alpha) end)
        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
    if part and part.Parent then
        pcall(function() part.CFrame = targetCFrame end)
    end
end
function applyAutoTicket()
    local contexts = FindAutoTicketContexts()
    if not contexts then return end
    local duration = 0.5 + math.clamp(GetServerPing(), 0, 0.5)
    for i = 1, #contexts do
        if not Config.EnableAutoTicket then return end
        local ctx = contexts[i]
        if type(ctx) == "table" and ctx.CollectableIDs then
            for _, collectable in pairs(ctx.CollectableIDs) do
                if not Config.EnableAutoTicket then return end
                local ok, position = pcall(function()
                    return collectable.CollectableData.Position
                end)
                if ok and position then
                    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if root then
                        SmoothTp(root, CFrame.new(position), duration)
                        task.wait(2.5)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
local autoTicketLoopStarted = false
function StartAutoTicketLoop()
    if autoTicketLoopStarted then return end
    autoTicketLoopStarted = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            if Config.EnableAutoTicket then
                pcall(applyAutoTicket)
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end
StartAutoTicketLoop()
print("✓ AUTO TICKET: Function loaded successfully")
local VOID_Y_THRESHOLD = -350
function applyVoidEscape()
    if not Config.EnableVoidEscape then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end
    if root.Position.Y > VOID_Y_THRESHOLD then return end
    pcall(function()
        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 9e9, 0)
    end)
end
print("✓ VOID ESCAPE: Function loaded successfully")
local CharServiceModule = nil
local InteractRemote = nil
function FindCharService()
    if CharServiceModule then return CharServiceModule end
    local ok, gcTable = pcall(getgc, true)
    if not ok or not gcTable then return nil end
    for i = 1, #gcTable do
        local v = gcTable[i]
        if type(v) == "table" then
            local ok2, hasGetCharacters = pcall(rawget, v, "GetCharacters")
            if ok2 and hasGetCharacters then
                CharServiceModule = v
                break
            end
        end
    end
    return CharServiceModule
end
function GetInteractRemote()
    if InteractRemote then return InteractRemote end
    local ok, remote = pcall(function()
        return ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Events", 10):WaitForChild("Interact", 5)
    end)
    if ok and remote then
        InteractRemote = remote
    end
    return InteractRemote
end
function applyReviveAura()
    if not Config.EnableReviveAura then return end
    local charService = FindCharService()
    local interact = GetInteractRemote()
    if not charService or not interact then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myRoot = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return end
    local ok, chars = pcall(function() return charService:GetCharacters() end)
    if not ok or not chars then return end
    for i = 1, #chars do
        if not Config.EnableReviveAura then return end
        local v = chars[i]
        if type(v) == "table" and v.Done and not v.Local then
            local okState, isDowned = pcall(function() return v.State.State == "Downed" end)
            if okState and isDowned and v.PrimaryPart then
                local okDist, distance = pcall(function()
                    return (v.PrimaryPart.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                end)
                if okDist and distance < 30 then
                    pcall(function()
                        interact:FireServer("Revive", v.Tag, true)
                    end)
                    local reviveLength = 1.5
                    pcall(function()
                        reviveLength = v.DataRegistry:Get("ReviveLength") or 1.5
                    end)
                    task.wait(reviveLength)
                    local stillOk, stillDowned = pcall(function()
                        return v.State.State == "Downed" and v.PrimaryPart ~= nil
                    end)
                    if Config.EnableReviveAura and stillOk and stillDowned then
                        local okDist2, distance2 = pcall(function()
                            return (v.PrimaryPart.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                        end)
                        if okDist2 and distance2 < 30 then
                            pcall(function()
                                interact:FireServer("Revive", v.Tag)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
local reviveAuraLoopStarted = false
function StartReviveAuraLoop()
    if reviveAuraLoopStarted then return end
    reviveAuraLoopStarted = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            if Config.EnableReviveAura then
                pcall(applyReviveAura)
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end
StartReviveAuraLoop()
print("OK REVIVE AURA: Function loaded successfully")

local SetPlayerModeRemote = nil
local autoSpawnLastFired  = 0
local AUTO_SPAWN_COOLDOWN = 3
local function GetSetPlayerModeRemote()
    if SetPlayerModeRemote then return SetPlayerModeRemote end
    local ok, remote = pcall(function()
        return ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Events", 10):WaitForChild("SetPlayerMode", 5)
    end)
    if ok and remote then
        SetPlayerModeRemote = remote
    end
    return SetPlayerModeRemote
end
local function applyAutoSpawn()
    if not Config.EnableAutoForceSpawn then return end
    local now = tick()
    if now - autoSpawnLastFired < AUTO_SPAWN_COOLDOWN then return end
    local charService = FindCharService()
    if not charService then return end
    local ok, chars = pcall(function() return charService:GetCharacters() end)
    if not ok or not chars then return end
    for i = 1, #chars do
        local v = chars[i]
        if type(v) == "table" and v.Done and v.Local then
            local okState, isDowned = pcall(function()
                return v.State and v.State.State == "Downed"
            end)
            if okState and isDowned then
                autoSpawnLastFired = now
                local remote = GetSetPlayerModeRemote()
                if remote then
                    pcall(function() remote:FireServer(true) end)
                    print("✓ [AutoSpawn] Downed state detected — Force Spawn fired")
                else
                    warn("[AutoSpawn] SetPlayerMode remote not found")
                end
            end
            break
        end
    end
end
local autoSpawnLoopStarted = false
local function StartAutoSpawnLoop()
    if autoSpawnLoopStarted then return end
    autoSpawnLoopStarted = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            if Config.EnableAutoForceSpawn then
                pcall(applyAutoSpawn)
            end
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
    end)
end
StartAutoSpawnLoop()

do -- bloque de locales: libera 8 variables locales del script principal
local antiBotLastFired = 0
local function antiBotFindGroundY(position, excludeInstance)
    _rpAntiBot.FilterDescendantsInstances = {excludeInstance}
    local origin = position + Vector3.new(0, 50, 0)
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        return Workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -1000, 0), _rpAntiBot)
    end)
    if ok and result then
        return result.Position.Y
    end
    return nil
end
local function antiBotGetOtherPlayers(chars)
    local list = {}
    for i = 1, #chars do
        local v = chars[i]
        if type(v) == "table" and v.Done and not v.Local and v.PlayerReference and v.PrimaryPart then
            list[#list + 1] = v
        end
    end
    return list
end
local function applyAntiBot()
    if not Config.EnableAntiBot then return end
    local now = tick()
    if now - antiBotLastFired < Config.AntiBotCooldown then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myRoot = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return end
    local charService = FindCharService()
    if not charService then return end
    local ok, chars = pcall(function() return charService:GetCharacters() end)
    if not ok or not chars then return end
    local botDistance = nil
    for i = 1, #chars do
        local v = chars[i]
        if type(v) == "table" and v.Done and v.AI == true and v.PrimaryPart then
            local okDist, distance = pcall(function()
                return (v.PrimaryPart.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
            end)
            if okDist and distance < Config.AntiBotDistance then
                botDistance = distance
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if not botDistance then return end
    local function tryPlayer()
        local others = antiBotGetOtherPlayers(chars)
        if #others == 0 then return false end
        local target = others[math.random(1, #others)]
        local okPos, pos = pcall(function() return target.PrimaryPart.Position end)
        if not okPos then return false end
        pcall(function()
            myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(math.random(-6, 6), 3, math.random(-6, 6)))
        end)
        return true
    end
    local function tryNearby()
        for attempt = 1, 5 do
            local angle  = math.random() * math.pi * 2
            local radius = math.random(20, 40)
            local candidate = myRoot.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * radius, 0, math.sin(angle) * radius)
            local groundY = antiBotFindGroundY(candidate, char)
            if groundY then
                pcall(function()
                    myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(candidate.X, groundY + 3, candidate.Z)
                end)
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local preferPlayer = math.random() < 0.5
    local teleported
    if preferPlayer then
        teleported = tryPlayer() or tryNearby()
    else
        teleported = tryNearby() or tryPlayer()
    end
    if teleported then
        antiBotLastFired = now
        print("✓ [AntiBot] Bot a " .. string.format("%.1f", botDistance) .. " studs — teletransportado")
    end
end
local antiBotLoopStarted = false
local antiBotAccumulator = 0
local ANTIBOT_REFRESH = 0.25
local function StartAntiBotLoop()
    if antiBotLoopStarted then return end
    antiBotLoopStarted = true
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not Config.EnableAntiBot then return end
        antiBotAccumulator = antiBotAccumulator + dt
        if antiBotAccumulator < ANTIBOT_REFRESH then return end
        antiBotAccumulator = 0
        pcall(applyAntiBot)
    end)
end
StartAntiBotLoop()
end -- fin del bloque de locales
print("OK ANTI BOT: Function loaded successfully")
do -- bloque de locales: libera 9 variables locales del script principal
local espDownEntries      = {}
local espDownAccumulator  = 0
local espDownConnection   = nil
local ESP_DOWN_COLOR      = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 0)
local ESP_DOWN_REFRESH    = 0.15
local function espDownClearEntry(tag)
    local e = espDownEntries[tag]
    if not e then return end
    pcall(function() if e.highlight then e.highlight:Destroy() end end)
    pcall(function() if e.billboard then e.billboard:Destroy() end end)
    espDownEntries[tag] = nil
end
local function espDownClearAll()
    for tag in pairs(espDownEntries) do
        espDownClearEntry(tag)
    end
end
local function espDownCreateEntry(tag, primaryPart, displayName)
    espDownClearEntry(tag)
    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.Name = "__EspDown_Highlight"
    highlight.Adornee = primaryPart.Parent or primaryPart
    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    highlight.FillColor = ESP_DOWN_COLOR
    highlight.OutlineColor = ESP_DOWN_COLOR
    highlight.FillTransparency = 0.5
    highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
    highlight.Enabled = true
    highlight.Parent = primaryPart
    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "__EspDown_Billboard"
    billboard.Adornee = primaryPart
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.LightInfluence = 0
    pcall(function() billboard.MaxDistance = math.huge end)
    billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 36)
    billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
    billboard.Enabled = true
    billboard.Parent = primaryPart
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = "Info"
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.TextColor3 = ESP_DOWN_COLOR
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    label.TextSize = 17
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    label.Text = (displayName or "?") .. " [DOWN]"
    label.Parent = billboard
    espDownEntries[tag] = { highlight = highlight, billboard = billboard, label = label, displayName = displayName or "?" }
end
local function espDownRefresh()
    if not Config.EnableEspDown then return end
    local charService = FindCharService()
    if not charService then return end
    local ok, chars = pcall(function() return charService:GetCharacters() end)
    if not ok or not chars then return end
    local downedNow = {}
    for i = 1, #chars do
        local v = chars[i]
        if type(v) == "table" and v.Done and not v.Local and v.Tag and v.PrimaryPart then
            local okState, isDowned = pcall(function()
                return v.State and v.State.State == "Downed"
            end)
            if okState and isDowned then
                downedNow[v.Tag] = v
            end
        end
    end
    for tag in pairs(espDownEntries) do
        if not downedNow[tag] then
            espDownClearEntry(tag)
        end
    end
    for tag, v in pairs(downedNow) do
        if not espDownEntries[tag] then
            local displayName = "?"
            pcall(function()
                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p.Character then
                        local attr = p.Character:GetAttribute("Tag")
                        if attr == tag then
                            displayName = p.DisplayName
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            espDownCreateEntry(tag, v.PrimaryPart, displayName)
        else
            local e = espDownEntries[tag]
            if e and e.label then
                pcall(function()
                    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                    local myRoot = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if myRoot and v.PrimaryPart then
                        local dist = math.floor((v.PrimaryPart.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude + 0.5)
                        e.label.Text = e.displayName .. "  |  " .. dist .. " m  [DOWN]"
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end
end
function espDownSetEnabled(state)
    if state then
        if not espDownConnection then
            espDownConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
                espDownAccumulator = espDownAccumulator + dt
                if espDownAccumulator >= ESP_DOWN_REFRESH then
                    espDownAccumulator = 0
                    pcall(espDownRefresh)
                end
            end)
        end
    else
        if espDownConnection then
            espDownConnection:Disconnect()
            espDownConnection = nil
        end
        espDownClearAll()
    end
end
end -- fin del bloque de locales
if Config.EnableEspDown then
    task.defer(function()
        espDownSetEnabled(true)
    end)
end
print("OK ESP DOWN: Function loaded successfully")
do -- bloque de locales: libera 9 variables locales del script principal
local autoCarryLoopStarted = false
local carryLastAttempt = 0
local CarryCharacterService = nil
local carryStates = {
    Carry = true, CarrySlide = true, CarrySlideAir = true,
    CarryAir = true, Carried = true, CarryMove = true, CarryIdle = true,
}
local function getCarryCharacterService()
    if CarryCharacterService then return CarryCharacterService end
    local ok, svc = pcall(function()
        local services = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Services")
        local asset = services and services:FindFirstChild("Asset")
        local module = asset and asset:FindFirstChild("CharacterService")
        return module and require(module) or nil
    end)
    if ok and svc then CarryCharacterService = svc end
    return CarryCharacterService
end
local function carryIsDowned(player)
    if not player.Character then return false end
    local svc = getCarryCharacterService()
    if not svc then return false end
    local charData = svc:GetCharacterFromPlayer(player)
    if charData and charData.DataRegistry and charData.DataRegistry:Get("Downed") then
        return true
    end
    return false
end
local function carryIsRagdolled(player)
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return false end
    if char:FindFirstChild("RagdollConstraints") then return true end
    local svc = getCarryCharacterService()
    local charData = svc and svc:GetCharacterFromPlayer(player)
    if charData and charData.DataRegistry and charData.DataRegistry:Get("Ragdolling") then
        return true
    end
    if char.PrimaryPart and char.PrimaryPart.Anchored then return true end
    return false
end
local function applyAutoCarry()
    if not Config.EnableAutoCarry then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end
    local svc = getCarryCharacterService()
    if not svc then return end
    local charData = svc:GetCharacterFromPlayer(LocalPlayer)
    local movement = charData and charData.Movement
    if not movement then return end
    if carryStates[movement.State] then return end
    local interact = GetInteractRemote()
    if not interact then return end
    local cooldown = (type(Config.CarryInterval) == "number" and Config.CarryInterval > 0)
        and Config.CarryInterval or 0.2
    local now = tick()
    if now - carryLastAttempt < cooldown then return end
    local range = (type(Config.CarryRange) == "number" and Config.CarryRange > 0)
        and Config.CarryRange or 30
    for _, other in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if other ~= LocalPlayer then
            local otherChar = other.Character
            if otherChar and carryIsDowned(other) and not carryIsRagdolled(other) then
                local otherHRP = otherChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if otherHRP and (hrp.Position - otherHRP.Position).Magnitude <= range then
                    local targetTag = otherChar:GetAttribute("Tag")
                    if targetTag then
                        pcall(function()
                            interact:FireServer("Carry", targetTag)
                        end)
                        carryLastAttempt = now
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
local function StartAutoCarryLoop()
    if autoCarryLoopStarted then return end
    autoCarryLoopStarted = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            if Config.EnableAutoCarry then
                pcall(applyAutoCarry)
            end
            local interval = (type(Config.CarryInterval) == "number" and Config.CarryInterval > 0)
                and Config.CarryInterval or 0.2
            task.wait(interval)
        end
    end)
end
StartAutoCarryLoop()
end -- fin del bloque de locales
print("OK AUTO CARRY: Function loaded successfully")
do -- bloque de locales: libera 4 variables locales del script principal
local autoSaveLoopStarted = false
local autoSaveBusy        = false
local function applyAutoSave()
    if not Config.EnableAutoSave then return end
    if autoSaveBusy then return end
    local charService = FindCharService()
    local interact    = GetInteractRemote()
    if not charService or not interact then return end
    local char   = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myRoot = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return end
    local ok, chars = pcall(function() return charService:GetCharacters() end)
    if not ok or not chars then return end
    for i = 1, #chars do
        if not Config.EnableAutoSave then return end
        local v = chars[i]
        if type(v) == "table" and v.Done and not v.Local then
            local okState, isDowned = pcall(function()
                return v.State and v.State.State == "Downed"
            end)
            if okState and isDowned and v.PrimaryPart then
                autoSaveBusy = true
                local savedTag = v.Tag
                pcall(function()
                    local targetPos = v.PrimaryPart.Position
                    myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    interact:FireServer("Revive", savedTag, true)
                    local reviveLength = 1.5
                    pcall(function()
                        reviveLength = v.DataRegistry:Get("ReviveLength") or 1.5
                    end)
                    task.wait(reviveLength)
                    local stillOk, stillDowned = pcall(function()
                        return v.State.State == "Downed" and v.PrimaryPart ~= nil
                    end)
                    if Config.EnableAutoSave and stillOk and stillDowned then
                        interact:FireServer("Revive", savedTag)
                    end
                end)
                autoSaveBusy = false
                break
            end
        end
    end
end
local function StartAutoSaveLoop()
    if autoSaveLoopStarted then return end
    autoSaveLoopStarted = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            if Config.EnableAutoSave then
                pcall(applyAutoSave)
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end
StartAutoSaveLoop()
end -- fin del bloque de locales
print("OK AUTO SAVE: Function loaded successfully")
do -- bloque de locales: libera 5 variables locales del script principal
local figure8T            = 0
local figure8CenterCF     = nil
local figure8Conn         = nil
local figure8PrevAnchor   = false
local figure8PrevWalkSpd  = 16
function StopFigure8Loop()
    if figure8Conn then
        figure8Conn:Disconnect()
        figure8Conn = nil
    end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if char then
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = figure8PrevWalkSpd end
    end
    figure8CenterCF = nil
    print("X Figure-8 Loop STOPPED")
end
function StartFigure8Loop()
    StopFigure8Loop()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then
        warn("[Figure-8] No character found")
        return
    end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then
        warn("[Figure-8] HumanoidRootPart or Humanoid not found")
        return
    end
    figure8PrevAnchor  = hrp.Anchored
    figure8PrevWalkSpd = hum.WalkSpeed
    hum.WalkSpeed  = 0
    figure8T       = 0
    local lookVec = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
    figure8CenterCF = CFrame.new(hrp.Position)
        * CFrame.Angles(0, math.atan2(lookVec.X, lookVec.Z), 0)
    print("✓ Figure-8 Loop STARTED at " .. tostring(hrp.Position))
    figure8Conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not Config.EnableFigure8 then return end
        local char2 = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char2 then return end
        local hrp2 = char2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp2 then return end
        figure8T = figure8T + Config.Figure8Speed * dt
        local R  = Config.Figure8Radius
        local t  = figure8T
        local localX =  R       * math.cos(t)
        local localZ = (R * 0.5)* math.sin(2 * t)
        local tanX   = -R       * math.sin(t)
        local tanZ   =  R       * math.cos(2 * t)
        local right   = figure8CenterCF.RightVector
        local forward = figure8CenterCF.LookVector
        local origin  = figure8CenterCF.Position
        local worldPos = origin + right * localX + forward * localZ
        worldPos = Vector3.new(worldPos.X, origin.Y, worldPos.Z)
        local worldTan = right * tanX + forward * tanZ
        local tanMag   = worldTan.Magnitude
        local newCF
        if tanMag > 0.01 then
            newCF = CFrame.new(worldPos, worldPos + worldTan)
        else
            newCF = CFrame.new(worldPos, worldPos + forward)
        end
        hrp2.CFrame = newCF
        hrp2.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
        hrp2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
end
end -- fin del bloque de locales
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    if Config.EnableFigure8 then
        Config.EnableFigure8 = false
        StopFigure8Loop()
        print("[Figure-8] Character respawned — loop deactivated automatically")
    end
end)
print("OK FIGURE-8 LOOP: Function loaded successfully")
