local lissajousT            = 0
local lissajousCenterCF     = nil
local lissajousConn         = nil
local lissajousPrevAnchor   = false
local lissajousPrevWalkSpd  = 16
function StopLissajousLoop()
    if lissajousConn then
        lissajousConn:Disconnect()
        lissajousConn = nil
    end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if char then
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = lissajousPrevWalkSpd end
    end
    lissajousCenterCF = nil
    print("X Lissajous Loop STOPPED")
end
function StartLissajousLoop()
    StopLissajousLoop()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then
        warn("[Lissajous] No character found")
        return
    end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then
        warn("[Lissajous] HumanoidRootPart or Humanoid not found")
        return
    end
    lissajousPrevAnchor  = hrp.Anchored
    lissajousPrevWalkSpd = hum.WalkSpeed
    hum.WalkSpeed = 0
    lissajousT    = 0
    local lookVec = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
    lissajousCenterCF = CFrame.new(hrp.Position)
        * CFrame.Angles(0, math.atan2(lookVec.X, lookVec.Z), 0)
    print("✓ Lissajous Loop STARTED at " .. tostring(hrp.Position))
    lissajousConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not Config.EnableLissajous then return end
        local char2 = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char2 then return end
        local hrp2 = char2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp2 then return end
        lissajousT = lissajousT + Config.LissajousSpeed * dt
        local R  = Config.LissajousRadius
        local t  = lissajousT
        local ph = math.pi / 4
        local localX = R * math.sin(3 * t)
        local localZ = R * math.sin(2 * t + ph)
        local tanX   = 3 * R * math.cos(3 * t)
        local tanZ   = 2 * R * math.cos(2 * t + ph)
        local right   = lissajousCenterCF.RightVector
        local forward = lissajousCenterCF.LookVector
        local origin  = lissajousCenterCF.Position
        local worldPos = origin + right * localX + forward * localZ
        worldPos = Vector3.new(worldPos.X, origin.Y, worldPos.Z)
        local worldTan = right * tanX + forward * tanZ
        local tanMag   = worldTan.Magnitude
        local newCF
        if tanMag > 0.01 then
            newCF = CFrame.new(worldPos, worldPos + worldTan)
        else
            newCF = CFrame.new(worldPos, worldPos + forward)
        end
        hrp2.CFrame = newCF
        hrp2.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
        hrp2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
end
end -- fin del bloque de locales
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    if Config.EnableLissajous then
        Config.EnableLissajous = false
        StopLissajousLoop()
        print("[Lissajous] Character respawned — loop deactivated automatically")
    end
end)
print("OK LISSAJOUS LOOP: Function loaded successfully")
do -- bloque de locales: libera 5 variables locales del script principal
local spiroT            = 0
local spiroCenterCF     = nil
local spiroConn         = nil
local spiroPrevAnchor   = false
local spiroPrevWalkSpd  = 16
function StopSpiroLoop()
    if spiroConn then
        spiroConn:Disconnect()
        spiroConn = nil
    end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if char then
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = spiroPrevWalkSpd end
    end
    spiroCenterCF = nil
    print("X Spirograph Loop STOPPED")
end
function StartSpiroLoop()
    StopSpiroLoop()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then
        warn("[Spirograph] No character found")
        return
    end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then
        warn("[Spirograph] HumanoidRootPart or Humanoid not found")
        return
    end
    spiroPrevAnchor  = hrp.Anchored
    spiroPrevWalkSpd = hum.WalkSpeed
    hum.WalkSpeed = 0
    spiroT        = 0
    local lookVec = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
    spiroCenterCF = CFrame.new(hrp.Position)
        * CFrame.Angles(0, math.atan2(lookVec.X, lookVec.Z), 0)
    print("✓ Spirograph Loop STARTED at " .. tostring(hrp.Position))
    spiroConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not Config.EnableSpiro then return end
        local char2 = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char2 then return end
        local hrp2 = char2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp2 then return end
        spiroT = spiroT + Config.SpiroSpeed * dt
        local R  = Config.SpiroRadius
        local r  = R * Config.SpiroRatio
        local Rr = R - r
        local k  = Rr / r
        local t  = spiroT
        local localX = Rr * math.cos(t) + r * math.cos(k * t)
        local localZ = Rr * math.sin(t) - r * math.sin(k * t)
        local tanX   = -Rr * math.sin(t) - r * k * math.sin(k * t)
        local tanZ   =  Rr * math.cos(t) - r * k * math.cos(k * t)
        local right   = spiroCenterCF.RightVector
        local forward = spiroCenterCF.LookVector
        local origin  = spiroCenterCF.Position
        local worldPos = origin + right * localX + forward * localZ
        worldPos = Vector3.new(worldPos.X, origin.Y, worldPos.Z)
        local worldTan = right * tanX + forward * tanZ
        local tanMag   = worldTan.Magnitude
        local newCF
        if tanMag > 0.01 then
            newCF = CFrame.new(worldPos, worldPos + worldTan)
        else
            newCF = CFrame.new(worldPos, worldPos + forward)
        end
        hrp2.CFrame = newCF
        hrp2.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
        hrp2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
end
end -- fin del bloque de locales
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    if Config.EnableSpiro then
        Config.EnableSpiro = false
        StopSpiroLoop()
        print("[Spirograph] Character respawned — loop deactivated automatically")
    end
end)
print("OK SPIROGRAPH LOOP: Function loaded successfully")
local MovementStatModule = nil
local originalSpeedChange = nil
local speedMultiplierHooked = false
local inEmote = false
getgenv().YYSpeedApplyRequested = Config.EnableSpeedMultiplier == true
function FindMovementStat()
    if MovementStatModule then return MovementStatModule end
    local ok, gcTable = pcall(getgc, true)
    if not ok or not gcTable then return nil end
    for i = 1, #gcTable do
        local v = gcTable[i]
        if type(v) == "table" then
            local ok2, isValid = pcall(function()
                return rawget(v, "SpeedChange") and type(v.SpeedChange) == "function"
            end)
            if ok2 and isValid then
                MovementStatModule = v
                break
            end
        end
    end
    return MovementStatModule
end
function SetupSpeedMultiplierHook()
    if speedMultiplierHooked then return end
    if not hookfunction then return end
    local movementStat = FindMovementStat()
    if not movementStat then return end
    inEmote = false
    local ok = pcall(function()
        originalSpeedChange = hookfunction(movementStat.SpeedChange, function(self, state, value)
            if state == "Emote" then
                if value ~= nil then
                    inEmote = true
                    inEvadeEmote = true
                    if Config.EnableEmoteSpeed then
                        return originalSpeedChange(self, state, Config.EmoteSpeedMultiplier)
                    end
                    return originalSpeedChange(self, state, value)
                else
                    inEmote = false
                    inEvadeEmote = false
                    task.spawn(cleanupActiveEmote)
                    return originalSpeedChange(self, state, value)
                end
            end
            if state == "State" then
                lastSpeedSelf, lastSpeedState, lastSpeedValue = self, state, value
                if getgenv().YYSpeedRestoreRequested and not Config.EnableSpeedMultiplier then
                    task.defer(function()
                        if getgenv().YYRestoreSpeedMultiplier then
                            getgenv().YYRestoreSpeedMultiplier()
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
            if (state ~= "State" and state ~= "Emote") or not Config.EnableSpeedMultiplier then
                return originalSpeedChange(self, state, value)
            end
            return originalSpeedChange(self, state, Config.SpeedMultiplierValue)
        end)
    end)
    if ok then
        speedMultiplierHooked = true
        print("✓ SPEED MULTIPLIER: Hook conectado")
        if Config.EnableSpeedMultiplier then
            getgenv().YYSpeedApplyRequested = true
            task.defer(function()
                if getgenv().YYApplySpeedMultiplier then getgenv().YYApplySpeedMultiplier() end
            end)
        end
        if getgenv().YYSpeedRestoreRequested and not Config.EnableSpeedMultiplier then
            task.defer(function()
                if getgenv().YYRestoreSpeedMultiplier then
                    getgenv().YYRestoreSpeedMultiplier()
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end
getgenv().YYApplySpeedMultiplier = function()
    if not Config.EnableSpeedMultiplier or not originalSpeedChange then return false end
    local target = lastSpeedSelf or MovementStatModule
    if not target then return false end
    local ok = pcall(originalSpeedChange, target, "State", Config.SpeedMultiplierValue)
    if ok then getgenv().YYSpeedApplyRequested = false end
    return ok
end
getgenv().YYRestoreSpeedMultiplier = function()
    if not originalSpeedChange or not lastSpeedSelf or lastSpeedState ~= "State" then
        return false
    end
    if lastSpeedValue == nil then
        return false
    end
    local ok = pcall(originalSpeedChange, lastSpeedSelf, lastSpeedState, lastSpeedValue)
    if ok then
        getgenv().YYSpeedRestoreRequested = false
        lastSpeedSelf, lastSpeedState, lastSpeedValue = nil, nil, nil
    end
    return ok
end
local speedHookDiagLabel = TabEmote:CreateLabel(
    "Speed Hook: esperando...",
    "Speed Hook: waiting...",
    11
)
local function TryHookSpeedMultiplier()
    if speedMultiplierHooked then return end
    pcall(SetupSpeedMultiplierHook)
    if speedHookDiagLabel then
        if speedMultiplierHooked then
            speedHookDiagLabel.Text = scriptText("✓ Speed Hook: logrado", "✓ Speed Hook: hooked")
        else
            speedHookDiagLabel.Text = scriptText("X Speed Hook: fallo, reintentará en el próximo respawn", "X Speed Hook: failed, will retry on next respawn")
        end
    end
end
TryHookSpeedMultiplier()
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    if speedMultiplierHooked then return end
    task.defer(TryHookSpeedMultiplier)
end)
task.spawn(function()
    local updateMapBound = false
    local stateBound = false
    for _ = 1, 90 do
        if speedMultiplierHooked then break end
        local events = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Events")
        local updateMap = events and events:FindFirstChild("UpdateMap")
        if not updateMapBound and updateMap and updateMap:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            updateMapBound = true
            updateMap.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                if speedMultiplierHooked then return end
                task.defer(TryHookSpeedMultiplier)
            end)
        end
        local services = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Services")
        local data = services and services:FindFirstChild("Data")
        local registry = data and data:FindFirstChild("ServerStateRegistryService")
        local changedEvent = registry and registry:FindFirstChild("ChangedEvent")
        if not stateBound and changedEvent and changedEvent:IsA("BindableEvent") then
            stateBound = true
            changedEvent.Event:Connect(function(key, value)
                if speedMultiplierHooked then return end
                if (key == "MapLoading" and value == true)
                or (key == "Voting" and value == true) then
                    task.defer(TryHookSpeedMultiplier)
                end
            end)
        end
        if updateMapBound and stateBound then break end
        task.wait(1)
    end
end)
print("✓ SPEED MULTIPLIER: Function loaded successfully")
local trimpWasAir = false
function applyAutoTrimp()
    if not Config.EnableAutoTrimp and not Config.EnableTrimpTeleport then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    if not root or not humanoid then return end
    if humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
    local isAir = humanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
    pcall(function()
        if isAir then
            local vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            if vel.Y < 0 then
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                    vel.X,
                    vel.Y * Config.AutoTrimpFallSpeed,
                    vel.Z
                )
            end
        else
            if trimpWasAir then
                local vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                local horizontalSpeed = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z).Magnitude
                local impulse = math.max(horizontalSpeed, 20) * Config.AutoTrimpMultiplier
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                    vel.X,
                    impulse,
                    vel.Z
                )
            end
        end
    end)
    trimpWasAir = isAir
end
print("OK AUTO TRIMP: Function loaded successfully")
print("\nActivating main loop...")
local function mainLoopTick()
    applyTeleportWalk()
    applyEnhancedJump()
    applyAutoJump()
    applyAutoCrouch()
    applyImpulseJump()
    applyGravityModification()
    applyFrontalJump()
    applyRampMultiplier()
    applyBackJump()
    applyVoidEscape()
    applyAutoTrimp()
end
local connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    pcall(mainLoopTick)
end)
print("✓ LOOP: Main loop started")

function setupAllTab()
local TabAll = UI:CreateTab("All", "All", "rbxassetid://125111822154519")

local AllCount
local AllCarousel = nil

    Catalog = nil,
    Cosmetics = nil,
    Skins = nil,
}
local AllActivePage = nil
local AllCarouselMode = nil
local AllCarouselAnimating = false
local AllCarouselHeightUpdating = false
local AllCarouselSwipe = {
    input = nil,
    start = nil,
    threshold = 36,
}
local function allTrackInstance(inst)
    if type(inst) == "table" then
        inst = inst.Holder
    end
    return inst
end
local function allEnsureCarousel()
    if AllCarousel and AllCarousel.Parent then
        return AllCarousel
    end
    AllCarousel = Instance.new("Frame")
    AllCarousel.Name = "AllCarousel"
    AllCarousel.Parent = TabAll.Page
    AllCarousel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 430)
    AllCarousel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    AllCarousel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    AllCarousel.ClipsDescendants = true
    AllCarousel.LayoutOrder = -900
    AllCarousel.ZIndex = 8
    return AllCarousel
end
local function allUpdateCarouselHeight(page)
    if AllCarouselHeightUpdating then return end
    if not page or not page.Parent then return end
    AllCarouselHeightUpdating = true
    task.defer(function()
        if page and page.Parent then
            local layout = page:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
            if layout then
                local contentHeight = layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 8
                local h = math.max(430, contentHeight)
                if page.Size.Y.Offset ~= h then
                    page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h)
                end
                if AllActivePage == page and AllCarousel and AllCarousel.Parent
                    and AllCarousel.Size.Y.Offset ~= h then
                    AllCarousel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h)
                end
            end
        end
        AllCarouselHeightUpdating = false
    end)
end
local function allMakeCarouselPage(name)
    local viewport = allEnsureCarousel()
    local page = Instance.new("Frame")
    page.Name = name or "AllCarouselContent"
    page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 430)
    page.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    page.BorderSizePixel = 0
    page.ZIndex = 9
    page.ClipsDescendants = false
    page.Parent = viewport
    local pageLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    pageLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pageLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    pageLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
    pageLayout.Parent = page
    pageLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
        allUpdateCarouselHeight(page)
    end)
    task.defer(function()
        allUpdateCarouselHeight(page)
    end)
    return page
end
local function allSetCarouselMode(mode, direction)
    if AllCarouselAnimating then return end
    local newPage = AllCarouselPages[mode]
    if not newPage or not newPage.Parent then return end
    local oldPage = AllActivePage
    if oldPage == newPage and AllCarouselMode == mode then
        return
    end
    AllCarouselMode = mode
    allUpdateCarouselHeight(newPage)
    if not oldPage or not oldPage.Parent then
        newPage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        AllActivePage = newPage
        return
    end
    local incomingStart = direction and direction > 0 and 1 or -1
    local outgoingEnd = direction and direction > 0 and -1 or 1
    newPage.Position = UDim2.new(incomingStart, 0, 0, 0)
    AllCarouselAnimating = true
    KeyTweenService:Create(oldPage, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Position = UDim2.new(outgoingEnd, 0, 0, 0)
    }):Play()
    local tween = KeyTweenService:Create(newPage, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    })
    tween:Play()
    AllActivePage = newPage
    tween.Completed:Connect(function()
        AllCarouselAnimating = false
    end)
end

function allLower(value)
    return string.lower(tostring(value or ""))
end

function allIsExcludedName(name)
    return AllExcludedNames[allLower(name)] == true
end
function allPathOf(instance)
    local parts = {}
    local current = instance
    while current and current ~= game do
        parts[#parts + 1] = current.Name
        current = current.Parent
    end
    local result = {}
    for index = #parts, 1, -1 do
        result[#result + 1] = parts[index]
    end
    return table.concat(result, ".")
end
function allIsCosmeticsPath(instance)
    local path = allLower(allPathOf(instance))
    return string.find(path, ".cosmetics", 1, true) ~= nil
        or allLower(instance.Name) == "cosmetics"
end
function allMatchesSearch(candidate)
    if allIsExcludedName(candidate.name) then return false end
    local query = allLower(AllSearch.Text)
    if query == "" then return true end
    return string.find(allLower(candidate.name), query, 1, true) ~= nil
        or string.find(allLower(candidate.path), query, 1, true) ~= nil
end
function allClearRows()
    for _, row in ipairs(AllRows) do
        pcall(function()
            if row and row.Destroy then
                row:Destroy()
            elseif row and row.Holder and row.Holder.Destroy then
                row.Holder:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
    AllRows = {}
end
local bodyNames = {
    ["head"] = true, ["torso"] = true, ["left arm"] = true, ["right arm"] = true,
    ["left leg"] = true, ["right leg"] = true, ["uppertorso"] = true,
    ["lowertorso"] = true, ["lefthand"] = true, ["righthand"] = true,
    ["leftupperarm"] = true, ["rightupperarm"] = true,
    ["leftlowerarm"] = true, ["rightlowerarm"] = true,
    ["leftupperleg"] = true, ["rightupperleg"] = true,
    ["leftlowerleg"] = true, ["rightlowerleg"] = true,
    ["leftfoot"] = true, ["rightfoot"] = true,
    ["humanoidrootpart"] = true, ["torsorot"] = true, ["armrot"] = true,
}
function allIsReferenceBodyPart(instance)
    if not instance:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
    local name = allLower(instance.Name)
    return bodyNames[name]
        or string.find(name, "body", 1, true) ~= nil
        or string.find(name, "limb", 1, true) ~= nil
end
function allPrepareVisualModel(model)
    local hidden = 0
    local parts = 0
    local vfx = 0
    local sounds = 0
    for _, instance in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
        if instance:IsA("BasePart") then
            parts = parts + 1
            instance.CanCollide = false
            instance.CanTouch = false
            instance.CanQuery = false
            instance.Massless = true
            if allIsReferenceBodyPart(instance) then
                instance.Transparency = 1
                hidden = hidden + 1
            end
        elseif instance:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
            or instance:IsA("Beam")
            or instance:IsA("Trail")
            or instance:IsA("Highlight")
            or instance:IsA("PointLight")
            or instance:IsA("SpotLight")
            or instance:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
            vfx = vfx + 1
        elseif instance:IsA("Sound") then
            sounds = sounds + 1
        end
    end
    return hidden, parts, vfx, sounds
end
function allRepairInternalJoints(model)
    local repaired = 0
    for _, joint in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
        if joint:IsA("Motor6D") or joint:IsA("Weld") then
            local p0 = joint.Part0
            local p1 = joint.Part1
            if p0 and not p0:IsDescendantOf(model) then
                local replacement = model:FindFirstChild(p0.Name, true)
                if replacement and replacement:IsA("BasePart") then
                    pcall(function() joint.Part0 = replacement end)
                    repaired = repaired + 1
                end
            end
            if p1 and not p1:IsDescendantOf(model) then
                local replacement = model:FindFirstChild(p1.Name, true)
                if replacement and replacement:IsA("BasePart") then
                    pcall(function() joint.Part1 = replacement end)
                    repaired = repaired + 1
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return repaired
end
function allGetTargetRig()
    local rigs = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Rigs")
    local rig = rigs and rigs:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name)
    if not rig then rig = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name) end
    if not rig then return nil, nil end
    local root = rig:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or rig:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        or rig:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        or rig.PrimaryPart
    return rig, root
end
function allRemoveCurrent()
    if AllCurrentRoot then
        pcall(function() AllCurrentRoot:Destroy() end)
        AllCurrentRoot = nil
    end
    local existing = Workspace:FindFirstChild("__YY_AllVisualCandidates")
    if existing then pcall(function() existing:Destroy() end) end
    allSetStatus("Todos los candidatos fueron retirados")
end
function allTestCandidate(candidate)
    allRemoveCurrent()
    local sourceModule = candidate.module
    if not sourceModule or not sourceModule.Parent then
        allSetStatus(candidate.name .. " ya no está disponible")
        return
    end
    local rig, targetRoot = allGetTargetRig()
    if not rig or not targetRoot then
        allSetStatus("No se encontró el rig visual o su root")
        return
    end
    local characterModel = sourceModule:FindFirstChild("CharacterClassic")
        or sourceModule:FindFirstChild("Character")
    local sourceAura = sourceModule:FindFirstChild("Aura")
    local useModel = candidate.kind == "model" and characterModel and characterModel:IsA("Model")
    local useAura = candidate.kind == "aura"
        and sourceAura
        and (sourceAura:IsA("BasePart") or sourceAura:IsA("Model"))
    if not useModel and not useAura then
        allSetStatus(candidate.name .. " ya no tiene un formato visual compatible")
        return
    end
    local container = Workspace:FindFirstChild("__YY_AllVisualCandidates")
    if not container then
        container = Instance.new("Folder")
        container.Name = "__YY_AllVisualCandidates"
        container.Parent = Workspace
    end
    local ok, clone = pcall(function()
        if useModel then
            return characterModel:Clone()
        end
        if sourceAura:IsA("BasePart") then
            local wrapper = Instance.new("Model")
            local auraClone = sourceAura:Clone()
            auraClone.Parent = wrapper
            wrapper.PrimaryPart = auraClone
            return wrapper
        end
        return sourceAura:Clone()
    end)
    if not ok or not clone then
        allSetStatus("No se pudo clonar " .. candidate.name)
        return
    end
    clone.Name = candidate.name .. (useModel and "_VisualOnly" or "_AuraVisualOnly")
    clone.Parent = container
    local hidden, parts, vfx, sounds = allPrepareVisualModel(clone)
    if useAura then
        local carrier = clone:FindFirstChild("Aura", true)
        if carrier and carrier:IsA("BasePart") then
            carrier.Transparency = 1
        end
    end
    local repaired = allRepairInternalJoints(clone)
    local cloneRoot = clone:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true)
        or clone:FindFirstChild("Torso", true)
        or clone.PrimaryPart
    if not cloneRoot or not cloneRoot:IsA("BasePart") then
        pcall(function() clone:Destroy() end)
        allSetStatus(candidate.name .. " no tiene root part")
        return
    end
    clone.PrimaryPart = cloneRoot
    pcall(function() clone:PivotTo(targetRoot.CFrame) end)
    local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
    weld.Name = "__YY_AllFollowTarget"
    weld.Part0 = cloneRoot
    weld.Part1 = targetRoot
    weld.Parent = cloneRoot
    AllCurrentRoot = container
    allSetStatus(candidate.name .. " activo • cuerpo oculto " .. tostring(hidden) .. "/" .. tostring(parts) .. " • VFX " .. tostring(vfx) .. " • joints " .. tostring(repaired) .. " • sonidos " .. tostring(sounds))
end

function allRebuildRows()
    allClearRows()
    if not AllTabVisible then
        allSetStatus(tostring(#AllCandidates) .. " candidatos descubiertos • abrí la pestaña para verlos")
        return
    end
    local matched = 0
    local shown = 0
    for _, candidate in ipairs(AllCandidates) do
        if allMatchesSearch(candidate) then
            matched = matched + 1
            if shown < ALL_MAX_VISIBLE then
                shown = shown + 1
                AllRows[#AllRows + 1] = allCreateCard(candidate, shown)
            end
        end
    end
    if matched > shown then
        allSetStatus(tostring(shown) .. "/" .. tostring(matched) .. " tarjetas visibles (máx " .. tostring(ALL_MAX_VISIBLE) .. ") • filtrá para encontrar rápido")
    else
        allSetStatus(tostring(shown) .. " tarjetas visibles • " .. tostring(#AllCandidates) .. " candidatos descubiertos")
    end
end
function allDiscoverCandidates()
    if AllDiscoveryRunning then return end
    AllDiscoveryRunning = true
    AllDiscoveryToken = AllDiscoveryToken + 1
    local token = AllDiscoveryToken
    allClearRows()
    AllCandidates = {}
    allSetCount(0)
    allSetStatus("Descubriendo módulos bajo Cosmetics...")
    task.spawn(function()
        local items = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Items")
        local roots = {}
        if items then
            local baseItems = items:FindFirstChild("BaseItems")
            local baseCosmetics = baseItems and baseItems:FindFirstChild("Cosmetics")
            if baseCosmetics then roots[#roots + 1] = baseCosmetics end
            local itemPacks = items:FindFirstChild("ItemPacks")
            if itemPacks then roots[#roots + 1] = itemPacks end
        end
        local queue = {}
        for _, root in ipairs(roots) do queue[#queue + 1] = root end
        local queueIndex = 1
        local visited = {}
        local seenModules = {}
        local scanned = 0
        local limitReached = false
        while queueIndex <= #queue and scanned < AllMaxDiscoveryNodes and token == AllDiscoveryToken do
            local batch = 0
            while batch < AllDiscoveryBatch
                and queueIndex <= #queue
                and scanned < AllMaxDiscoveryNodes
                and token == AllDiscoveryToken do
                local node = queue[queueIndex]
                queueIndex = queueIndex + 1
                batch = batch + 1
                scanned = scanned + 1
                if node and not visited[node] then
                    visited[node] = true
                    if node:IsA("ModuleScript") and allIsCosmeticsPath(node)
                        and not allIsExcludedName(node.Name)
                        and not seenModules[node] then
                        local characterModel = node:FindFirstChild("CharacterClassic")
                            or node:FindFirstChild("Character")
                        local auraObject = node:FindFirstChild("Aura")
                        local candidateKind
                        if characterModel and characterModel:IsA("Model") then
                            candidateKind = "model"
                        elseif auraObject
                            and (auraObject:IsA("BasePart") or auraObject:IsA("Model")) then
                            candidateKind = "aura"
                        end
                        if candidateKind then
                            seenModules[node] = true
                            AllCandidates[#AllCandidates + 1] = {
                                name = node.Name,
                                path = allPathOf(node),
                                module = node,
                                kind = candidateKind,
                            }
                        end
                    end
                    if node:IsA("Folder") then
                        local nodeName = allLower(node.Name)
                        local skipFolder = nodeName == "emotes"
                            or nodeName == "boomboxes"
                            or nodeName == "loadout"
                            or nodeName == "carryanimations"
                        if not skipFolder then
                            for _, child in ipairs(node:GetChildren()) do
                                if child:IsA("Folder") or child:IsA("ModuleScript") then
                                    queue[#queue + 1] = child
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            allSetCount(#AllCandidates)
            allSetStatus("Escaneando " .. tostring(scanned) .. " nodos • " .. tostring(#AllCandidates) .. " candidatos")
            task.wait(AllDiscoveryWait)
        end
        if scanned >= AllMaxDiscoveryNodes and queueIndex <= #queue then
            limitReached = true
        end
        table.sort(AllCandidates, function(a, b)
            local al = allLower(a.name)
            local bl = allLower(b.name)
            if al == bl then return allLower(a.path) < allLower(b.path) end
            return al < bl
        end)
        AllDiscoveryRunning = false
        allSetCount(#AllCandidates)
        if AllTabVisible then
            allRebuildRows()
        end
        if limitReached then
            allSetStatus("Límite alcanzado • " .. tostring(#AllCandidates) .. " candidatos • pulsa REFRESH")
        else
            allSetStatus("Terminado • " .. tostring(#AllCandidates) .. " candidatos • busca o desplázate")
        end
    end)
end
local AllRefresh = TabAll:CreateButton("↻  ACTUALIZAR LISTA", "↻  REFRESH LIST", allDiscoverCandidates)
local AllRemove = TabAll:CreateButton("×  REMOVE ALL", "×  REMOVE ALL", allRemoveCurrent)
AllRefresh.Name = "AllRefresh"
AllRemove.Name = "AllRemove"
TabAll:ResetContentContainer()
local AllCosmeticsPage = allMakeCarouselPage("AllCosmeticsCarouselContent")
AllCarouselPages.Cosmetics = AllCosmeticsPage
TabAll:SetContentContainer(AllCosmeticsPage)
AllDesign.Header(
    AllCosmeticsPage,
    "CAMBIADOR DE COSMÉTICOS", "COSMETICS CHANGER",
    "Escribe el nombre del cosmético actual y el cosmético que quieres usar.",
    "Enter the current cosmetic name and the cosmetic you want to use."
)
local cosCurrentName = ""
local cosNewName = ""
local cosStatusLabel
local cosSwaps = {}
local function cosTrim(value)
    local text = tostring(value or "")
    text = text:gsub("^%s+", "")
    text = text:gsub("%s+$", "")
    return text
end
local function cosSetStatus(text, color)
    AllDesign.SetStatus(cosStatusLabel, text, color)
end
local function cosCreateNameBox(titleSpanish, titleEnglish, placeholderSpanish, placeholderEnglish, setter)
    local input = AllDesign.Field(AllCosmeticsPage, titleSpanish, titleEnglish, placeholderSpanish, placeholderEnglish)
    input.Name = "AllCosmeticNameInput"
    input:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        setter(cosTrim(input.Text))
    end)
    return input
end
cosCreateNameBox(
    "COSMÉTICO ACTUAL", "CURRENT COSMETIC",
    "Nombre exacto (distingue mayúsculas)", "Exact name (case-sensitive)",
    function(value) cosCurrentName = value end
)
cosCreateNameBox(
    "COSMÉTICO NUEVO", "NEW COSMETIC",
    "Nombre exacto (distingue mayúsculas)", "Exact name (case-sensitive)",
    function(value) cosNewName = value end
)
local function cosFindInFolder(folder, name)
    for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == name then return child end
        local result = cosFindInFolder(child, name)
        if result then return result end
    end
    return nil
end
local function cosScanAsync(root, matchFolderName, targetName, callback)
    local stack = {}
    for _, child in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
        table.insert(stack, child)
    end
    local iterations = 0
    local found = nil
    local function step()
        while #stack > 0 do
            local node = table.remove(stack)
            if node:IsA("Folder") or node:IsA("Model") then
                if matchFolderName and node.Name == matchFolderName then
                    local innerResult = cosFindInFolder(node, targetName)
                    if innerResult then
                        found = innerResult
                        break
                    end
                elseif not matchFolderName and node.Name == targetName then
                    found = node
                    break
                else
                    for _, child in ipairs(node:GetChildren()) do
                        table.insert(stack, child)
                    end
                end
            end
            iterations = iterations + 1
            if iterations % 100 == 0 then
                task.wait()
            end
        end
    end
    coroutine.wrap(function()
        step()
        callback(found)
    end)()
end
local function cosSwap(current, target, onComplete)
    if current == "" or target == "" or current == target then
        onComplete(false)
        return
    end
    local key = current .. "_" .. target
    if cosSwaps[key] then
        local old = cosSwaps[key]
        cosSwap(old.target, old.current, function() end)
        cosSwaps[key] = nil
    end
    local items = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Items")
    if not items then
        onComplete(false)
        return
    end
    local curFound, tarFound = nil, nil
    local curDone, tarDone = false, false
    local finished = false
    local function checkDone()
        if finished or not (curDone and tarDone) then return end
        finished = true
        if not (curFound and tarFound) then
            onComplete(false)
            return
        end
        local cur = curFound
        local tar = tarFound
        local temp = Instance.new("Folder")
        temp.Name = "__temp_cos_swap_" .. tostring(tick())
        temp.Parent = ReplicatedStorage or workspace
        local curChildren = {}
        local tarChildren = {}
        for _, child in ipairs(cur:GetChildren()) do table.insert(curChildren, child) end
        for _, child in ipairs(tar:GetChildren()) do table.insert(tarChildren, child) end
        for _, child in ipairs(curChildren) do child.Parent = temp end
        for _, child in ipairs(tarChildren) do child.Parent = cur end
        for _, child in ipairs(temp:GetChildren()) do child.Parent = tar end
        temp:Destroy()
        cosSwaps[key] = { current = current, target = target }
        onComplete(true)
    end
    cosScanAsync(items, "Cosmetics", current, function(result)
        curFound = result
        curDone = true
        checkDone()
    end)
    cosScanAsync(items, "Cosmetics", target, function(result)
        tarFound = result
        tarDone = true
        checkDone()
    end)
end
local function cosResetAll(onComplete)
    local list = {}
    for _, v in pairs(cosSwaps) do table.insert(list, v) end
    local count = #list
    if count == 0 then
        onComplete(0)
        return
    end
    local done = 0
    for _, v in ipairs(list) do
        cosSwap(v.target, v.current, function()
            done = done + 1
            if done == count then
                cosSwaps = {}
                onComplete(count)
            end
        end)
    end
end
local function cosApply()
    local current = cosTrim(cosCurrentName)
    local target = cosTrim(cosNewName)
    if current == "" or target == "" then
        cosSetStatus(scriptText("Faltan los dos nombres de cosmético.", "Both cosmetic names are required."), Color3.fromRGB(255, 185, 115))
        return
    end
    if current == target then
        cosSetStatus(scriptText("El cosmético actual y el nuevo deben ser diferentes.", "The current and new cosmetic must be different."), Color3.fromRGB(255, 185, 115))
        return
    end
    cosSetStatus(scriptText("Buscando cosméticos...", "Searching cosmetics..."), Color3.fromRGB(175, 185, 205))
    task.spawn(function()
        cosSwap(current, target, function(success)
            if success then
                cosSetStatus(scriptText("Aplicado: ", "Applied: ") .. current .. " → " .. target, Color3.fromRGB(150, 255, 190))
                print("✅ COSMETICS CHANGER: " .. current .. " → " .. target)
            else
                cosSetStatus(scriptText("No se encontró alguno de los nombres. Revisa mayúsculas y minúsculas.", "One of the names was not found. Check upper/lower case."), Color3.fromRGB(255, 125, 125))
            end
        end)
    end)
end
local function cosReset()
    task.spawn(function()
        cosResetAll(function(count)
            if count == 0 then
                cosSetStatus(scriptText("No hay cosméticos cambiados para restablecer.", "There are no swapped cosmetics to reset."), Color3.fromRGB(255, 185, 115))
            else
                cosSetStatus(scriptText("Cosméticos restablecidos a su estado original.", "Cosmetics reset to their original state."), Color3.fromRGB(150, 255, 190))
            end
        end)
    end)
end
AllDesign.Button(AllCosmeticsPage, "APLICAR COSMÉTICO", "APPLY COSMETIC", cosApply, true)
AllDesign.Button(AllCosmeticsPage, "RESTABLECER COSMÉTICOS", "RESET COSMETICS", cosReset, false)
cosStatusLabel = AllDesign.Status(AllCosmeticsPage, "Listo para aplicar un cambio de cosmético.", "Ready to apply a cosmetic change.")
TabAll:ResetContentContainer()
for index, child in ipairs(AllCosmeticsPage:GetChildren()) do
    if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
        child.LayoutOrder = index
    end
end
allUpdateCarouselHeight(AllCosmeticsPage)
allUpdateCarouselHeight(AllCatalogPage)
do
    local AllSkinsPage = allMakeCarouselPage("AllSkinsCarouselContent")
    AllCarouselPages.Skins = AllSkinsPage
    TabAll:SetContentContainer(AllSkinsPage)
    AllDesign.Header(
        AllSkinsPage,
        "CAMBIADOR DE SKINS DE TOOLS", "TOOLS SKIN CHANGER",
        "Escribe el tool, la skin actual y la skin que quieres usar.",
        "Enter the tool, the current skin and the skin you want to use."
    )
    local ToolSkinSawps = {}
    local activeToolsSwaps = {}
    local currentToolInput = ""
    local currentSkinInput = ""
    local selectedSkinInput = ""
    local skinStatusLabel
    local function skinTrim(value)
        local text = tostring(value or "")
        text = text:gsub("^%s+", "")
        text = text:gsub("%s+$", "")
        return text
    end
    local function skinSetStatus(text, color)
        AllDesign.SetStatus(skinStatusLabel, text, color)
    end
    local function skinCreateBox(titleSpanish, titleEnglish, placeholderSpanish, placeholderEnglish, setter)
        local input = AllDesign.Field(AllSkinsPage, titleSpanish, titleEnglish, placeholderSpanish, placeholderEnglish)
        input.Name = "AllSkinNameInput"
        input:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            setter(skinTrim(input.Text))
        end)
        return input
    end
    skinCreateBox(
        "NOMBRE DEL TOOL", "TOOL NAME",
        "Nombre del tool", "Enter tool name",
        function(value) currentToolInput = value end
    )
    skinCreateBox(
        "SKIN ACTUAL", "CURRENT SKIN",
        "Nombre de la skin actual", "Enter current skin name",
        function(value) currentSkinInput = value end
    )
    skinCreateBox(
        "SKIN NUEVA", "NEW SKIN",
        "Nombre de la skin nueva", "Enter new skin name",
        function(value) selectedSkinInput = value end
    )
    local swapSkins
    local normalizeString, isValidTool, isValidSkin, revertSkinSwap, revertAllToolSkinSawps
    function normalizeString(str)
    return str:gsub("%s+", ""):lower()
    end
    function isValidTool(toolName)
    local tools = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Tools")
    if not tools then return false end
    local tool = tools:FindFirstChild(toolName)
    if not tool then return false end
    local variants = tool:FindFirstChild("Variants")
    if not variants then return false end
    return true, tool, variants
    end
    function isValidSkin(toolName, skinName)
    local toolValid, tool, variants = isValidTool(toolName)
    if not toolValid then return false end
    local skin = variants:FindFirstChild(skinName)
    if not skin then return false end
    return true, tool, variants, skin
    end
    function revertSkinSwap(toolName, currentSkinName, selectedSkinName)
    local key = toolName .. "_" .. currentSkinName
    if not activeToolsSwaps[key] then return false end
    local currentValid, currentTool, currentVariants, currentSkin = isValidSkin(toolName, currentSkinName)
    local selectedValid, selectedTool, selectedVariants, selectedSkin = isValidSkin(toolName, selectedSkinName)
    if currentValid and selectedValid then
    pcall(function()
    local tempRoot = Instance.new("Folder")
    tempRoot.Name = "__temp_skin_revert_" .. tostring(tick()):gsub("%.", "_")
    tempRoot.Parent = currentVariants
    local tempCurrent = Instance.new("Folder")
    tempCurrent.Name = "tempCurrent"
    tempCurrent.Parent = tempRoot
    local tempSelected = Instance.new("Folder")
    tempSelected.Name = "tempSelected"
    tempSelected.Parent = tempRoot
    for _, child in ipairs(currentSkin:GetChildren()) do
    child.Parent = tempCurrent
    end
    for _, child in ipairs(selectedSkin:GetChildren()) do
    child.Parent = tempSelected
    end
    for _, child in ipairs(tempCurrent:GetChildren()) do
    child.Parent = selectedSkin
    end
    for _, child in ipairs(tempSelected:GetChildren()) do
    child.Parent = currentSkin
    end
    tempRoot:Destroy()
    end)
    end
    activeToolsSwaps[key] = nil
    return true
    end
    function revertAllToolSkinSawps()
    local swapsToRevert = {}
    for key, swapData in pairs(activeToolsSwaps) do
    table.insert(swapsToRevert, swapData)
    end
    for _, swapData in ipairs(swapsToRevert) do
    revertSkinSwap(swapData.toolName, swapData.currentSkin, swapData.selectedSkin)
    end
    ToolSkinSawps = {}
    activeToolsSwaps = {}
    end
    function swapSkins(toolName, currentSkinName, selectedSkinName)
        local currentNorm = normalizeString(currentSkinName)
        local selectedNorm = normalizeString(selectedSkinName)
        if toolName == "" or currentNorm == "" or selectedNorm == "" then
            skinSetStatus(scriptText("Debes llenar todos los campos.", "All fields must be filled"), Color3.fromRGB(255, 185, 115))
            return false
        end
        if currentNorm == selectedNorm then
            skinSetStatus(scriptText("Los nombres de skin no pueden ser iguales.", "Skin names cannot be the same"), Color3.fromRGB(255, 185, 115))
            return false
        end
        local currentValid, currentTool, currentVariants, currentSkin = isValidSkin(toolName, currentSkinName)
        local selectedValid, selectedTool, selectedVariants, selectedSkin = isValidSkin(toolName, selectedSkinName)
        if not currentValid then
            skinSetStatus(scriptText("Skin actual no encontrada: ", "Current skin not found: ") .. currentSkinName, Color3.fromRGB(255, 125, 125))
            return false
        end
        if not selectedValid then
            skinSetStatus(scriptText("Skin nueva no encontrada: ", "Selected skin not found: ") .. selectedSkinName, Color3.fromRGB(255, 125, 125))
            return false
        end
        local key = toolName .. "_" .. currentSkinName
        if activeToolsSwaps[key] then
            revertSkinSwap(toolName, currentSkinName, activeToolsSwaps[key].selectedSkin)
        end
        pcall(function()
            local tempRoot = Instance.new("Folder")
            tempRoot.Name = "__temp_skin_swap_" .. tostring(tick()):gsub("%.", "_")
            tempRoot.Parent = currentVariants
            local tempCurrent = Instance.new("Folder")
            tempCurrent.Name = "tempCurrent"
            tempCurrent.Parent = tempRoot
            local tempSelected = Instance.new("Folder")
            tempSelected.Name = "tempSelected"
            tempSelected.Parent = tempRoot
            for _, child in ipairs(currentSkin:GetChildren()) do
                child.Parent = tempCurrent
            end
            for _, child in ipairs(selectedSkin:GetChildren()) do
                child.Parent = tempSelected
            end
            for _, child in ipairs(tempCurrent:GetChildren()) do
                child.Parent = selectedSkin
            end
            for _, child in ipairs(tempSelected:GetChildren()) do
                child.Parent = currentSkin
            end
            tempRoot:Destroy()
            local swapData = {
                toolName = toolName,
                currentSkin = currentSkinName,
                selectedSkin = selectedSkinName
            }
            activeToolsSwaps[key] = swapData
            local found = false
            for i, swap in ipairs(ToolSkinSawps) do
                if swap.toolName == toolName and swap.currentSkin == currentSkinName then
                    ToolSkinSawps[i] = swapData
                    found = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if not found then
                table.insert(ToolSkinSawps, swapData)
            end
            skinSetStatus(
                scriptText("Cambio aplicado: ", "Successfully swapped ") .. currentSkinName
                    .. scriptText(" por ", " with ") .. selectedSkinName
                    .. scriptText(" en ", " for ") .. toolName,
                Color3.fromRGB(150, 255, 190)
            )
        end)
        return true
    end
    AllDesign.Button(AllSkinsPage, "REEMPLAZAR SKIN", "REPLACE SKIN SWAP", function()
        swapSkins(currentToolInput, currentSkinInput, selectedSkinInput)
    end, true)
    AllDesign.Button(AllSkinsPage, "RESTABLECER SKINS", "RESET ALL SKINS", function()
        revertAllToolSkinSawps()
        skinSetStatus(
            scriptText("Todas las skins se restablecieron a su estado original.", "All skins have been reset to original"),
            Color3.fromRGB(150, 255, 190)
        )
    end, false)
    skinStatusLabel = AllDesign.Status(AllSkinsPage, "Listo para cambiar una skin.", "Ready to swap a skin.")
    TabAll:ResetContentContainer()
    for index, child in ipairs(AllSkinsPage:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
            child.LayoutOrder = index
        end
    end
    allUpdateCarouselHeight(AllSkinsPage)
end
allSetCarouselMode("Catalog")
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
    if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
    local page = TabAll.Page
    if not page.Visible then return end
    local pos = input.Position
    local ap, as = page.AbsolutePosition, page.AbsoluteSize
    if pos.X < ap.X or pos.X > ap.X + as.X or pos.Y < ap.Y or pos.Y > ap.Y + as.Y then return end
    AllCarouselSwipe.input = input
    AllCarouselSwipe.start = input.Position
end)
UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input ~= AllCarouselSwipe.input or not AllCarouselSwipe.start then return end
    local delta = input.Position - AllCarouselSwipe.start
    AllCarouselSwipe.input = nil
    AllCarouselSwipe.start = nil
    if math.abs(delta.X) < AllCarouselSwipe.threshold
        or math.abs(delta.X) <= math.abs(delta.Y) * 1.20
        or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
    local direction = delta.X < 0 and 1 or -1
    local carouselOrder = { "Catalog", "Cosmetics", "Skins" }
    local currentIndex = table.find(carouselOrder, AllCarouselMode) or 1
    local nextMode = carouselOrder[currentIndex + direction]
    if not nextMode then return end
    allSetCarouselMode(nextMode, direction)
end)
TabAll.Page:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
    AllTabVisible = TabAll.Page.Visible
    if not AllDiscoveryRunning then
        allRebuildRows()
    end
end)
local AllSearchDebounceToken = 0
AllSearch:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
    AllSearchDebounceToken = AllSearchDebounceToken + 1
    local token = AllSearchDebounceToken
    task.delay(0.25, function()
        if token ~= AllSearchDebounceToken then return end
        if not AllDiscoveryRunning and AllTabVisible then
            allRebuildRows()
        end
    end)
end)
local oldAllVisuals = Workspace:FindFirstChild("__YY_AllVisualCandidates")
if oldAllVisuals then pcall(function() oldAllVisuals:Destroy() end) end
local AllInitOk, AllInitError = pcall(allDiscoverCandidates)
if not AllInitOk then
    allSetStatus("Error de All: " .. tostring(AllInitError))
    warn("[All] Error al iniciar el descubrimiento: " .. tostring(AllInitError))
end
end
setupAllTab()
refreshScriptLanguage()
return UI
